# Sattakanipāta (AN 7)

> **Nikāya:** Aṅguttaranikāya
> **Nipāta:** AN 7 (Sattakanipāta)
> **PTS Volume:** Aṅguttara Nikāya Vol. 4
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

---


<!-- PTS Page 001 -->
<a id="page-001"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 001]*

AṄGUTTARA-NIKĀYA. SATTAKA-NIPĀTA.

<p align="center">Namo Tassa Bhagavato Arahato Sammāsambuddhassa.</p>


I.

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante[^001-1] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Sattahi[^001-2] bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyo ca hoti amanāpo ca agaru ca[^001-3] abhāvanīyo ca[^001-3]. Katamehi sattahi?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu lābhakāmo ca hoti sakkārakāmo ca[^001-4] anavaññattikāmo ca[^001-4] ahiriko ca[^001-5] anottappī ca pāpiccho[^001-6] ca micchādiṭṭhi ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyo ca hoti[^001-7] amanāpo ca agaru ca abhāvanīyo ca.

4. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca[^001-8] hoti manāpo ca garu ca bhāvanīyo[^001-9] ca. Katamehi sattahi?


<!-- PTS Page 002 -->
<a id="page-002"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 002]*

5. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na lābhakāmo ca hoti na sakkārakāmo ca[^002-1] na anavaññattikāmo ca[^002-1] hirimā[^002-2] ca[^002-3] ottappī[^002-4] ca appiccho ca sammādiṭṭhi ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave. sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca hoti manāpo ca garu ca bhāvanīyo cā ti. II.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacarīnaṃ appiyo ca hoti amanāpo ca agaru ca abhāvanīyo ca. Katamehi sattahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu lābhakāmo ca hoti[^002-5] sakkārakāmo ca[^002-6] anavaññattikāmo ca[^002-7] ahiriko ca[^002-6] anottappī ca issukī ca maccharī ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyo ca hoti amanāpo ca agaru ca abhāvanīyo ca[^002-8].

3. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca hoti manāpo ca[^002-9] garu ca bhāvanīyo ca. Katamehi sattahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na lābhakāmo ca[^002-10] hoti na sakkārakāmo ca[^002-10] na anavaññattikāmo ca[^002-10] hirimā[^002-11] ca[^002-12] ottappī[^002-13] ca anissukī[^002-14] ca amaccharī ca. Imehi[^002-15] kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca hoti manāpo ca garu ca bhāvanīyo cā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 003 -->
<a id="page-003"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 003]*

III.

1. Satt'; imāni[^003-1] bhikkhave balāni. Katamāni satta?

2. Saddhābalaṃ, viriyabalaṃ, hiribalaṃ[^003-2], ottappabalaṃ[^003-3], satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, paññābalaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave satta balānī ti. Saddhābalaṃ viriyañ ca[^003-4] hiri[^003-5] ottappiyaṃ balaṃ satibalaṃ samādhiñ ca[^003-6] paññā ve sattamaṃ balaṃ. Etehi balavā bhikkhu sukhaṃ jīvati paṇḍito, yoniso vicine[^003-7] dhammaṃ, paññāyatthaṃ[^003-8] vipassati, pajjotass'[^003-9] eva nibbānaṃ, vimokho[^003-10] hoti cetaso ti. IV.

1. Satt'; imāni bhikkhave balāni. Katamāni satta?

2. Saddhābalaṃ, viriyabalaṃ, hiribalaṃ[^003-2], ottappabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, paññābalaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave saddhābalaṃ?

3. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako saddho hoti, saddahati Tathāgatassa bodhiṃ ‘iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno[^003-11] sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave saddhābalaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave viriyabalaṃ?

4. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave viriyabalaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave hiribalaṃ[^003-2]?

5. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako hirimā[^003-2] hoti, hiriyati kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena, hiriyati


<!-- PTS Page 004 -->
<a id="page-004"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 004]*

pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave hiribalaṃ[^004-1]. Katamañ ca bhikkhave ottappabalaṃ?

6. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako ottappī hoti, ottappati[^004-2] kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena, ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ottappabalaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave satibalaṃ?

7. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako satimā hoti paramena satinepakkena samannāgato, cirakatam pi cirabhāsitam pi saritā anussaritā. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave satibalaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave samādhibalam?

8. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . . pe[^004-3] . . . catutthaṃ[^004-4] jhānaṃ[^004-4] upasampajja viharati. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave samādhibalaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave paññābalaṃ?

9. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako paññavā hoti, udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave paññābalaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave satta balānī ti. Saddhābalaṃ viriyañ ca[^004-5] hiri ottappiyaṃ balaṃ satibalaṃ[^004-6] samādhiñ ca[^004-7] paññā ve sattamaṃ balaṃ. Etehi balavā bhikkhu sukhaṃ jīvati paṇḍito, yoniso vicine dhammaṃ, paññāyatthaṃ vipassati, pajjotass'[^004-8] eva nibbānaṃ, vimokho[^004-9] hoti cetaso ti. V.

1. Satt'; imāni bhikkhave dhanāni. Katamāni satta?

2. Saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, hiridhanaṃ, ottappadhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, paññādhanaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 005 -->
<a id="page-005"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 005]*

Imāni kho bhikkhave satta dhanānī ti[^005-1]. Saddhādhanaṃ sīladhanaṃ hiri ottappiyaṃ dhanaṃ[^005-2] sutadhanañ ca cāgo ca paññā ve sattamaṃ dhanaṃ. Yassa ete[^005-3] dhanā atthi itthiyā purisassa vā. adaliddo[^005-4] ti taṃ āhu, amoghaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ. Tasmā saddhañ ca sīlañ ca pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ anuyuñjetha medhāvī saraṃ[^005-5] buddhānasāsanan ti. VI.

1. Satt'; imāni bhikkhave dhanāni. Katamāni satta?

2. Saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, hiridhanam, ottappadhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, paññādhanam. Katamañ ca bhikkhave saddhādhanaṃ?

3. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako saddho hoti, saddahati Tathāgatassa bodhim ‘iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho . . . pe[^005-6] . . . buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave saddhādhanaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave sīladhanaṃ?

4. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako pāṇātipātā pativirato hoti . . . pe[^005-6] . . . surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā pativirato hoti. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave sīladhanaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave hiridhanaṃ?

5. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako hirimā hoti, hiriyati kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena, hiriyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave hiridhanaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave ottappadhanaṃ?

6. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako ottappī[^005-7] hoti, ottappati[^005-8] kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena, ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ottappadhanaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave sutadhanaṃ?


<!-- PTS Page 006 -->
<a id="page-006"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 006]*

7. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako bahussuto hoti sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ[^006-1] savyañjanaṃ[^006-2] kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa[^006-3] dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā[^006-4] vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave sutadhanaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave cāgadhanaṃ?

8. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgo payatapāṇi vossaggarato yācayogo dānasaṃvibhāgarato. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave cāgadhanaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave paññādhanaṃ?

9. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako paññavā hoti . . . pe[^006-5] . . . sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave paññādhanaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave satta dhanānī ti. Saddhādhanaṃ sīladhanaṃ hiri ottappiyaṃ dhanaṃ sutadhanañ ca cāgo ca paññā ve sattamaṃ dhanaṃ. Yassa ete[^006-6] dhanā[^006-7] atthi itthiyā purisassa vā, adaliddo[^006-8] ti taṃ āhu, amoghaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ. Tasmā saddhañ ca sīlañ ca pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ anuyuñjetha medhāvī saraṃ[^006-9] buddhānasāsanan ti. VII.

1. Atha kho Uggo rājamahāmatto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ[^006-10] nisīdi[^006-10]. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Uggo rājamahāmatto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘acchariyaṃ bhante abbhutaṃ bhante, yāva aḍḍho 'vāyaṃ[^006-11] bhante Migāro Rohaṇeyyo yāva mahaddhano yāva mahābhogo'; ti. ‘Kīva[^006-12]


<!-- PTS Page 007 -->
<a id="page-007"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 007]*

aḍḍho pan'; Ugga Migāro Rohaṇeyyo kīva[^007-1] mahaddhano kīva[^007-1] mahābhogo'; ti? ‘Sataṃ bhante satasahassānaṃ[^007-2] hiraññassa, ko pana vādo rūpiyassā'; ti. ‘Atthi kho etaṃ[^007-3] Ugga dhanaṃ? N'; etaṃ[^007-4] natthī ti vadāmi[^007-5], tañ ca kho etaṃ Ugga dhanaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ agginā udakena rājūhi corehi appiyehi dāyādehi. Satta kho imāni Ugga dhanāni asādhāraṇāni agginā udakena rājūhi corehi appiyehi dāyādehi. Katamāni satta? Saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, hiridhanaṃ, ottappadhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, paññādhanaṃ. Imāni kho Ugga satta dhanāni asādhāraṇāni agginā udakena rājūhi corehi appiyehi dāyādehī'; ti. Saddhādhanam sīladhanaṃ hiri ottappiyaṃ dhanaṃ sutadhanañ ca cāgo ca paññā ve sattamaṃ dhanaṃ. Yassa ete[^007-6] dhanā atthi itthiyā purisassa vā, sa ve[^007-7] mahaddhano loke ajeyyo devamānuse[^007-8]. Tasmā saddhañ ca sīlañ ca pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ anuyuñjetha[^007-9] medhāvī saraṃ[^007-10] buddhānasāsanan[^007-11] ti. VIII.

1. Satt'; imāni[^007-12] bhikkhave saññojanāni. Katamāni satta?

2. Anunayasaññojanaṃ, paṭighasaññojanaṃ, diṭṭhisaññojanaṃ, vicikicchāsaññojanaṃ, mānasaññojanaṃ, bhavarāgasaññojanaṃ, avijjāsaññojanaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave satta saññojanānī ti. IX.

1. Sattannaṃ bhikkhave saññojanānaṃ pahānāya samucchedāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati[^007-13]. Katamesaṃ sattannaṃ?


<!-- PTS Page 008 -->
<a id="page-008"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 008]*

2. Anunayasaññojanassa pahānāya samucchedāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati, paṭighasaññojanassa . . . pe[^008-1] . . . diṭṭhisaññojanassa[^008-2] . . . vicikicchāsaññojanassa . . . mānasaññojanassa . . . bhavarāgasaññojanassa . . . avijjāsaññojanassa pahānāya samucchedāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave sattannaṃ saññojanānaṃ pahānāya samucchedāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati. Yato[^008-3] kho[^008-4] bhikkhave bhikkhuno anunayasaññojanaṃ pahīnaṃ hoti ucchinnamūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvakataṃ āyatiṃ[^008-5] anuppādadhammaṃ, paṭighasaññojanaṃ . . . pe[^008-6] . . . diṭṭhisaññojanaṃ . . . vicikicchāsaññojanaṃ . . . mānasaññojanaṃ . . . bhavarāgasaññojanaṃ . . . avijjāsaññojanaṃ pahīnaṃ hoti ucchinnamūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvakataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ: ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu acchejji[^008-7] taṇhaṃ vivattayi[^008-8] saññojanaṃ sammāmānābhisamayā antam akāsi dukkhassā ti. X.

1. Satt'; imāni bhikkhave saññojanāni. Katamāni satta?

2. Anunayasaññojanaṃ, paṭighasaññojanaṃ, diṭṭhisaññojanaṃ, vicikicchāsaññojanaṃ, mānasaññojanaṃ, issāsaññojanaṃ, macchariyasaññojanaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave satta saññojanānī ti. Dhanavaggo paṭhamo[^008-9]. Tass'; uddānaṃ[^008-10]:


<!-- PTS Page 009 -->
<a id="page-009"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 009]*

Dve piyaṃ dve[^009-1] balaṃ dhanaṃ[^009-2] (saṃkhittañ c'; eva vitthataṃ)[^009-3] Ugga saññojanañ c'; eva pahāna macchariyenā[^009-4] ti. XI.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave anusayā[^009-5]. Katame satta?

2. Kāmarāgānusayo, paṭighānusayo, diṭṭhānusayo, vicikicchānusayo, mānānusayo, bhavarāgānusayo, avijjānusayo. Ime kho bhikkhave satta anusayā ti. XII.

1. Sattannaṃ bhikkhave anusayānaṃ pahānāya samucchedāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati. Katamesaṃ sattannaṃ?

2. Kāmarāgānusayassa pahānāya samucchedāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati, paṭighānusayassa . . . pe[^009-6] . . . diṭṭhānusayassa . . . vicikicchānusayassa . . . mānānusayassa . . . bhavarāgānusayassa . . . avijjānusayassa pahānāya samucchedāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave sattannaṃ anusayānaṃ pahānāya samucchedāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati. Yato kho bhikkhave bhikkhuno kāmarāgānusayo pahīno hoti ucchinnamūlo tālāvatthukato anabhāvakato āyatiṃ anuppādadhammo, paṭighānusayo . . . pe[^009-7] . . . diṭṭhānusayo . . . vicikicchānusayo . . . mānānusayo . . . bhavarāgānusayo . . . avijjānusayo pahīno hoti ucchinnamūlo tālāvatthukato anabhāvakato āyatiṃ anuppādadhammo: ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu acchejji[^009-8] taṇhaṃ vivattayi[^009-9] saññojanaṃ sammāmānābhisamayā antam akāsi dukkhassā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 010 -->
<a id="page-010"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 010]*

XIII.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā nālaṃ upagantuṃ upagantvā vā nālaṃ upanisīdituṃ[^010-1]. Katamehi sattahi?

2. Na manāpena paccuṭṭhenti[^010-2], na manāpena abhivādenti, na manāpena āsanaṃ denti, santam[^010-3] assa parigūhanti, bahukamhi[^010-4] thokaṃ denti, paṇītamhi[^010-5] lūkhaṃ denti, asakkaccaṃ denti no sakkaccaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi aṅgehi samannāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā nālaṃ upagantuṃ upagantvā vā nālaṃ upanisīdituṃ[^010-1].

3. Sattahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgatam kulaṃ anupagantvā vā alaṃ upagantuṃ upagantvā vā alaṃ upanisīdituṃ[^010-6]. Katamehi sattahi?

4. Manāpena paccuṭṭhenti[^010-7], manāpena abhivādenti, manāpena āsanaṃ denti, santam assa na parigūhanti, bahukamhi[^010-8] bahukaṃ denti, paṇītamhi[^010-9] paṇītaṃ denti, sakkaccaṃ denti no asakkaccaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi aṅgehi samannāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā alaṃ upagantuṃ upagantvā vā alaṃ upanisīditun[^010-10] ti. XIV.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave puggalā āhuneyyā pāhuneyyā dakkhiṇeyyā añjalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katame satta?

2. Ubhato bhāgavimutto, paññāvimutto, kāyasakkhi, diṭṭhippatto, saddhāvimutto, dhammānusārī, saddhānusārī[^010-11].


<!-- PTS Page 011 -->
<a id="page-011"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 011]*

Ime kho bhikkhave satta puggalā āhuneyyā pāhuneyyā dakkhiṇeyyā añjalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. XV.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave udakūpamā puggalā santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmiṃ. Katame satta?

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo sakiṃ nimuggo nimuggo 'va hoti, idha pana bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjitvā nimujjati, idha pana bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjitvā ṭhito hoti, idha pana bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjitvā vipassati viloketi, idha pana bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjitvā patarati, idha pana bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjitvā patigādhappatto hoti, idha pana bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjitvā tiṇṇo hoti pāragato[^011-1] thale tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇo. Kathañ ca bhikkhave puggalo sakiṃ nimuggo nimuggo 'va[^011-2] hoti?

3. Idha[^011-3] bhikkhave ekacco puggalo samannāgato hoti ekantakāḷakehi[^011-4] akusalehi dhammehi. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave puggalo sakiṃ nimuggo nimuggo 'va hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā nimujjati?

4. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjati[^011-5] ‘sādhu saddhā kusalesu dhammesu, sādhu hiri . . . sādhu ottappaṃ . . . sādhu viriyaṃ . . . sādhu paññā kusalesu dhammesū' ti. Tassa sā saddhā neva tiṭṭhati no vaḍḍhati, hāyati yeva[^011-6]. Tassa sā hiri . . . pe[^011-7] . . . tassa taṃ ottappaṃ . . . tassa taṃ viriyaṃ . . . tassa sā paññā neva tiṭṭhati no vaḍḍhati, hāyati yeva[^011-8]. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā nimujjati. Kathañ ca bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā ṭhito hoti?


<!-- PTS Page 012 -->
<a id="page-012"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 012]*

5. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjati[^012-1] ‘sādhu saddhā kusalesu dhammesu, sādhu hiri . . . sādhu ottappaṃ . . . sādhu viriyaṃ . . . sādhu paññā kusalesu dhammesū' ti. Tassa sā saddhā neva hāyati no vaḍḍhati, ṭhitā[^012-2] hoti. Tassa sā hiri . . . pe[^012-3] . . . tassa taṃ ottappaṃ . . . tassa taṃ viriyaṃ . . . tassa sā paññā neva hāyati no vaḍḍhati, ṭhitā[^012-4] hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā ṭhito hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā vipassati viloketi?

6. Idha[^012-5] bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjati ‘sādhu saddhā kusalesu dhammesu, sādhu hiri . . . sādhu ottappaṃ . . . sādhu viriyaṃ . . . sādhu paññā kusalesu dhammesū' ti. So tiṇṇaṃ[^012-6] saññojanānaṃ parikkhayā sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā vipassati viloketi. Kathañ ca bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā patarati?

7. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjati ‘sādhu saddhā kusalesu dhammesu, sādhu hiri . . .[^012-7] sādhu ottappaṃ . . . sādhu viriyaṃ . . . sādhu paññā kusalesu dhammesū' ti. So tiṇṇaṃ saññojanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadosamohānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hoti saki-d-eva imaṃ lokaṃ āgantvā dukkhass'; antaṃ karoti[^012-8]. Evam kho bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā patarati. Kathañ ca bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā patigādhappatto hoti?

8. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjati ‘sādhu saddhā kusalesu dhammesu, sādhu hiri . . .[^012-7] sādhu ottappaṃ . . . sādhu viriyaṃ . . . sādhu paññā kusalesu dhammesū' ti[^012-9]. So pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saññojanānaṃ parikkhayā opapātiko hoti, tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave puggalo[^012-10] ummujjitvā patigādhappatto hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā tiṇṇo hoti pāragato thale tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇo?


<!-- PTS Page 013 -->
<a id="page-013"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 013]*

9. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo ummujjati ‘sādhu saddhā kusalesu dhammesu, sādhu hiri[^013-1] . . . sādhu ottappaṃ . . . sādhu viriyaṃ . . . sādhu paññā kusalesu dhammesū' ti. So āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave puggalo ummujjitvā tiṇṇo hoti pāragato thale tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇo. Ime kho bhikkhave satta udakūpamā[^013-2] puggalā santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmin ti. XVI.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave puggalā āhuneyyā pāhuneyyā dakkhiṇeyyā añjalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katame satta?

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo sabbasaṅkhāresu aniccānupassī viharati aniccasaññī aniccapaṭisaṃvedī satataṃ samitaṃ abbokiṇṇaṃ cetasā adhimuccamāno paññāya pariyogāhamāno. So āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^013-3] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Ayaṃ[^013-4] bhikkhave paṭhamo puggalo āhuneyyo pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco puggalo sabbasaṅkhāresu aniccānupassī viharati aniccasaññī aniccapaṭisaṃvedī satataṃ samitaṃ abbokiṇṇaṃ cetasā adhimuccamāno paññāya pariyogāhamāno. Tassa apubbaṃ acarimaṃ āsavapariyādānañ ca hoti[^013-5] jīvitapariyādānañ ca. Ayaṃ[^013-6] bhikkhave dutiyo puggalo āhuneyyo . . . pe[^013-7] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco puggalo sabbasaṅkhāresu aniccānupassī viharati aniccasaññī aniccapaṭisaṃvedī satataṃ samitaṃ abbokiṇṇaṃ cetasā adhimuccamāno paññāya pariyogāhamāno. So pañcannaṃ orambhā-


<!-- PTS Page 014 -->
<a id="page-014"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 014]*

giyānaṃ saññojanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī[^014-1] hoti . . . pe[^014-2] . . . upahaccaparinibbāyī hoti . . .[^014-3] asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hoti . . .[^014-3] sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hoti . . .[^014-3] uddhaṃsoto hoti akaniṭṭhagāmī. Ayaṃ[^014-4] bhikkhave sattamo puggalo āhuneyyo pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Ime kho bhikkhave satta puggalā āhuneyyā pāhuneyyā[^014-5] dakkhiṇeyyā añjalikaraṇīya anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. XVII.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave puggalā āhuneyyā . . . pe[^014-3] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katame satta?

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo sabbasaṅkhāresu dukkhānupassī viharati . . . pe[^014-3] . . . sabbesu dhammesu anattānupassī viharati . . .[^014-3] nibbāne sukhānupassī viharati sukhasaññī sukhapaṭisaṃvedī satataṃ samitaṃ abbokiṇṇaṃ cetasā adhimuccamāno paññāya pariyogāhamāno. So āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^014-6] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo puggalo āhuneyyo . . . pe[^014-6] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco puggalo nibbāne sukhānupassī viharati sukhasaññī sukhapaṭisaṃvedī satataṃ samitaṃ abbokiṇṇaṃ cetasā adhimuccamāno paññāya pariyogāhamāno. Tassa apubbaṃ acarimaṃ āsavapariyādānañ ca hoti jīvitapariyādānañ ca. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo puggalo āhuneyyo . . . pe[^014-3] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco puggalo nibbāne sukhānupassī viharati sukhasaññī sukhapaṭisaṃvedī satataṃ samitaṃ abbokiṇṇaṃ cetasā adhimuccamāno paññāya pariyogāhamāno. So pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saññojanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hoti . . . pe[^014-6] . . .


<!-- PTS Page 015 -->
<a id="page-015"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 015]*

upahaccaparinibbāyī[^015-1] hoti . . . asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hoti . . . sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hoti . . . uddhaṃsoto hoti kaniṭṭhagāmī. Ayaṃ bhikkhave sattamo puggalo āhuneyyo . . . pe[^015-2] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Ime kho bhikkhave satta puggalā āhuneyyā . . . pe[^015-3] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. XVIII.

1. Satt'; imāni bhikkhave niddasavatthūni. Katamāni satta?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sikkhāsamādāne tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca sikkhāsamādāne avigatapemo[^015-4], dhammanisantiyā tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca dhammanisantiyā avigatapemo, icchāvinaye tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca icchāvinaye avigatapemo, paṭisallāne[^015-5] tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca paṭisallāne[^015-5] avigatapemo, viriyārambhe tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca viriyārambhe avigatapemo, satinepakke tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca satinepakke avigatapemo, diṭṭhipaṭivedhe[^015-6] tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca diṭṭhipaṭivedhe[^015-6] avigatapemo. Imāni kho bhikkhave satta niddasavatthūnī ti. Anusayavaggo[^015-7] dutiyo. Tass'; udānaṃ: Dve anusayā[^015-8] kusalaṃ[^015-9] puggalaṃ udakūpamaṃ[^015-10] Aniccā[^015-11] dukkhā[^015-12] anattā ca nibbānaṃ[^015-13] niddasavatthūnī[^015-14] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 016 -->
<a id="page-016"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 016]*

XIX.

1. Evaṃ[^016-1] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Sārandade[^016-2] cetiye. Atha kho sambahulā Licchavī yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinne kho te Licchavī Bhagavā etad avoca ‘satta vo[^016-3] Licchavī aparihāniye dhamme desessāmi[^016-4], taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante' ti kho te Licchavī Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Katame ca[^016-5] Licchavī satta aparihāniyā dhammā Yāvakīvañ ca Licchavī Vajjī abhiṇhaṃ sannipātā[^016-6] bhavissanti[^016-6] sannipātabahulā, vuddhi[^016-7] yeva Licchavī Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca Licchavī Vajjī samaggā sannipatissanti[^016-8], samaggā vuṭṭhahissanti, samaggā Vajjī karaṇīyāni karissanti, vuddhi yeva Licchavī Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca Licchavī Vajjī appaññattaṃ na paññāpessanti[^016-9], paññattaṃ na samucchindissanti, yathā paññatte porāṇe Vajjidhamme samādāya vattissanti, vuddhi yeva Licchavī Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca Licchavī Vajjī, ye te Vajjīnaṃ Vajjimahallakā[^016-10], te sakkarissanti garukarissanti mānessanti[^016-11] pūjessanti[^016-12] tesañ ca sotabbaṃ maññissanti, vuddhi yeva Licchavī Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca Licchavī Vajjī, yā tā kulitthiyo kulakumāriyo, tā na okkassa[^016-13] pasayha[^016-14] vāsessanti[^016-15], vuddhi yeva Licchavī Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca Licchavī Vajjī[^016-16], yāni[^016-16] tāni[^016-16] Vajjīnaṃ Vajjicetiyāni abbhantarāni c'; eva bāhirāni ca, tāni sakkarissanti garukarissanti


<!-- PTS Page 017 -->
<a id="page-017"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 017]*

mānessanti[^017-1] pūjessanti tesañ ca dinnapubbaṃ katapubbaṃ[^017-2] dhammikaṃ baliṃ no parihāpessanti[^017-3], vuddhi yeva Licchavī Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca Licchavī Vajjīnaṃ arahantesu dhammikā rakkhāvaraṇagutti susaṃvihitā bhavissati[^017-4] ‘kinti anāgatā ca[^017-5] arahanto[^017-6] vijitaṃ āgaccheyyuṃ, āgatā ca arahanto vijite phāsuṃ vihareyyun' ti, vuddhi yeva Licchavī Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca Licchavī ime satta aparihāniyā dhammā Vajjīsu ṭhassanti[^017-7] imesu ca sattasu aparihāniyesu dhammesu Vajjī sandississanti, vuddhi yeva Licchavī Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihānī ti. XX.

1. Evaṃ[^017-8] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate. Tena kho pana samayena rājā Māgadho Ajātasattu Vedehiputto Vajjī abhiyātukāmo hoti. So evam āha ‘ahaṃ ime Vajjī evaṃmahiddhike evaṃmahānubhāve ucchejjissāmi[^017-9] Vajjī[^017-10] vināsessāmi[^017-10] Vajjī anayavyasanaṃ āpādessāmi[^017-11] Vajjī[^017-8]'; ti[^017-12]. Atha kho rājā Māgadho Ajātasattu Vedehiputto Vassakāraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ Magadhamahāmattaṃ[^017-13] āmantesi ‘ehi tvaṃ brāhmaṇa, yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanena Bhagavato pāde sirasā[^017-14] vanda[^017-15]; appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha <<rājā bhante Māgadho Ajātasattu Vedehiputto Bhagavato pāde sirasā[^017-14] vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī>> ti, evañ ca vadehi: Rājā bhante Māgadho Ajātasattu Vedehiputto Vajjī abhiyātukāmo. So evam āha <<ahaṃ ime[^017-16] Vajjī evaṃmahiddhike evaṃmahānubhāve ucchejjissāmi[^017-9] Vajjī vināsessāmi Vajjī anayavyasanaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 018 -->
<a id="page-018"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 018]*

āpādessāmi Vajjī[^018-1]>> ti. Yathā Bhagavā vyākaroti, taṃ sādhukaṃ uggahetvā mama āroceyyāsi. Na hi Tathāgatā vitathaṃ bhaṇantī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bho'; ti kho Vassakāro brāhmaṇo Magadhamahāmatto[^018-2] rañño Māgadhassa[^018-3] Ajātasattussa Vedehiputtassa paṭissuṇitvā[^018-4] yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Vassakāro brāhmaṇo Magadhamahāmatto[^018-2] Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘rājā bho Gotama Māgadho[^018-5] Ajātasattu Vedehiputto bhoto Gotamassa pāde sirasā[^018-6] vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ[^018-7] phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati evañ[^018-1] ca[^018-1] vadeti[^018-1]: Rājā bho Gotama Māgadho Ajātasattu Vedehiputto Vajjī abhiyātukāmo. So evam āha: ahaṃ ime[^018-8] Vajjī evaṃmahiddhike evaṃmahānubhāve ucchejjissāmi[^018-9] Vajjī vināsessāmi Vajjī anayavyasanaṃ āpādessāmi Vajjī[^018-1]'; ti.

2. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato piṭṭhito ṭhito[^018-10] hoti Bhagavantaṃ vījamāno[^018-11]. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ āmantesi:-- Kinti te Ānanda sutaṃ ‘Vajjī abhiṇhaṃ sannipātā sannipātabahulā'; ti? Sutaṃ me taṃ bhante ‘Vajjī abhiṇhaṃ sannipātā sannipātabahulā'; ti. Yāvakīvañ ca Ānanda Vajjī abhiṇhaṃ sannipātā bhavissanti sannipātabahulā, yuddhi yeva Ānanda Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni[^018-12]. Kinti te Ānanda sutaṃ ‘Vajjī samaggā[^018-13] sannipatanti[^018-13], samaggā vuṭṭhahanti, samaggā Vajjikaraṇīyāni karontī'; ti? Sutaṃ me taṃ bhante ‘Vajjī samaggā sannipatanti[^018-14], samaggā vuṭṭhahanti, samaggā Vajjikaraṇīyāni karontī'; ti. Yāvakīvañ ca Ānanda Vajjī samaggā sannipatissanti[^018-14], samaggā


<!-- PTS Page 019 -->
<a id="page-019"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 019]*

vuṭṭhahissanti, samaggā Vajjikaraṇīyāni karissanti, vuddhi yeva Ānanda Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni[^019-1]. Kinti te Ānanda sutaṃ ‘Vajjī appaññattaṃ na paññāpenti, paññattaṃ na samucchindanti, yathā paññatte porāṇe Vajjidhamme samādāya vattantī[^019-2]'; ti? Sutaṃ me taṃ bhante ‘Vajjī appaññattaṃ na paññāpenti, paññattaṃ na samucchindanti, yathā paññatte porāṇe Vajjidhamme samādāya vattantī'; ti. Yāvakīvañ ca Ānanda Vajjī appaññattaṃ na paññāpessanti, paññattaṃ na samucchindissanti, yathā paññatte porāṇe Vajjidhamme samādāya vattissanti, vuddhi yeva Ānanda Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Kinti te Ānanda sutaṃ ‘Vajjī, ye te Vajjīnaṃ Vajjimahallakā, te sakkaronti garukaronti mānenti pūjenti tesañ ca sotabbaṃ maññantī'; ti? Sutaṃ me taṃ bhante ‘Vajjī, ye te Vajjīnaṃ Vajjimahallakā, te sakkaronti garukaronti mānenti pūjenti tesañ ca sotabbaṃ maññantī'; ti. Yāvakīvañ ca Ānanda Vajjī, ye te Vajjīnaṃ Vajjimahallakā, te sakkarissanti garukarissanti mānessanti[^019-3] pūjessanti[^019-4] tesañ ca sotabbaṃ maññissanti[^019-5], vuddhi yeva Ānanda Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Kinti te Ānanda sutaṃ ‘Vajjī, yā tā kulitthiyo kulakumāriyo, tā na okkassa pasayha[^019-6] vāsentī'; ti? Sutaṃ me taṃ bhante ‘Vajjī, yā tā kulitthiyo kulakumāriyo, tā na okkassa pasayha[^019-6] vāsentī'; ti. Yāvakīvañ ca Ānanda Vajjī, yā tā kulitthiyo kulakumāriyo, tā na okkassa pasayha[^019-6] vāsessanti[^019-7], vuddhi yeva Ānanda Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Kinti te Ānanda sutam ‘Vajjī, yāni tāni Vajjīnaṃ Vajjicetiyāni abbhantarāni c'; eva bāhirāni ca, tāni sakkaronti garukaronti mānenti pūjenti tesañ ca dinnapubbaṃ katapubbaṃ dhammikaṃ baliṃ no parihāpentī'; ti? Sutaṃ me taṃ bhante ‘Vajjī, yāni tāni Vajjīnaṃ Vajjicetiyāni abbhantarāni c'; eva bāhirāni ca, tāni sakkaronti garukaronti mānenti pūjenti tesañ ca dinnapubbaṃ katapubbaṃ dhammikaṃ baliṃ no parihāpentī'; ti. Yāvakīvañ ca Ānanda Vajjī,


<!-- PTS Page 020 -->
<a id="page-020"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 020]*

yāni tāni Vajjīnaṃ Vajjicetiyāni abbhantarāni c'; eva bāhirāni ca, tāni sakkarissanti garukarissanti mānessanti[^020-1] pūjessanti[^020-2] tesañ ca dinnapubbaṃ katapubbaṃ dhammikaṃ baliṃ no parihāpessanti, vuddhi yeva Ānanda Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Kinti te Ānanda sutaṃ ‘Vajjīnaṃ arahantesu dhammikā rakkhāvaraṇagutti susaṃvihitā: kinti anāgatā ca arahanto vijitaṃ āgaccheyyuṃ, āgatā[^020-3] ca arahanto vijite phāsuṃ vihareyyun'; ti? Sutaṃ me taṃ bhante ‘Vajjīnaṃ arahantesu dhammikā rakkhāvaraṇagutti[^020-4] susaṃvihitā[^020-5]: kinti anāgatā ca arahanto vijitaṃ āgaccheyyuṃ, āgatā[^020-6] ca arahanto vijite phāsuṃ vihareyyun'; ti. Yāvakīvañ ca Ānanda Vajjīnaṃ arahantesu dhammikā rakkhāvaraṇagutti[^020-4] susaṃvihitā bhavissati[^020-7]: kinti anāgatā ca arahanto vijitaṃ āgaccheyyuṃ, āgatā ca arahanto vijite phāsuṃ vihareyyun ti, vuddhi yeva Ānanda Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihānī ti.

3. Atha kho Bhagavā Vassakāraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ Magadhamahāmattaṃ āmantesi:-- Ekam idāhaṃ brāhmaṇa samayaṃ Vesāliyaṃ viharāmi Sārandade cetiye, tatrāhaṃ[^020-8] Vajjīnaṃ ime satta aparihāniye dhamme desesiṃ. Yāvakīvañ ca brāhmaṇa ime satta aparihāniyā dhammā Vajjīsu ṭhassanti[^020-9], imesu ca sattasu aparihāniyesu dhammesu Vajjī sandissanti, vuddhi yeva brāhmaṇa Vajjīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihānī ti.

4. Evaṃ[^020-10] vutte Vassakāro brāhmaṇo Magadhamahāmatto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:-- ‘Ekamekena pi[^020-11] bho Gotama aparihāniyena dhammena samannāgatānaṃ Vajjīnaṃ, vuddhi yeva pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni, ko pana vādo sattahi aparihāniyehi dhammehi? Akaraṇīyā[^020-12] ca[^020-13] bho Gotama Vajjī raññā Māgadhena Ajātasattunā Vedehiputtena, yad idaṃ yuddhassa, aññatra


<!-- PTS Page 021 -->
<a id="page-021"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 021]*

upalāpanā[^021-1] aññatra mithubhedā. Handa ca[^021-2] dāni mayaṃ bho Gotama gacchāma, bahukiccā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā' ti. ‘Yassa dāni tvaṃ brāhmaṇa kālaṃ maññasī'; ti. Atha kho Vassakāro brāhmaṇo Magadhamahāmatto Bhagavato bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkamī ti. XXI.

1. Evaṃ[^021-3] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Satta vo bhikkhave aparihāniye dhamme desessāmi, taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. Evaṃ bhante ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Katame ca bhikkhave satta aparihāniyā dhammā? Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū abhiṇhaṃ sannipātā bhavissanti sannipātabahulā, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū samaggā sannipatissanti, samaggā[^021-4] vuṭṭhahissanti[^021-4], samaggā saṅghakaraṇīyāni karissanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, na parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū appaññattaṃ na paññāpessanti[^021-5], paññattaṃ na samucchindissanti, yathā paññattesu sikkhāpadesu samādāya vattissanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū, ye[^021-4] te[^021-4] bhikkhū[^021-4] therā rattaññū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitaro saṅghaparināyakā[^021-6], te sakkarissanti garukarissanti mānessanti[^021-7] pūjessanti[^021-8] tesañ ca sotabbaṃ maññissanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū uppannāya taṇhāya ponobbhavikāya na[^021-9] vasaṃ gacchissanti[^021-10], vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū araññakesu[^021-11] senāsanesu sāpekkhā


<!-- PTS Page 022 -->
<a id="page-022"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 022]*

bhavissanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū paccattaṃ yeva satiṃ upaṭṭhapessanti[^022-1] ‘kinti anāgatā ca pesalā sabrahmacārī āgaccheyyuṃ, āgatā ca pesalā sabrahmacārī phāsuṃ vihareyyun'; ti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave ime satta aparihāniyā dhammā bhikkhūsu ṭhassanti, imesu ca sattasu aparihāniyesu dhammesu bhikkhū sandissanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihānī ti. XXII.

1. Satta vo bhikkhave[^022-2] aparihāniye dhamme desessāmi[^022-3] . . . pe . . .

2. Katame ca bhikkhave satta aparihāniyā dhammā? Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū na kammārāmā[^022-4] bhavissanti na[^022-5] kammaratā[^022-5] na kammārāmataṃ[^022-6] anuyuttā, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū na bhassārāmā bhavissanti . . .[^022-7] na niddārāmā bhavissanti . . . na saṅgaṇikārāmā bhavissanti . . . na pāpicchā bhavissanti na[^022-8] pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā . . . na pāpamittā bhavissanti na pāpasahāyā na pāpasampavaṅkā . . . na oramattakena visesādhigamena antarā vosānaṃ āpajjissanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave ime satta aparihāniyā dhammā bhikkhūsu ṭhassanti, imesu ca sattasu aparihāniyesu dhammesu bhikkhū sandississanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihānī ti. XXV.

1. Satta vo bhikkhave aparihāniye dhamme desessāmi . . . pe[^024-1] . . .

2. Katame ca bhikkhave satta aparihāniyā dhammā? Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū aniccasaññaṃ bhāvessanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhū anattasaññaṃ bhāvessanti . . .[^024-2] asubhasaññaṃ bhāvessanti . . . ādīnavasaññaṃ[^024-3] bhāvessanti[^024-3] . . . pahānasaññaṃ bhāvessanti . . . virāgasaññaṃ bhāvessanti . . . nirodhasaññaṃ bhāvessanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihāni. Yāvakīvañ ca bhikkhave ime satta aparihāniyā dhammā bhikkhūsu ṭhassanti, imesu ca sattasu aparihāniyesu dhammesu bhikkhū sandississanti, vuddhi yeva bhikkhave bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, no parihānī ti. XXVI.

1. Satt'; ime[^024-4] bhikkhave dhammā sekhassa bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame satta?

2. Kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyesu aguttadvāratā, bhojane amattaññutā. Santi kho pana saṅghe saṅghakaranīyāni, tatra sekho[^024-5] bhikkhu na[^024-6] iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘santi kho pana[^024-7] saṅghe therā rattaññū cirapabbajitā bhāravāhino, te[^024-8] tena[^024-9] paññāyissantī'; ti, attanā[^024-10] vo[^024-11] yogaṃ āpajjati. Ime kho bhikkhave satta dhammā sekhassa bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattanti.

3. Satt'; ime bhikkhave dhammā sekhassa bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame satta?

<a id="page-025"></a>

4. Na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyesu guttadvāratā, bhojane mattaññutā. Santi kho pana saṅghe saṅghakaraṇīyāni, tatra sekho[^025-1] bhikkhu iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘santi kho pana[^025-2] saṅghe therā rattaññū cirapabbajitā bhāravāhino, te[^025-3] tena paññāyissantī'; ti[^025-3], attanā na vo[^025-4] yogaṃ āpajjati. Ime kho bhikkhave satta dhammā sekhassa bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattantī ti. XXVII.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave dhammā upāsakassa parihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame satta?

2. Bhikkhudassanaṃ[^025-5] hāpeti, saddhammasavanaṃ[^025-6] pamajjati, adhisīle na sikkhati, appasādabahulo hoti bhikkhūsu theresu c'; eva[^025-7] navesu ca majjhimesu ca, upārambhacitto dhammaṃ suṇāti randhagavesī, ito bahiddhā dakkhineyyaṃ gavesati, tattha ca[^025-8] pubbakāraṃ karoti. Ime kho bhikkhave satta dhammā upāsakassa parihānāya saṃvattanti.

3. Satt'; ime bhikkhave dhammā upāsakassa aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame satta?

4. Bhikkhudassanaṃ na hāpeti, saddhammasavanaṃ[^025-9] na ppamajjati, adhisīle sikkhati, pasādabahulo[^025-10] hoti bhikkhūsu theresu c'; eva navesu ca majjhimesu[^025-11] ca, anupārambhacitto dhammaṃ suṇāti na randhagavesī, na[^025-12] ito bahiddhā dakkhiṇeyyaṃ gavesati, idha ca pubbakāraṃ karoti. Ime kho bhikkhave satta dhammā upāsakassa aparihānāya saṃvattantī ti[^025-13].

Dassanaṃ bhāvitattānaṃ yo hāpeti upāsako savanañ ca[^026-1] ariyadhammānaṃ adhisīle na sikkhati appasādo ca bhikkhūsu bhīyo bhīyo pavaḍḍhati upārambhakacitto ca saddhammaṃ sotum icchati ito ca bahiddhā aññaṃ dakkhiṇeyyaṃ gavesati tatth'; eva[^026-2] ca pubbakāraṃ yo karoti upāsako: ete kho parihānīye satta dhamme sudesite upāsako sevamāno saddhammā parihāyati. Dassanaṃ bhāvitattānaṃ yo na hāpeti upāsako savanañ ca[^026-3] ariyadhammānaṃ adhisīle ca sikkhati pasādo c'; assa bhikkhūsu bhīyo bhīyo pavaḍḍhati anupārambhacitto ca saddhammaṃ sotum icchati na ito[^026-4] bahiddhā aññaṃ dakkhiṇeyyaṃ gavesati idh'; eva[^026-5] ca pubbakāraṃ yo karoti upāsako: ete kho aparihānīye[^026-6] satta dhamme sudesite upāsako sevamāno saddhammā na parihāyatī ti. XXVIII-XXX.

Ime kho bhikkhave satta upāsakassa sambhavā ti.

Dassanaṃ bhāvitattānaṃ yo hāpeti upāsako savanañ ca ariyadhammānaṃ adhisīle na sikkhati[^027-1] appasādo ca bhikkhūsu bhīyo bhīyo pavaḍḍhati upārambhakacitto ca saddhammaṃ sotum icchati ito ca bahiddhā aññaṃ dakkhiṇeyyaṃ gavesati tatth'; eva ca pubbakāraṃ yo karoti upāsako: ete kho parihānīye satta dhamme sudesite upāsako sevamāno saddhammā parihāyati. Dassanaṃ bhāvitattānaṃ yo na hāpeti upāsako savanañ ca ariyadhammānaṃ adhisīle ca sikkhati pasādo c'; assa bhikkhūsu bhīyo bhīyo pavaḍḍhati anupārambhacitto ca saddhammaṃ sotum icchati na ito bahiddhā aññaṃ dakkhiṇeyyaṃ gavesati idh'; eva ca pubbakāraṃ yo karoti upāsako: ete kho aparihānīye satta dhamme sudesite upāsako sevamāno saddhammā na parihāyatī ti. Vajjīvaggo[^027-2] tatiyo[^027-3]. Tass'[^027-4] uddānaṃ: Sārandado[^027-5] Vassakāro[^027-6] bhikkhu kammañ[^027-7] ca saddhiyaṃ[^027-8] Bodhisaññaṃ[^027-9] sekho[^027-10] ca hāni[^027-11] vipatti ca[^027-12] parābhavo ti[^027-13]. XXXI.

1. Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhasetvā yena


<!-- PTS Page 028 -->
<a id="page-028"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 028]*

Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘satt'; ime bhante dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame satta? Satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā[^028-1], saṅghagāravatā[^028-1], sikkhāgāravatā, samādhigāravatā, appamādagāravatā, paṭisanthāragāravatā[^028-2]. Ime kho bhante satta dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattantī'; ti. Idam avoca sā devatā. Samanuñño[^028-3] Satthā[^028-3] ahosi[^028-3]. Atha[^028-3] kho[^028-3] sā[^028-3] devatā[^028-3] ‘samanuñño me Satthā'; ti Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyi.

2. Atha kho Bhagavā tassā rattiyā accayena bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Imaṃ[^028-4] bhikkhave rattiṃ aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho bhikkhave[^028-5] sā devatā maṃ etad avoca ‘satt'; ime bhante dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame satta? Satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, samādhigāravatā, appamādagāravatā, paṭisanthāragāravatā. Ime kho bhante satta dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattantī'; ti. Idam avoca bhikkhave sā devatā, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyī ti. Satthugaru dhammagaru saṅghe ca tibbagāravo samādhigaru[^028-6] ātāpī[^028-6] sikkhāya[^028-7] tibbagāravo appamādagaru bhikkhu paṭisanthāragāravo abhabbo parihānāya nibbānass'; eva santike ti. XXXII.

4. Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta, sādhu kho tvaṃ Sāriputta imassa mayā[^030-9] saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāsi[^030-3]. Idha Sāriputta bhikkhu attanā ca[^030-9] satthugāravo hoti satthugāravatāya ca[^030-10] vaṇṇavādī, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū na satthugāravā te ca satthugāravatāya

samādapeti, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū satthugāravā tesañ ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālena. Attanā ca dhammagāravo hoti . . . pe[^031-1] . . . saṅghagāravo hoti . . . sikkhāgāravo hoti[^031-2] . . . samādhigāravo hoti . . . suvaco hoti . . . kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇamittatāya ca vaṇṇavādī, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū na kalyāṇamittā te ca kalyāṇamittatāya samādapeti, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū kalyāṇamittā tesañ ca[^031-3] vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālena. Imassa kho Sāriputta mayā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena attho daṭṭhabbo ti. XXXV.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgato bhikkhu[^031-4] mitto sevitabbo. Katamehi sattahi?

2. Duddadaṃ dadāti, dukkaraṃ karoti, dukkhamaṃ khamati, guyham[^031-5] assa[^031-5] āvikaroti, guyham[^031-6] assa[^031-6] pariguyhati[^031-7], āpadāsu[^031-8] na jahati[^031-9], khīṇena[^031-10] nātimaññati[^031-10]. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattah'; aṅgehi samannāgato bhikkhu[^031-11] mitto sevitabbo ti. Duddadaṃ dadāti[^031-12] mitto[^031-13], dukkarañ cāpi[^031-14] kubbati, atho pi'; ssa duruttāni khamati dukkhamāni pi, guyhañ ca tassa[^031-15] akkhāti, guyhassa[^031-16] pariguyhati[^031-17], āpadāsu na jahāti[^031-18], khīṇena[^031-19] nātimaññati[^031-19]: yasmiṃ[^031-20] etāni ṭhānāni saṃvijjanti'; dha[^031-21] puggale, so mitto mittakāmena bhajitabbo tathāvidho ti.


<!-- PTS Page 032 -->
<a id="page-032"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 032]*

XXXVI.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu mitto sevitabbo bhajitabbo payirupāsitabbo api panujjamānena pi. Katamehi sattahi?

2. Piyo[^032-1] hoti manāpo ca, garu ca, bhāvanīyo ca, vattā ca, vacanakkhamo ca, gambhīrañ[^032-2] ca[^032-3] kathaṃ kattā hoti, no ca[^032-4] aṭṭhāne[^032-4] niyojeti. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu mitto sevitabbo bhajitabbo payirupāsitabbo api panujjamānena pī ti. Piyo ca[^032-3] garu bhāvanīyo vattā ca vacanakkhamo gambhīrañ ca kathaṃ kattā no c'; aṭṭhāne[^032-5] niyojaye[^032-6]: yasmiṃ[^032-7] etāni ṭhānāni saṃvijjanti 'dha[^032-8] puggale, so mitto mittakāmena atthakāmānukampako[^032-9] api nāsiyamānena bhajitabbo tathāvidho ti. XXXVII.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu na cirass'; eva catasso paṭisambhidā sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyya. Katamehi sattahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu ‘idam[^032-10] me cetaso līnattan[^032-11]' ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ vā[^032-3] cittaṃ ‘ajjhattaṃ me saṃkhittaṃ cittan'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘bahiddhā me vikkhittaṃ cittan'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Tassa viditā vedanā uppajjanti viditā[^032-12] upaṭṭhahanti[^032-12] viditā abbhatthaṃ[^032-13] gacchanti, viditā saññā uppajjanti viditā upaṭṭhahanti[^032-14]


<!-- PTS Page 033 -->
<a id="page-033"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 033]*

viditā abbhatthaṃ[^033-1] gacchanti, viditā vitakkā uppajjanti viditā upaṭṭhahanti viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti, sappāyāsappāyesu kho pan'; assa dhammesu hīnappaṇītesu[^033-2] kaṇhasukkasappaṭibhāgesu nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ hoti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ[^033-3] suppaṭividdhaṃ paññāya. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu na cirass'; eva catasso paṭisambhidā sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyya[^033-4].

3. Sattahi bhikkhave[^033-5] dhammehi samannāgato Sāriputto catasso paṭisambhidā sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Katamehi sattahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave Sāriputto ‘idam me cetaso līnattan'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ vā[^033-6] cittaṃ[^033-7] ‘ajjhattaṃ me saṃkhittaṃ cittan'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, bahiddhā vā[^033-6] vikkhittaṃ[^033-8] cittaṃ ‘bahiddhā me vikkhittaṃ cittan'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Tassa viditā vedanā uppajjanti viditā upaṭṭhahanti viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti, viditā saññā[^033-9] uppajjanti viditā upaṭṭhahanti viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti, viditā vitakkā uppajjanti viditā upaṭṭhahanti viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti, sappāyāsappāyesu kho pan' assa dhammesu hīnappaṇītesu kaṇhasukkasappaṭibhāgesu nimittaṃ[^033-10] suggahitaṃ hoti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ paññāya. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato Sāriputto catasso paṭisambhidā sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharatī ti.


<!-- PTS Page 034 -->
<a id="page-034"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 034]*

XXXVIII.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu cittaṃ[^034-1] vase[^034-2] vatteti[^034-3], no ca bhikkhu cittassa vasena vattati. Katamehi sattahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu samādhikusalo hoti, samādhissa samāpattikusalo hoti, samādhissa[^034-4] ṭhitikusalo[^034-4] hoti[^034-4], samādhissa vuṭṭhānakusalo hoti, samādhissa kallitakusalo[^034-5] hoti, samādhissa gocarakusalo hoti, samādhissa abhinihārakusalo hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu cittaṃ[^034-6] vase[^034-2] vatteti, no ca bhikkhu cittassa vasena vattati[^034-7].

3. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato Sāriputto cittaṃ vase[^034-8] vatteti, no ca Sāriputto cittassa vasena vattati. Katamehi sattahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave Sāriputto samādhikusalo hoti[^034-9], samādhissa samāpattikusalo, samādhissa[^034-4] ṭhitikusalo[^034-4], samādhissa vuṭṭhānakusalo, samādhissa kallitakusalo[^034-10], samādhissa gocarakusalo, samādhissa abhinihārakusalo[^034-11]. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato Sāriputto cittaṃ vase[^034-8] vatteti, no ca[^034-4] Sāriputto cittassa vasena vattatī ti. XXXIX.

1. Ekaṃ[^034-12] samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Sāvatthiyaṃ[^034-13] piṇḍāya pāvisi. Atha kho āyasmato Sāri-


<!-- PTS Page 035 -->
<a id="page-035"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 035]*

puttassa etad ahosi ‘atippago kho tāva Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ; yan nūnāhaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkameyyan'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi.

2. Tena kho pana samayena tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ sannisinnānaṃ sannipatitānaṃ ayam antarākathā udapādi ‘yo hi koci āvuso dvādasa vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, <<niddaso[^035-1] bhikkhū>> ti alaṃ vacanāyā'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ neva[^035-2] abhinandi na ppaṭikkosi[^035-3]; anabhinanditvā[^035-4] appaṭikkositvā[^035-4] uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi ‘Bhagavato santike etassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī'[^035-5] ti.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ[^035-6] nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘idhāhaṃ bhante pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Sāvatthiyaṃ[^035-7] piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ[^035-8]. Tassa mayhaṃ bhante etad ahosi: atippago kho tāva Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ; yan nūnāhaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkameyyan ti. Atha khvāhaṃ[^035-9] bhante yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkamiṃ[^035-10], upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodiṃ[^035-11], sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃ[^035-12]. Tena kho pana bhante samayena tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ sannisinnānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 036 -->
<a id="page-036"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 036]*

sannipatitānaṃ ayam antarākathā udapādi: yo hi koci āvuso dvādasa vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, <<niddaso bhikkhū>> ti alaṃ vacanāyā ti. Atha khvāhaṃ bhante tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ neva abhinandiṃ[^036-1] na ppaṭikkosiṃ[^036-2]; anabhinanditvā appaṭikkositvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmiṃ[^036-3]: Bhagavato santike etassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī[^036-4] ti. Sakkā nu kho bhante imasmiṃ dhammavinaye kevalaṃ vassagaṇanamattena niddaso bhikkhu[^036-5] paññāpetun'; ti? ‘Na kho Sāriputta sakkā imasmiṃ dhammavinaye kevalaṃ vassagaṇanamattena niddaso bhikkhu[^036-6] paññāpetuṃ[^036-7]. Satta kho imāni Sāriputta niddasavatthūni mayā sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā paveditāni. Katamāni satta?

4. Idha Sāriputta bhikkhu sikkhāsamādāne tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca sikkhāsamādāne avigatapemo[^036-8], dhammanisantiyā[^036-9] tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca dhammanisantiyā[^036-9] avigatapemo, icchāvinaye tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca icchāvinaye avigatapemo, paṭisallāne[^036-10] tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca paṭisallāne[^036-10] avigatapemo, viriyārambhe tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca viriyārambhe avigatapemo, satinepakke tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca satinepakke avigatapemo, diṭṭhipaṭivedhe[^036-11] tibbacchando hoti āyatiñ ca diṭṭhipativedhe[^036-11] avigatapemo. Imāni kho Sāriputta satta[^036-12] niddasavatthūni mayā sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā paveditāni[^036-6].

5. Imehi[^036-13] kho Sāriputta sattahi[^036-14] niddasavatthūhi[^036-15] samannāgato bhikkhu dvādasa ce[^036-16] pi[^036-16] vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddaso bhikkhū'; ti alaṃ vacanāya, catuvīsati[^036-17] ce pi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddaso[^036-18] bhikkhū'; ti


<!-- PTS Page 037 -->
<a id="page-037"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 037]*

alaṃ vacanāya, chattiṃsa[^037-1] ce pi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddaso[^037-2] bhikkhū'; ti alaṃ vacanāya, aṭṭhacattārisañ[^037-3] ce pi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddaso[^037-4] bhikkhū'; ti alaṃ vacanāyā ti. XL.

1. Evaṃ[^037-5] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kosambiyaṃ viharati Ghositārāme. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Kosambiyaṃ[^037-6] piṇḍāya pāvisi. Atha kho āyasmato Ānandassa etad ahosi ‘atippago kho tāva Kosambiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ; yan nūnāhaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkameyyan'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo[^037-7] ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ[^037-8] sārāṇīyaṃ[^037-8] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi.

2. Tena kho pana samayena tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ sannisinnānaṃ sannipatitānaṃ[^037-8] ayam antarākathā udapādi ‘yo[^037-9] hi koci āvuso dvādasa vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, <<niddaso bhikkhū>> ti alaṃ vacanāyā'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ neva abhinandi na ppaṭikkosi[^037-10]; anabhinanditvā[^037-11] appaṭikkositvā[^037-11] uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi ‘Bhagavato santike etassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī[^037-12] ti.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando Kosambiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto yena[^037-8] Bhagavā[^037-8] ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando


<!-- PTS Page 038 -->
<a id="page-038"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 038]*

Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘idhāhaṃ bhante pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Kosambiyaṃ[^038-1] piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ[^038-2]. Tassa mayhaṃ bhante etad ahosi: atippago kho tāva Kosambiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ; yan nūnāhaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkameyyan ti[^038-3]. Atha khvāhaṃ bhante yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkamiṃ[^038-4], upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodiṃ[^038-5], sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ[^038-6] sārāṇīyaṃ[^038-6] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃ[^038-7]. Tena kho pana bhante samayena tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ sannisinnānaṃ sannipatitānaṃ ayam antarākathā udapādi: yo hi koci āvuso dvādasa vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, <<niddaso bhikkhū>> ti alaṃ vacanāyā ti. Atha khvāhaṃ bhante tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ neva abhinandiṃ[^038-8] na ppaṭikkosiṃ[^038-9]; anabhinanditvā appaṭikkositvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmiṃ[^038-10]: Bhagavato santike etassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī[^038-11] ti. Sakkā nu kho bhante imasmiṃ dhammavinaye kevalaṃ vassagaṇanamattena niddaso bhikkhu paññāpetun'; ti? Na kho Ānanda sakkā imasmiṃ dhammavinaye kevalaṃ vassagaṇanamattena niddaso bhikkhu paññāpetuṃ[^038-12]. Satta kho imāni Ānanda niddasavatthūni mayā sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā paveditāni. Katamāni satta?

4. Idh'; Ānanda bhikkhu saddho hoti, hirimā[^038-13] hoti, ottappī[^038-14] hoti, bahussuto hoti, āraddhavirayo hoti, satimā hoti, paññavā hoti. Imāni kho Ānanda satta niddasavatthūni mayā sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā paveditāni.


<!-- PTS Page 039 -->
<a id="page-039"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 039]*

5. Imehi kho Ānanda sattahi niddasavatthūhi samannāgato bhikkhu dvādasa[^039-1] ce pi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, <<niddaso bhikkhū>> ti alaṃ vacanāya[^039-2], catuvīsati[^039-3] ce pi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, <<niddaso[^039-4] bhikkhū>> ti alaṃ vacanāya[^039-5], chattiṃsa[^039-6] ce pi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, <<niddaso[^039-7] bhikkhū>> ti alaṃ vacanāya, aṭṭhacattārisañ[^039-8] ce pi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, <<niddaso[^039-9] bhikkhū>> ti alaṃ vacanāyā ti. Devatāvaggo[^039-10] catuttho. Tassa[^039-11] uddānaṃ: Appamādo hirimā ca dve suvacā duve sakhā Dve paṭisambhidā dve vasā niddasavatthū 'pare dve ti[^039-12]. XLI.

1. Satt'; imā bhikkhave viññāṇaṭṭhitiyo. Katamā satta?

2. Santi bhikkhave sattā nānattakāyā nānattasaññino, seyyathā pi manussā ekacce ca[^039-13] devā[^039-14] ekacce ca[^039-15] vinipātikā. Ayaṃ paṭhamā viññāṇaṭṭhiti.


<!-- PTS Page 040 -->
<a id="page-040"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 040]*

3. Santi bhikkhave sattā nānattakāyā ekattasaññino, seyyathā pi devā {Brahmakāyikā}[^040-1] paṭhamābhinibbattā. Ayaṃ dutiyā viññāṇaṭṭhiti.

4. Santi bhikkhave sattā ekattakāyā nānattasaññino, seyyathā pi devā {Ābhassarā}. Ayaṃ tatiyā viññāṇaṭṭhiti.

5. Santi bhikkhave sattā ekattakāyā ekattasaññino, seyyathā pi devā {Subhakiṇhā}[^040-2]. Ayaṃ catutthā[^040-3] viññāṇaṭṭhiti.

6. Santi bhikkhave sattā sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamma paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto[^040-4] ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanūpagā. Ayaṃ pañcamā[^040-5] viññāṇaṭṭhiti.

7. Santi bhikkhave sattā sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanūpagā. Ayaṃ chaṭṭhā viññāṇaṭṭhiti.

8. Santi bhikkhave sattā sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanūpagā. Ayaṃ sattamā[^040-6] viññāṇaṭṭhiti. Imā kho bhikkhave satta viññāṇaṭṭhitiyo ti. XLII.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave samādhiparikkhārā. Katame satta?

2. Sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvācā, sammākammanto, sammā-ājīvo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati. Yā kho bhikkhave imehi sattah'[^040-7] aṅgehi[^040-8] cittass'; ekaggatā parikkhatā-, ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ariyo sammāsamādhi[^040-10] sa[^040-11]-upaniso iti pi saparikkhāro[^040-12] iti pī ti.


<!-- PTS Page 041 -->
<a id="page-041"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 041]*

XLIII.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave aggī. Katame satta?

2. Rāgaggi, dosaggi, mohaggi, āhuneyyaggi[^041-1], gahapataggi, dakkhiṇeyyaggi, kaṭṭhaggi. Ime kho bhikkhave satta aggī ti. XLIV.

1. Ekaṃ[^041-2] samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tena kho pana samayena Uggatasarīrassa brāhmaṇassa mahāyañño[^041-3] upakkhaṭo[^041-4] hoti: pañca usabhasatāni thūṇūpanītāni[^041-5] honti yaññatthāya, pañca vacchatarasatāni thūṇūpanītāni honti yaññatthāya, pañca vacchatarisatāni thūṇūpanītāni honti yaññatthāya, pañca ajasatāni thūṇūpanītāni honti yaññatthāya, pañca urabbhasatāni thūṇūpanītāni honti yaññatthāya. Atha kho Uggatasarīro brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi; sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Uggatasarīro brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sutaṃ me taṃ bho Gotama: aggissa ādhānaṃ[^041-6] yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti mahānisaṃsan'; ti. ‘Mayā pi kho etaṃ brāhmaṇa sutaṃ: aggissa ādhānaṃ yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti mahānisaṃsan'; ti. Dutiyam pi kho[^041-7] . . .[^041-8] tatiyam pi kho Uggatasarīro brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sutaṃ me taṃ bho Gotama: aggissa ādhānaṃ yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti mahānisaṃsan'; ti. ‘Mayā pi kho etaṃ[^041-9] brāhmaṇa sutaṃ: aggissa ādhānaṃ yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti mahānisaṃsan'; ti. ‘Tayidaṃ[^041-10] bho


<!-- PTS Page 042 -->
<a id="page-042"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 042]*

Gotama sameti[^042-1] bhoto c'; eva Gotamassa amhākañ[^042-2] ca, yad idaṃ sabbena sabban'; ti[^042-3].

2. Evaṃ vutte[^042-4] āyasmā Ānando Uggatasarīraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ etad avoca[^042-5] ‘na kho brāhmaṇa Tathāgatā evaṃ pucchitabbā: sutaṃ me taṃ bho Gotama <<aggissa ādhānaṃ yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti mahānisaṃsan>> ti. Evañ ca[^042-6] kho[^042-6] brāhmaṇa Tathāgatā pucchitabbā[^042-7]: ahañ hi bhante aggiṃ[^042-8] ādhātukāmo[^042-9] yūpaṃ ussāpetukāmo; ovadatu maṃ bhante Bhagavā, anusāsatu maṃ bhante Bhagavā, yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā'; ti.

3. Atha kho Uggatasarīro brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ahañ hi bho Gotama aggiṃ[^042-8] ādhātukāmo yūpaṃ ussāpetukāmo; ovadatu maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo, anusāsatu maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo, yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā'; ti. Aggiṃ brāhmaṇa ādhento[^042-10] yūpaṃ ussāpento pubb'; eva yaññā[^042-11] tīṇi satthāni ussāpeti[^042-12] akusalāni dukkhudrayāni[^042-13] dukkhavipākāni. Katamāni tīṇi?

4. Kāyasatthaṃ, vacīsatthaṃ, manosatthaṃ. Aggiṃ brāhmaṇa ādhento yūpaṃ ussāpento pubb'; eva yaññā[^042-14] evaṃ cittaṃ uppādeti ‘ettakā usabhā haññantu yaññatthāya, ettakā vacchatarā haññantu yaññatthāya, ettakā vacchatariyo haññantu yaññatthāya, ettakā ajā haññantu yaññatthāya, ettakā urabbhā haññantu yaññatthāyā'; ti. So[^042-6] ‘puññaṃ karomī'; ti[^042-15] apuññaṃ karoti, ‘kusalaṃ karomī'; ti akusalaṃ karoti, ‘sugatiyā[^042-16] maggaṃ pariyesāmī[^042-17]'; ti duggatiyā maggaṃ pariyesati. Aggiṃ brāhmaṇa ādhento yūpaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 043 -->
<a id="page-043"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 043]*

ussāpento pubb'; eva yaññā[^043-1] idaṃ paṭhamaṃ manosatthaṃ ussāpeti akusalaṃ[^043-2] dukkhudrayaṃ[^043-3] dukkhavipākaṃ.

5. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa aggiṃ ādhento yūpaṃ ussāpento pubb'; eva yaññā evaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati ‘ettakā usabhā haññantu yaññatthāya, ettakā vacchatarā haññantu yaññatthāya, ettakā vacchatariyo haññantu yaññatthāya, ettakā ajā haññantu yaññatthāya, ettakā urabbhā haññantu yaññatthāyā'; ti. So ‘puññaṃ karomī[^043-2]'; ti[^043-4] apuññaṃ[^043-2] karoti, ‘kusalaṃ karomī'; ti akusalaṃ karoti, ‘sugatiyā[^043-5] maggaṃ pariyesāmī'; ti duggatiyā maggaṃ pariyesati. Aggiṃ brāhmaṇa ādhento yūpaṃ ussāpento pubb'; eva yaññā idaṃ dutiyaṃ vacīsatthaṃ ussāpeti akusalaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ[^043-3] dukkhavipākaṃ.

6. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa aggiṃ ādhento yūpaṃ ussāpento pubb'; eva yaññā sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samārabbhati[^043-6] ‘usabhā haññantu yaññatthāya', sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samārabbhati ‘vacchatarā haññantu yaññatthāya', sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samārabbhati ‘vacchatariyo haññantu yaññatthāya', sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samārabbhati ‘ajā haññantu yaññatthāya', sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samābbharati ‘urabbhā haññantu yaññatthāyā' ti. So ‘puññaṃ karomī'; ti[^043-7] apuññaṃ karoti, ‘kusalaṃ karomī'; ti akusalaṃ karoti, ‘sugatiyā[^043-8] maggaṃ pariyesāmī' ti duggatiyā maggaṃ pariyesati. Aggiṃ brāhmaṇa ādhento yūpaṃ ussāpento pubb'; eva yaññā idaṃ tatiyaṃ kāyasatthaṃ ussāpeti akusalaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ[^043-3] dukkhavipākaṃ. Aggiṃ brāhmaṇa ādhento yūpaṃ ussāpento pubb'; eva yaññā imāni tīṇi satthāni ussāpeti akusalāni dukkhudrayāni[^043-9] dukkhavipākāni.

7. Tayo'; me brāhmaṇa aggī pahātabbā parivajjetabbā, na sevitabbā. Katame tayo?


<!-- PTS Page 044 -->
<a id="page-044"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 044]*

8. Rāgaggi, dosaggi, mohaggi. Kasmā cāyaṃ brāhmaṇa rāgaggi pahātabbo parivajjetabbo, na sevitabbo?

9. Ratto kho brāhmaṇa rāgena abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto kāyena duccaritaṃ carati, vācāya duccaritaṃ carati, manasā duccaritaṃ carati. So kāyena duccaritaṃ caritvā vācāya duccaritaṃ[^044-1] caritvā[^044-1] manasā duccaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^044-2]. Tasmāyaṃ[^044-3] rāgaggi pahātabbo parivajjetabbo, na sevitabbo. Kasmā cāyaṃ brāhmaṇa dosaggi[^044-4] pahātabbo parivajjetabbo, na sevitabbo?

10. Duṭṭho[^044-5] kho brāhmaṇa dosena abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto kāyena duccaritaṃ carati, vācāya[^044-6] duccaritaṃ[^044-7] carati[^044-7], manasā duccaritaṃ[^044-8] carati[^044-7]. So kāyena duccaritaṃ caritvā vācāya duccaritaṃ[^044-9] caritvā[^044-9] manasā duccaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati. Tasmāyaṃ[^044-10] dosaggi pahātabbo parivajjetabbo, na sevitabbo. Kasmā cāyaṃ brāhmaṇa mohaggi pahātabbo parivajjetabbo, na sevitabbo?

11. Mūḷho kho brāhmaṇa mohena abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto kāyena duccaritaṃ carati, vācāya duccaritaṃ carati, manasā duccaritaṃ carati. So kāyena duccaritaṃ caritvā vācāya[^044-11] duccaritaṃ caritvā manasā duccaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^044-2]. Tasmāyaṃ[^044-10] mohaggi pahātabbo parivajjetabbo, na sevitabbo. Ime kho brāhmaṇa tayo aggī pahātabbā parivajjetabbā, na sevitabbā.

12. Tayo kho[^044-12] 'me[^044-7] brāhmaṇa aggī sakkatvā garukatvā[^044-13] mānetvā pūjetvā sammā sukhaṃ parihātabbā. Katame tayo?


<!-- PTS Page 045 -->
<a id="page-045"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 045]*

13. Āhuneyyaggi[^045-1], gahapataggi, dakkhiṇeyyaggi. Katamo ca brāhmaṇa āhuneyyaggi?

14. Idha brāhmaṇa yassa[^045-2] te honti ‘mātā'; ti[^045-3] vā[^045-3] ‘pitā' ti vā: ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa āhuneyyaggi. Taṃ kissa hetu? Ato[^045-4] 'yaṃ[^045-5] brāhmaṇa āhuto sambhūto[^045-6]. Tasmāyaṃ[^045-7] āhuneyyaggi sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā sammā sukhaṃ parihātabbo[^045-8]. Katamo ca brāhmaṇa gahapataggi?

15. Idha brāhmaṇa[^045-9] yassa[^045-2] te honti ‘puttā'; ti vā ‘dārā' ti vā ‘dāsā'; ti vā[^045-10] ‘pessā[^045-11]'; ti vā ‘kammakarā'; ti vā[^045-12]: ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa[^045-13] gahapataggi. Tasmāyaṃ[^045-14] gahapataggi sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā sammā sukhaṃ parihātabbo. Katamo ca brāhmaṇa dakkhiṇeyyaggi?

16. Idha brāhmaṇa ye te samaṇabrāhmaṇā madappamādā[^045-15] paṭiviratā khantisoracce niviṭṭhā ekam attānaṃ damenti[^045-3] ekam[^045-3] attānaṃ[^045-3] samenti[^045-3] ekam[^045-3] attānaṃ[^045-3] parinibbāpenti: ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa dakkhiṇeyyaggi. Tasmāyaṃ[^045-14] dakkhiṇeyyaggi[^045-16] sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā sammā sukhaṃ parihātabbo. Ime kho brāhmaṇa tayo aggī sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā sammā sukhaṃ[^045-17] parihātabbā. Ayaṃ kho[^045-18] pana brāhmaṇa kaṭṭhaggi kālena kālaṃ ujjaletabbo, kālena kālaṃ ajjhupekkhitabbo, kālena kālaṃ nibbāpetabbo, kālena kālaṃ nikkhipitabbo ti.

17. Evaṃ vutte Uggatasarīro brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama abhikkantaṃ[^045-19] bho Gotama, upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge


<!-- PTS Page 046 -->
<a id="page-046"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 046]*

pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ. Esāhaṃ[^046-1] bho Gotama pañca[^046-2] usabhasatāni muñcāmi jīvitaṃ[^046-3] demi[^046-3], pañca vacchatarasatāni muñcāmi jīvitaṃ demi, pañca vacchatarisatāni muñcāmi jīvitaṃ demi, pañca ajasatāni muñcāmi jīvitaṃ demi, pañca[^046-4] urabbhasatāni muñcāmi jīvitaṃ demi; haritāni c'; eva tiṇāni khādantu sītāni ca pāniyāni pivantu, sīto ca nesaṃ[^046-5] vāto upavāyatū[^046-6]'; ti. XLV.

1. Satt'; imā bhikkhave saññā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā. Katamā satta?

2. Asubhasaññā, maraṇasaññā, āhāre paṭikkūlasaññā, sabbaloke anabhiratasaññā, aniccasaññā, anicce dukkhasaññā, dukkhe anattasaññā[^046-7]. Imā kho bhikkhave satta saññā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti. XLVI.

1. Satt'; imā bhikkhave saññā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā. Katamā satta?

2. Asubhasaññā, maraṇasaññā, āhāre paṭikkūlasaññā, sabbaloke anabhiratasaññā, aniccasaññā, anicce dukkhasaññā, dukkhe anattasaññā[^046-8].

3. Asubhasaññā bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

4. Asubhasaññāparicitena bhikkhave bhikkhuno[^046-9] cetasā bahulaṃ viharato methunadhammasamāpattiyā cittaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 047 -->
<a id="page-047"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 047]*

paṭilīyati paṭikuṭati[^047-1] paṭivaṭṭati[^047-2] na sampasārīyati, upekhā[^047-3] vā pāṭikkūlyatā[^047-4] vā saṇṭhāti. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave kukkuṭapattaṃ vā nahārudaddulaṃ[^047-5] vā aggimhi pakkhittaṃ paṭilīyati paṭikuṭati[^047-6] paṭivaṭṭati[^047-7] na sampasārīyati, evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhuno asubhasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato methunadhammasamāpattiyā cittaṃ paṭilīyati paṭikuṭati[^047-8] paṭivaṭṭati[^047-9] na sampasārīyati, upekhā[^047-3] vā pāṭikkūlyatā[^047-10] vā saṇṭhāti. Sace bhikkhave bhikkhuno asubhasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato methunadhammasamāpattiyā cittaṃ anusandati[^047-11], appāṭikkūlyatā vā[^047-12] saṇṭhāti, veditabbam etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā ‘abhāvitā me asubhasaññā, natthi me pubbenāparaṃ viseso, appattaṃ me bhāvanāphalan'; ti. Iti ha tattha sampajāno hoti. Sace pana bhikkhave bhikkhuno asubhasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato methunadhammasamāpattiyā cittaṃ paṭilīyati paṭikuṭati[^047-8] paṭivaṭṭati[^047-9] na sampasārīyati, upekhā[^047-3] vā pāṭikkūlyatā vā saṇṭhāti, veditabbam etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā ‘bhāvitā me asubhasaññā, atthi me pubbenāparaṃ viseso, pattaṃ me bhāvanāphalan'; ti. Iti ha tattha sampajāno hoti. Asubhasaññā bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

5. Maraṇasaññā bhikkhave[^047-13] bhāvitā bahulīkatā maha-

pphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

6. Maraṇasaññāparicitena bhikkhave bhikkhuno cetasā bahulaṃ viharato jīvitanikantiyā cittaṃ paṭilīyati paṭikuṭati[^048-1] paṭivaṭṭati[^048-2] na sampasārīyati, upekhā[^048-3] vā pāṭikkūlyatā vā saṇṭhāti. paṭikkūlasaññā bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti*) mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

9. Sabbaloke anabhiratasaññā[^050-1] bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ[^050-2] vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

10. Sabbaloke anabhiratasaññāparicitena bhikkhave bhikkhuno cetasā bahulaṃ viharato lokacittesu cittaṃ paṭilīyati[^050-3] paṭikuṭati[^050-4] paṭivaṭṭati[^050-5] na sampasārīyati, upekhā vā pāṭikkūlyatā[^050-6] vā saṇṭhāti. Seyyathā pi kukkuṭapattaṃ vā nahārudaddulaṃ vā aggimhi pakkhittaṃ paṭilīyati paṭikuṭati[^050-4] paṭivaṭṭati[^050-7] na sampasārīyati, evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhuno sabbaloke anabhiratasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato lokacittesu cittaṃ paṭilīyati[^050-8] paṭikuṭati[^050-4] paṭivaṭṭati[^050-9] na sampasārīyati, upekhā[^050-10] vā pāṭikkūlyatā[^050-11] vā saṇṭhāti. Sace[^050-12] bhikkhave bhikkhuno sabbaloke anabhiratasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato lokacittesu cittaṃ anusandati, appāṭikkūlyatā[^050-13] vā[^050-14] saṇṭhāti, veditabbam etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā[^050-15] ‘abhāvitā me sabbaloke anabhiratasaññā, natthi[^050-16] me pubbenāparaṃ viseso, appattaṃ[^050-17] me bhāvanāphalan'; ti. Iti ha tattha sampajāno hoti. Sace pana bhikkhave

##### *[PTS Page 051]*

bhikkhuno sabbaloke anabhiratasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato lokacittesu[^051-1] cittaṃ paṭilīyati[^051-2] paṭikuṭati[^051-3] paṭivaṭṭati[^051-4] na sampasārīyati, upekhā[^051-5] vā pāṭikkūlyatā vā saṇṭhāti, veditabbam etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā[^051-6] ‘bhāvitā me sabbaloke anabhiratasaññā, atthi me pubbenāparaṃ viseso, pattaṃ me bhāvanāphalan'; ti. Iti ha tattha sampajāno hoti. Sabbaloke anabhiratasaññā bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

11. Aniccasaññā bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

Seyyathā pi bhikkhave kukkuṭapattaṃ vā nahārudaddulaṃ vā aggimhi pakkhittaṃ paṭilīyati paṭikuṭati[^051-3] paṭivaṭṭati[^051-4] na sampasārīyati, evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhuno aniccasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato lābhasakkārasiloke cittaṃ paṭilīyati[^051-7] paṭikuṭati paṭivaṭṭati[^051-4] na sampasārīyati, upekhā[^051-5] vā pāṭikkūlyatā vā saṇṭhāti. Sace bhikkhave bhikkhuno aniccasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato lābhasakkārasiloke cittaṃ anusandati, appāṭikkūlyatā[^051-8] vā[^051-9] saṇṭhāti, veditabbam etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā[^051-10] ‘abhāvitā me aniccasaññā, natthi me pubbenāparaṃ viseso, appattaṃ me bhāvanāphalan'; ti. Iti ha tattha sampajāno hoti. Sace pana bhikkhave bhikkhuno aniccasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato lābhasakkārasiloke cittaṃ paṭilīyati

paṭikuṭati[^052-1] paṭivaṭṭati[^052-2] na sampasārīyati, upekhā[^052-3] vā pāṭikkūlyatā vā saṇṭhāti, veditabbam etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā[^052-4] ‘bhāvitā me aniccasaññā, atthi me pubbenāparaṃ viseso, pattaṃ me bhāvanāphalan'; ti. Iti ha tattha sampajāno hoti. Aniccasaññā bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

13. Anicce dukkhasaññā bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

14. Anicce dukkhasaññāparicitena bhikkhave bhikkhuno cetasā bahulaṃ viharato ālasse[^052-5] kosajje[^052-6] vissaṭṭhiye[^052-7] pamāde ananuyoge appaccavekkhaṇāya tibbā bhayasaññā paccupaṭṭhitā hoti, seyyathā pi[^052-8] ukkhittāsike vadhake[^052-9]. Sace bhikkhave bhikkhuno anicce dukkhasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato ālasse[^052-10] kosajje vissaṭṭhiye pamāde ananuyoge appaccavekkhaṇāya tibbā bhayasaññā na[^052-11] paccupaṭṭhitā hoti, seyyathā pi ukkhittāsike vadhake, veditabbam etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā[^052-4] ‘abhāvitā me anicce dukkhasaññā, natthi me pubbenāparaṃ viseso, appattaṃ me bhāvanāphalan'; ti.
