hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā'; ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

15. Dukkhe anattasaññā bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

16. Dukkhe anattasaññāparicitena bhikkhave bhikkhuno cetasā bahulaṃ viharato imasmiñ ca saviññāṇake kāye bahiddhā ca sabbanimittesu ahaṃkāramamaṃkāramānāpagataṃ[^053-1] mānasaṃ hoti vidhāsamatikkantaṃ santaṃ suvimuttaṃ. Sace bhikkhave bhikkhuno dukkhe[^053-2] anattasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato imasmiñ ca saviññāṇake kāye bahiddhā ca sabbanimittesu[^053-3] ahaṃkāramamaṃkāramānāpagataṃ[^053-4] mānasaṃ na[^053-5] hoti vidhāsamatikkantaṃ santaṃ suvimuttaṃ, veditabbam etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā[^053-6] ‘abhāvitā me dukkhe anattasaññā, natthi me pubbenāparaṃ viseso, appattaṃ me bhāvanāphalan'; ti. Iti ha tattha sampajāno hoti. Sace pana[^053-5] bhikkhave bhikkhuno dukkhe anattasaññāparicitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharato imasmiñ ca saviññāṇake kāye bahiddhā ca sabbanimittesu ahaṃkāramamaṃkāramānāpagataṃ[^053-7] mānasaṃ hoti vidhāsamatikkantaṃ santaṃ suvimuttaṃ, veditabbam etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā[^053-8] ‘bhāvitā me dukkhe anattasaññā, atthi me pubbenāparaṃ viseso, pattaṃ me bhāvanāphalan'; ti. Iti ha tattha sampajāno hoti. Dukkhe anattasaññā bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ. Imā kho bhikkhave satta saññā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 054 -->
<a id="page-054"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 054]*

XLVII.

1. Atha kho Jānussoṇī[^054-1] brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ . . . pe[^054-2] . . . etad avoca ‘bhavam pi no[^054-3] Gotamo brahmacārī paṭijānātī' ti. ‘Yaṃ hi taṃ brāhmaṇa sammā vadamāno vadeyya <<akhaṇḍaṃ[^054-4] acchiddaṃ asabalaṃ akammāsaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caratī>> ti; mam'; eva taṃ brāhmaṇa sammā vadamāno vadeyya, ahaṃ hi brāhmaṇa akhaṇḍaṃ acchiddaṃ asabalaṃ akammāsaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carāmī'; ti. ‘Kiṃ pana bho Gotama brahmacariyassa khaṇḍam[^054-5] pi chiddam[^054-6] pi sabalam[^054-7] pi kammāsam[^054-8] pī'; ti?

2. Idha brāhmaṇa ekacco samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā sammābrahmacārī paṭijānamāno na h'; eva kho mātugāmena saddhiṃ dvayandvayasamāpattiṃ[^054-9] samāpajjati, api ca kho mātugāmassa ucchādanaparimaddananahāpanasambāhanaṃ[^054-10] sādiyati. So taṃ[^054-11] assādeti[^054-12] taṃ[^054-13] nikāmeti tena ca vittiṃ āpajjati. Idam pi kho brāhmaṇa[^054-14] brahmacariyassa khaṇḍam pi chiddam pi sabalam pi kammāsam pi. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa aparisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati saṃyutto methunena saṃyogena, na parimuccati jātiyā jarāmaraṇena[^054-15] sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi, na parimuccati dukkhasmā ti vadāmi.


<!-- PTS Page 055 -->
<a id="page-055"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 055]*

3. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa idh'; ekacco samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā sammābrahmacārī paṭijānamāno na h'; eva kho mātugāmena saddhiṃ dvayandvayasamāpattiṃ[^055-1] samāpajjati, na pi mātugāmassa ucchādanaparimaddananahāpanasambāhanaṃ sādiyati, api ca kho mātugāmena saddhiṃ sañjagghati saṃkīḷati saṃkeḷāyati[^055-2] . . . pe[^055-3] . . . na pi mātugāmena saddhiṃ sañjagghati saṃkīḷati saṃkeḷāyati, api ca kho mātugāmassa cakkhunā cakkhuṃ upanijjhāyati pekkhati . . .[^055-4] na pi mātugāmassa cakkhunā cakkhuṃ upanijjhāyati pekkhati, api ca kho mātugāmassa saddaṃ suṇāti tirokuḍḍaṃ[^055-5] vā tiropākāraṃ vā hasantiyā vā bhaṇantiyā vā gāyantiyā vā rodantiyā vā . . .[^055-4] na pi mātugāmassa saddaṃ suṇāti tirokuḍḍaṃ vā tiropākāraṃ vā hasantiyā vā bhaṇantiyā vā gāyantiyā vā rodantiyā vā, api ca kho yāni 'ssa tāni pubbe mātugāmena saddhiṃ hasitalapitakīḷitāni[^055-6] anussarati . . .[^055-4] na pi yāni'; ssa tāni pubbe mātugāmena saddhiṃ hasitalapitakīḷitāni[^055-7] anussarati, api ca kho passati gahapatiṃ vā gahapatiputtaṃ vā pañcahi kāmaguṇehi samappitaṃ samaṅgibhūtaṃ paricāriyamānaṃ[^055-8] . . .[^055-4] na pi passati gahapatiṃ vā gahapatiputtaṃ vā pañcahi kāmaguṇehi samappitaṃ samaṅgibhūtaṃ paricāriyamānaṃ[^055-9], api ca kho aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati ‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā'; ti. So taṃ[^055-10] assādeti[^055-11] taṃ[^055-12] nikāmeti tena[^055-13] ca vittiṃ[^055-14] āpajjati. Idam pi[^055-15] kho brāhmaṇa brahmacariyassa khaṇḍam pi chiddam pi sabalam pi kammāsam pi. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa


<!-- PTS Page 056 -->
<a id="page-056"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 056]*

aparisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati saṃyutto methunena saṃyogena, na parimuccati jātiyā jarāmaraṇena[^056-1] sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi, na parimuccati dukkhasmā ti vadāmi. Yāvakīvañ cāhaṃ brāhmaṇa imesaṃ sattannaṃ methunasaṃyogānaṃ aññataraññataraṃ[^056-2] methunasaṃyogaṃ[^056-3] attani appahīnaṃ samanupassiṃ[^056-4], neva tāvāhaṃ[^056-5] brāhmaṇa sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhiṃ[^056-6] abhisambuddho[^056-7] paccaññāsiṃ[^056-8], yato ca kho ahaṃ[^056-9] brāhmaṇa imesaṃ sattannaṃ methunasaṃyogānaṃ aññataraññataraṃ[^056-10] methunasaṃyogaṃ[^056-11] attani appahīnaṃ na[^056-12] samanupassiṃ[^056-13], athāhaṃ brāhmaṇa sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhiṃ abhisambuddho[^056-7] paccaññāsiṃ[^056-14]. Ñāṇañ ca pana me dassanaṃ udapādi ‘akuppā me cetovimutti[^056-15], ayam antimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavo'; ti. Evaṃ vutte Jānussoṇī brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘abhikkantaṃ[^056-16] bho Gotama . . . pe[^056-17] . . . upāsakaṃ[^056-18] maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti.


<!-- PTS Page 057 -->
<a id="page-057"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 057]*

XLVIII.

1. Saṃyogavisaṃyogaṃ[^057-1] vo bhikkhave dhammapariyāyaṃ desessāmi[^057-2], taṃ suṇātha . . . pe[^057-3] . . . Katamo ca[^057-4] bhikkhave saṃyogavisaṃyogo[^057-5] dhammapariyāyo?

2. Itthi[^057-6] bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ itthindriyaṃ manasikaroti itthikuttaṃ[^057-7] itthākappaṃ itthividhaṃ itthicchandaṃ itthissaraṃ[^057-8] itthālaṃkāraṃ. Sā tattha rajjati tatrābhiramati, sā tattha rattā tatrābhiratā bahiddhā purisindriyaṃ manasikaroti purisakuttaṃ purisākappaṃ purisavidhaṃ purisacchandaṃ purisassaraṃ[^057-8] purisālaṃkāraṃ. Sā tattha rajjati tatrābhiramati[^057-9], sā tattha rattā tatrābhiratā bahiddhā saṃyogaṃ ākaṅkhati; yañ c'; assā[^057-10] saṃyogapaccayā uppajjati sukhaṃ somanassaṃ, tañ ca[^057-11] ākaṅkhati. Itthatte bhikkhave abhiratā sattā purisesu saṃyogaṃ gatā. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave itthi itthattaṃ nātivattati.

3. Puriso bhikkhave ajjattaṃ purisindriyaṃ manasikaroti purisakuttaṃ purisākappaṃ purisavidhaṃ purisacchandaṃ purisassaraṃ purisālaṃkāraṃ. So tattha rajjati tatrābhiramati[^057-12], so tattha ratto tatrābhirato[^057-13] bahiddhā itthindriyaṃ manasikaroti itthikuttaṃ itthākappaṃ[^057-14] itthividhaṃ itthicchandaṃ itthissaraṃ itthālaṃkāraṃ[^057-15]. So tattha rajjati tatrābhiramati[^057-12], so tattha ratto tatrābhirato[^057-12] bahiddhā saṃyogaṃ ākaṅkhati; yañ c'; assa saṃyogapaccayā uppajjati sukhaṃ somanassaṃ, tañ ca ākaṅkhati Purisatte bhikkhave abhirato[^057-16] satto[^057-16] itthīsu saṃyogaṃ gato[^057-16]


<!-- PTS Page 058 -->
<a id="page-058"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 058]*

Evaṃ kho bhikkhave puriso purisattaṃ[^058-1] nātivattati[^058-2]. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave saṃyogo hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave visaṃyogo hoti?

4. Itthi bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ itthindriyaṃ[^058-3] na[^058-4] manasikaroti itthikuttaṃ itthākappaṃ itthividhaṃ itthicchandaṃ itthissaraṃ itthālaṃkāraṃ. Sā tattha na rajjati tatra[^058-5] nābhiramati, sā tattha arattā tatra anabhiratā bahiddhā purisindriyaṃ na[^058-6] manasikaroti purisakuttaṃ purisākappaṃ purisavidhaṃ purisacchandaṃ purisassaraṃ purisālaṃkāraṃ. Sā tattha na rajjati tatra[^058-7] nābhiramati, sā tattha arattā tatra anabhiratā bahiddhā saṃyogaṃ nākaṅkhati; yañ c'; assā[^058-8] saṃyogapaccayā uppajjati[^058-9] sukhaṃ somanassaṃ, tañ ca nākaṅkhati. Itthatte kho bhikkhave anabhiratā asattā[^058-10] purisesu visaṃyogaṃ gatā. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave itthi itthattaṃ ativattati.

5. Puriso bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ purisindriyaṃ na manasikaroti purisakuttaṃ[^058-11] purisākappaṃ purisavidhaṃ purisacchandaṃ purisassaraṃ purisālaṃkāraṃ. So tattha na rajjati tatra nābhiramati, so tattha aratto[^058-12] tatra anabhirato bahiddhā itthindriyaṃ na[^058-13] manasikaroti[^058-14] itthikuttaṃ itthākappaṃ itthividhaṃ itthicchandaṃ itthissaraṃ itthālaṃkāraṃ. So tattha na rajjati tatra nābhiramati, so tattha aratto[^058-15] tatra[^058-16] anabhirato[^058-16] bahiddhā saṃyogaṃ nākaṅkhati; yañ c'; assa saṃyogapaccayā uppajjati sukhaṃ somanassaṃ, tañ ca nākaṅkhati. Purisatte bhikkhave anabhirato[^058-17] asatto[^058-10] itthīsu visaṃyogaṃ gato[^058-17]. Evaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 059 -->
<a id="page-059"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 059]*

kho bhikkhave puriso purisattaṃ ativattati. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave visaṃyogo hoti[^059-1]. Ayaṃ kho bhikkhave saṃyogavisaṃyogo dhammapariyāyo ti. XLIX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Campāyaṃ viharati Gaggarāya pokkharaṇiyā tīre. Atha kho sambahulā[^059-2] Campeyyakā upāsakā yenāyasmā Sāriputto ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho te[^059-3] Campeyyakā upāsakā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ etad avocuṃ ‘cirassutā[^059-4] no bhante[^059-5] Bhagavato sammukhā dhammikathā, sādhu mayaṃ bhante labheyyāma Bhagavato sammukhā[^059-6] dhammikathaṃ[^059-7] savanāyā'; ti. ‘Tena h'; āvuso[^059-8] tadahu'; posathe āgaccheyyātha, app[^059-9] eva[^059-9] nāma[^059-9] labheyyātha[^059-9] Bhagavato sammukhā[^059-10] dhammikathaṃ savanāyā'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho te[^059-11] Campeyyakā upāsakā āyasmato Sāriputtassa paṭissutvā[^059-12] uṭṭhāyāsanā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkamiṃsu. Atha[^059-13] kho te[^059-11] Campeyyakā upāsakā tadahu 'posathe yenāyasmā Sāriputto ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto tehi Campeyyakehi upāsakehi saddhiṃ yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--


<!-- PTS Page 060 -->
<a id="page-060"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 060]*

2. Siyā nu kho bhante idh'; ekaccassa tādisaṃ yeva dānaṃ dinnaṃ na[^060-1] mahapphalaṃ[^060-2] na mahānisaṃsaṃ, siyā pana bhante idh'; ekaccassa tādisaṃ yeva dānaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ[^060-3] mahānisaṃsan ti? Siyā Sāriputta idh'; ekaccassa tādisaṃ yeva dānaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ[^060-3] na mahānisaṃsaṃ, siyā pana Sāriputta idh'; ekaccassa tādisaṃ yeva dānaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ[^060-3] mahānisaṃsan ti.

3. Ko nu kho bhante hetu ko paccayo, yena-m-[^060-4] idh' ekaccassa tādisaṃ yeva dānaṃ dinnaṃ[^060-5] mahapphalaṃ hoti[^060-6] na mahānisaṃsaṃ; ko pana[^060-7] bhante hetu ko paccayo, yena-m[^060-8]-idh'; ekaccassa tādisaṃ yeva dānaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti[^060-6] mahānisaṃsan ti? Idha Sāriputta ekacco sāpekho dānaṃ deti, paṭibaddhacitto[^060-9] dānaṃ deti, sannidhipekho[^060-10] dānaṃ deti, ‘imaṃ pecca[^060-11] paribhuñjissāmī'; ti dānaṃ deti[^060-12]. So[^060-13] taṃ[^060-14] dānaṃ[^060-13] deti[^060-14] samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sāriputta: dadeyya idh'; ekacco evarūpaṃ[^060-15] dānan ti? Evaṃ bhante. Tatra Sāriputta yvāyaṃ sāpekho dānaṃ deti[^060-16], paṭibaddhacitto[^060-9] dānaṃ deti, sannidhipekho[^060-17] dānaṃ deti, ‘imaṃ[^060-18] pecca paribhuñjissāmī'; ti dānaṃ deti: so taṃ dānaṃ datvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Cātummahārājikānaṃ[^060-19] devānaṃ sahavyataṃ[^060-20] upapajjati[^060-21]. So taṃ


<!-- PTS Page 061 -->
<a id="page-061"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 061]*

kammaṃ khepetvā taṃ iddhiṃ taṃ yasaṃ taṃ adhipateyyaṃ[^061-1] āgāmī[^061-2] hoti āgantā[^061-3] itthattaṃ.

4. Idha pana[^061-4] Sāriputta ekacco na h'; eva kho sāpekho dānaṃ deti, na paṭibaddhacitto dānaṃ deti, na sannidhipekho dānaṃ deti, na ‘imaṃ pecca[^061-5] paribhuñjissāmī'; ti dānaṃ deti, api ca kho ‘sāhu dānan'; ti dānaṃ deti . . . pe[^061-6] . . . na pi ‘sāhu dānan'; ti dānaṃ deti, api ca kho ‘dinnapubbaṃ[^061-7] katapubbaṃ[^061-4] pitupitāmahehi[^061-8], na[^061-9] arahāmi[^061-9] porāṇaṃ kulavaṃsaṃ[^061-10] hāpetun'; ti dānaṃ deti . . .[^061-11] na pi ‘dinnapubbaṃ katapubbaṃ pitupitāmahehi, na arahāmi porāṇaṃ[^061-4] kulavaṃsaṃ hāpetun'; ti dānaṃ deti, api ca kho ‘ahaṃ pacāmi, ime[^061-12] na pacanti, na arahāmi[^061-13] pacanto apacantānaṃ dānaṃ[^061-14] adātun[^061-15]'; ti dānaṃ deti . . .[^061-16] na pi ‘ahaṃ pacāmi[^061-17], ime na pacanti, na[^061-5] arahāmi pacanto apacantānaṃ dānaṃ[^061-14] adātun[^061-15]'; ti dānaṃ deti, api ca kho ‘yathā[^061-18] tesaṃ pubbakānaṃ isīnaṃ tāni mahāyaññāni ahesuṃ, seyyathīdaṃ Aṭṭhakassa Vāmakassa[^061-5] Vāmadevassa Vessāmittassa[^061-19] Yamataggino[^061-20] Aṅgīrasassa Bhāradvājassa Vāseṭṭhassa Kassapassa Bhaguno, evaṃ me ayaṃ dānasaṃvibhāgo bhavissatī'; ti dānaṃ deti . . .[^061-21] na pi ‘yathā tesaṃ pubbakānaṃ isīnaṃ tāni mahāyaññāni ahesuṃ, seyyathīdaṃ Aṭṭhakassa Vāmakassa[^061-5] Vāmadevassa Vessāmittassa Yamataggino Aṅgīrasassa Bhāradvājassa Vāseṭṭhassa Kassapassa Bhaguno, evaṃ me[^061-5] ayaṃ dāna-

##### *[PTS Page 062]*

saṃvibhāgo bhavissatī'; ti dānaṃ deti, api ca kho[^062-1] ‘imaṃ me dānaṃ dadato cittaṃ pasīdati, attamanatāsomanassaṃ[^062-2] upajāyatī[^062-3]'; ti dānaṃ deti . . .[^062-4] na pi ‘imaṃ me[^062-5] dānaṃ dadato cittaṃ pasīdati, attamanatāsomanassaṃ upajāyatī[^062-3]' ti dānaṃ deti, api ca kho cittālaṃkāraṃ cittaparikkhāratthaṃ[^062-6] dānaṃ deti. So taṃ dānaṃ deti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sāriputta: dadeyya idh'; ekacco evarūpaṃ dānan ti? Evaṃ bhante. Tatra Sāriputta yvāyaṃ na h'; eva kho sāpekho dānaṃ deti, na paṭibaddhacitto dānaṃ deti, na[^062-7] sannidhipekho dānaṃ deti, na ‘imaṃ pecca paribhuñjissāmī'; ti dānaṃ deti, na pi ‘sāhu[^062-8] dānan'; ti dānaṃ deti, na pi dinnapubbaṃ katapubbaṃ pitupitāmahehi, na arahāmi porāṇaṃ kulavaṃsaṃ hāpetun'; ti dānaṃ deti, na pi ‘ahaṃ pacāmi, ime na pacanti, na[^062-9] arahāmi[^062-10] pacanto[^062-11] apacantānaṃ[^062-12] dānaṃ[^062-13] adātun[^062-14]'; ti dānaṃ deti, na pi ‘yathā tesaṃ pubbakānaṃ isīnaṃ tāni mahāyaññāni ahesuṃ, seyyathīdaṃ Aṭṭhakassa Vāmakassa[^062-9] Vāmadevassa Vessāmittassa Yamataggino[^062-15] Aṅgīrasassa Bhāradvājassa Vāseṭṭhassa Kassapassa Bhaguno, evaṃ me ayaṃ[^062-9] dānasaṃvibhāgo bhavissatī'; ti dānaṃ deti, na pi ‘imaṃ me dānaṃ dadato cittaṃ pasīdati, attamanatāsomanassaṃ upajāyatī[^062-16]'; ti dānaṃ deti, api ca kho cittālaṃkāraṃ cittaparikkhāratthaṃ[^062-17] dānaṃ deti: so taṃ dānaṃ datvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Brahmakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ[^062-18] upapajjati[^062-19]. So taṃ kam-


<!-- PTS Page 063 -->
<a id="page-063"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 063]*

maṃ khepetvā taṃ iddhiṃ taṃ yasaṃ taṃ adhipateyyaṃ[^063-1], anāgāmī hoti anāgantā[^063-2] itthattaṃ[^063-3]. Ayaṃ kho Sāriputta hetu ayaṃ paccayo, yena-m[^063-4]-idh' ekaccassa tādisaṃ yeva dānaṃ dinnaṃ na[^063-5] mahapphalaṃ hoti[^063-6] na[^063-7] mahānisaṃsaṃ, ayaṃ pana Sāriputta hetu ayaṃ paccayo, yena-m[^063-4]-idh'; ekaccassa tādisaṃ yeva dānaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti[^063-6] mahānisaṃsaṃ. L.

1. Evaṃ[^063-6] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ[^063-8] āyasmā ca Sāriputto āyasmā ca Mahāmoggallāno Dakkhiṇāgirismiṃ[^063-9] cārikaṃ caranti mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṃ. Tena kho pana samayena Veḷukaṇṭakī[^063-10] Nandamātā upāsikā rattiyā paccūsasamayaṃ paccuṭṭhāya[^063-11] pārāyanaṃ sarena bhāsati[^063-12]. Tena kho pana samayena Vessavaṇo[^063-13] mahārājā uttarāya[^063-14] disāya[^063-14] dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ gacchati kenacid-eva karaṇiyena. Assosi kho Vessavaṇo mahārājā Nandamātāya upāsikāya pārāyanaṃ[^063-15] sarena bhāsantiyā, sutvā kathāpariyosānaṃ āgamayamāno aṭṭhāsi. Atha kho Nandamātā upāsikā pārāyanaṃ[^063-16] sarena bhāsitvā tuṇhī ahosi. Atha kho Vessavaṇo mahārājā Nandamātāya upāsikāya kathāpariyosānaṃ viditvā abbhānumodi[^063-17] ‘sādhu bhagini sādhu bhaginī'; ti. ‘Ko pan'; eso[^063-18] bhadramukhā'; ti. ‘Ahan te[^063-19] bhagini bhātā Vessavaṇo mahārājā'; ti. ‘Sādhu bhadramukha, tena hīyo me ayaṃ dhammapariyāyo bhaṇito, idan te hotu ātitheyyan[^063-20]'; ti. ‘Sādhu bhagini, etañ c'; eva me[^063-21]


<!-- PTS Page 064 -->
<a id="page-064"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 064]*

hotu ātitheyyaṃ: sve ca[^064-1] Sāriputta-Moggallānapamukho bhikkhusaṅgho akatapātarāso Veḷukaṇṭakaṃ āgamissati, tañ ca bhikkhusaṅghaṃ parivisitvā mamaṃ[^064-2] dakkhiṇaṃ ādiseyyāsi[^064-3], etañ ca[^064-4] me bhavissati ātitheyyan'; ti.

2. Atha kho Nandamātā upāsikā tassā[^064-5] rattiyā accayena sake nivesane paṇītaṃ khādaniyaṃ bhojaniyaṃ paṭiyādāpesi. Atha kho Sāriputta-Moggallānapamukho bhikkhusaṅgho akatapātarāso yena Veḷukaṇṭako[^064-6] tad avasari. Atha kho Nandamātā upāsikā aññataraṃ[^064-7] purisaṃ āmantesi ‘ehi tvaṃ ambho purisa, ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhusaṅghassa kālaṃ ārocehi[^064-8]: kālo bhante, ayyāya Nandamātuyā[^064-9] nivesane niṭṭhitaṃ bhattan'; ti. ‘Evaṃ ayye[^064-10]'; ti kho so[^064-5] puriso Nandamātāya upāsikāya paṭissutvā[^064-11] ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhusaṅghassa kālaṃ ārocesi: kālo bhante, ayyāya Nandamātuyā[^064-12] nivesane niṭṭhitaṃ bhattan ti. Atha kho Sāriputta-Moggallānapamukho bhikkhusaṅgho pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Nandamātāya[^064-13] upāsikāya nivesanaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Atha kho Nandamātā upāsikā Sāriputta-Moggallānapamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītena khādaniyena bhojaniyena sahatthā santappesi sampavāresi. Atha kho Nandamātā upāsikā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ bhuttāviṃ[^064-14] onītapattapāṇiṃ[^064-15] ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Nandamātaraṃ upāsikaṃ āyasmā Sāriputto etad avoca ‘ko pana te[^064-16] Nandamāte bhikkhusaṅghassa abbhāgamanaṃ[^064-17] ārocesī'; ti? ‘Idhāhaṃ bhante rattiyā paccūsasamayaṃ paccuṭṭhāya pārāyanaṃ[^064-18] sarena bhāsitvā tuṇhī ahosiṃ[^064-19]. Atha kho[^064-5] bhante Vessavaṇo


<!-- PTS Page 065 -->
<a id="page-065"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 065]*

mahārājā mama[^065-1] kathāpariyosānaṃ viditvā abbhānumodi[^065-2] <<sādhu bhagini[^065-3] sādhu bhaginī>> ti. <<Ko pan'; eso bhadramukhā[^065-4]>> ti? <<Ahan te[^065-5] bhagini bhātā Vessavaṇo mahārājā>> ti. <<Sādhu bhadramukha, tena hīyo me[^065-6] ayaṃ dhammapariyāyo bhaṇito, idan te hotu ātitheyyan>> ti. <<Sādhu bhagini, etañ c'; eva me hotu ātitheyyaṃ, sve[^065-7] ca[^065-8] Sāriputta-Moggallānappamukho bhikkhusaṅgho akatapātarāso Veḷukaṇṭakaṃ āgamissati, tañ ca bhikkhusaṅghaṃ parivisitvā mamaṃ[^065-9] dakkhiṇaṃ ādiseyyāsi, etañ ca[^065-10] me bhavissati ātitheyyan>> ti. ‘Yad idaṃ bhante dāne puññaṃ[^065-11] hitaṃ[^065-12] Vessavaṇassa mahārājassa sukhāya hotū'; ti.

3. ‘Acchariyaṃ Nandamāte abbhutaṃ Nandamāte, yatra hi nāma Vessavaṇena mahārājena evaṃmahiddhikena[^065-13] evaṃmahesakkhena devaputtena sammukhā sallapissasī'; ti. ‘Na kho me bhante[^065-13] es'; eva[^065-14] acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, atthi me añño pi acchariyo abbhuto dhammo: idha me bhante Nando[^065-15] nāma ekaputtako[^065-16] piyo manāpo, taṃ rājāno kismiñci-d-eva[^065-17] kāraṇe[^065-18] okkassa[^065-19] pasayha jīvitā voropesuṃ, tasmiṃ kho panāhaṃ bhante dārake gahite vā[^065-20] gayhamāne vā vadhe[^065-21] vā vajjhamāne vā hate vā haññamāne vā nābhijānāmi[^065-22] cittassa aññathattan'; ti.

4. ‘Acchariyaṃ Nandamāte abbhutaṃ Nandamāte, yatra hi nāma cittuppādaṃ[^065-23] pi parisodhessasī'; ti. ‘Na kho me

##### *[PTS Page 066]*

bhante es'; eva acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, atthi me añño pi acchariyo abbhuto dhammo: idha me bhante sāmiko kālakato[^066-1] aññataraṃ yakkhayoniṃ[^066-2] upapanno[^066-3], so me ten[^066-4]' eva[^066-4] purimena attabhāvena uddassesi[^066-5]. Na kho panāhaṃ bhante abhijānāmi tato nidānaṃ cittassa aññathattan'; ti.

5. ‘Acchariyaṃ Nandamāte abbhutaṃ Nandamāte, yatra hi nāma cittuppādaṃ[^066-6] pi parisodhessasī[^066-7]'; ti. ‘Na kho me[^066-8] bhante es'; eva acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, atthi me añño pi[^066-4] acchariyo abbhuto dhammo: yato'; haṃ bhante sāmikassa daharass'; eva daharā ānītā[^066-9], nābhijānāmi sāmikaṃ[^066-10] manasā pi aticarittā[^066-11], kuto pana kāyenā'; ti?

6. ‘Acchariyaṃ Nandamāte abbhutaṃ Nandamāte, yatra hi nāma cittuppādamattam pi parisodhessasī'; ti. ‘Na kho me bhante es'; eva acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, atthi me añño pi acchariyo abbhuto dhammo: yadāhaṃ[^066-12] bhante upāsikā paṭidesitā[^066-13], nābhijānāmi kiñci sikkhāpadaṃ sañcicca vītikkamitā[^066-14]'; ti.

7. ‘Acchariyaṃ Nandamāte abbhutaṃ Nandamāte'; ti[^066-15]. ‘Na kho me bhante es'; eva acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, atthi me añño pi acchariyo abbhuto dhammo: idhāhaṃ bhante yāva-d-eva ākaṅkhāmi, vivicc'; eva kāmehi[^066-16] vivicca[^066-16] akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ[^066-17] jhānaṃ[^066-17] upasampajja viharāmi; vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ[^066-18] pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ[^066-19] jhānaṃ upasampajja viharāmi; pītiyā ca virāgā upekhakā[^066-20] ca[^066-4] viharāmi, satā[^066-21] ca sampajānā[^066-22] sukhañ ca


<!-- PTS Page 067 -->

##### *[PTS Page 067]*

kāyena paṭisaṃvedemi, yan taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upekhako satimā sukhavihārī'; ti tatiyaṃ[^067-1] jhānaṃ[^067-1] upasampajja viharāmi; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa[^067-2] ca[^067-2] pahānā[^067-2] pubb' eva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upekhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ[^067-3] jhānaṃ[^067-3] upasampajja viharāmī'; ti.

8. ‘Acchariyaṃ Nandamāte abbhutaṃ Nandamāte'; ti. ‘Na kho me bhante es'; eva acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, atthi me añño pi acchariyo abbhuto dhammo: yānīmāni bhante Bhagavatā[^067-4] desitāni {pañcorambhāgiyāni}[^067-5] saṃyojanāni, nāhaṃ tesaṃ kiñci attani appahīnaṃ samanupassāmī'; ti. ‘Acchariyaṃ Nandamāte abbhutaṃ Nandamāte'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto Nandamātaraṃ upāsikaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassetvā samādapetvā samuttejetvā sampahaṃsetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmī ti. Mahāyaññavaggo[^067-6] pañcamo. Tass'; uddānaṃ: Ṭhiti[^067-7]-parikkhāraṃ[^067-8] dve aggi[^067-9] saññā[^067-10] aparā[^067-11] duve[^067-12] Methunā[^067-13] saṃyogo[^067-14] dānaṃ[^067-15] Nandamātena[^067-16] te[^067-17] dasā[^067-18] ti. LI. *)

1. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā


<!-- PTS Page 068 -->
<a id="page-068"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 068]*

ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca: ‘Ko nu kho bhante hetu ko paccayo, yena sutavato ariyasāvakassa vicikicchā n'; uppajjati avyākatavatthūsū'; ti?

2. ‘Diṭṭhinirodhā kho bhikkhu sutavato ariyasāvakassa vicikicchā n'; uppajjati avyākatavatthūsu. <<Hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti kho bhikkhu diṭṭhigatam etaṃ; <<na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti kho bhikkhu diṭṭhigatam etaṃ; <<hoti ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti kho bhikkhu diṭṭhigatam etaṃ; <<neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti kho bhikkhu diṭṭhigatam etaṃ. Assutavā bhikkhu puthujjano diṭṭhiṃ na ppajānāti, diṭṭhisamudayaṃ na ppajānāti, diṭṭhinirodhaṃ[^068-1] na[^068-1] ppajānāti[^068-1], diṭṭhinirodhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ[^068-2] na ppajānāti. Tassa sā diṭṭhi pavaḍḍhati. So na parimuccati jātiyā jarāya[^068-3] maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi, na parimuccati dukkhasmā ti vadāmi. Sutavā ca kho bhikkhu ariyasāvako diṭṭhiṃ pajānāti, diṭṭhisamudayaṃ pajānāti, diṭṭhinirodhaṃ pajānāti, diṭṭhinirodhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti. Tassa[^068-4] sā diṭṭhi nirujjhati. So parimuccati jātiyā jarāya[^068-5] maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi, parimuccati dukkhasmā ti vadāmi. Evaṃ jānaṃ[^068-6] kho bhikkhu sutavā ariyasāvako evaṃ passaṃ <<hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti pi na[^068-7] vyākaroti; <<na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti pi na vyākaroti; <<hoti ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti pi na vyākaroti; <<neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti pi na vyākaroti. Evaṃ jānaṃ[^068-6] kho bhikkhu sutavā ariyasāvako evaṃ passaṃ evaṃ avyākaraṇadhammo[^068-8] hoti avyākatavatthūsu. Evaṃ jānaṃ kho bhikkhu sutavā ariyasāvako evaṃ[^068-9] passaṃ[^068-9] na cchambhati na kampati[^068-10] na vedhati na santāsaṃ āpajjati avyākatavatthūsu. <<Hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti kho bhikkhu taṇhāgatam etaṃ[^068-11] saññā-


<!-- PTS Page 069 -->
<a id="page-069"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 069]*

gatam[^069-1] etaṃ[^069-2] maññitam[^069-3] etaṃ[^069-4] papañcitam etaṃ[^069-5] upādānagatam etaṃ[^069-2] vippaṭisāro eso; <<na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti kho bhikkhu[^069-6] vippaṭisāro eso; <<hoti ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti kho bhikkhu vippaṭisāro eso; <<neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti kho bhikkhu vippaṭisāro eso. Assutavā bhikkhu puthujjano vippaṭisāraṃ na ppajānāti, vippaṭisārasamudayaṃ na ppajānāti, vippaṭisāranirodhaṃ na ppajānāti, vippaṭisāranirodhagāminiṃ[^069-7] paṭipadaṃ na ppajānāti. Tassa so vippaṭisāro pavaḍḍhati. So na parimuccati jātiyā jarāya[^069-8] maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi, na parimuccati dukkhasmā ti vadāmi. Sutavā ca[^069-9] kho bhikkhu ariyasāvako vippaṭisāraṃ pajānāti, vippaṭisārasamudayaṃ pajānāti, vippaṭisāranirodhaṃ pajānāti, vippaṭisāranirodhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti. Tassa so vippaṭisāro nirujjhati[^069-10]. So parimuccati jātiyā[^069-11] jarāya[^069-8] maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi, parimuccati dukkhasmā ti vadāmi. Evaṃ jānaṃ kho bhikkhu sutavā ariyasāvako evaṃ passaṃ <<hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti pi na vyākaroti[^069-12]; <<na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti pi na vyākaroti; <<hoti ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti pi na vyākaroti; <<neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā>> ti pi na vyākaroti. Evaṃ jānaṃ kho bhikkhu sutavā ariyasāvako evaṃ passaṃ evaṃ avyākaraṇadhammo hoti avyākatavatthūsu. Evaṃ jānaṃ kho bhikkhu sutavā ariyasāvako evaṃ passaṃ na cchambhati na kampati[^069-13] na vedhati na santāsaṃ āpajjati avyākatavatthūsu.


<!-- PTS Page 070 -->
<a id="page-070"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 070]*

Ayaṃ kho bhikkhu hetu ayaṃ paccayo, yena sutavato ariyasāvakassa vicikicchā n'; uppajjati avyākatavatthūsū'; ti. LII.

1. Satta[^070-1] bhikkhave purisagatiyo desissāmi[^070-2] anupādā[^070-3] ca parinibbānaṃ, taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca. Katamā ca bhikkhave satta purisagatiyo?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ paṭipanno hoti ‘no c'; assa[^070-4], no ca me siyā, na bhavissati, na me bhavissati, yad atthi yaṃ bhūtaṃ, taṃ pajahāmī'; ti; upekhaṃ paṭilabhati. So bhave na rajjati, sambhave na rajjati[^070-5], atthuttariṃ[^070-6] padaṃ santaṃ sammappaññāya passati; tañ ca khvassa[^070-7] padaṃ na sabbena sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ hoti, tassa na[^070-8] sabbena sabbaṃ mānānusayo pahīno hoti, na sabbena sabbaṃ bhavarāgānusayo pahīno hoti, na[^070-9] sabbena sabbaṃ avijjānusayo pahīno hoti. So pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hoti. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave divasasantatte[^070-10] ayokapāle[^070-11] {haññamāne} papaṭikā nibbattitvā[^070-12] nibbāyeyya, evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ paṭipanno hoti ‘no c'; assa[^070-4], no ca me siyā, na bhavissati, na[^070-9] me[^070-9] bhavissati[^070-9], yad atthi yaṃ bhūtaṃ, taṃ pajahāmī'; ti; upekhaṃ paṭilabhati. So bhave na rajjati, sambhave[^070-13] na rajjati[^070-5], atthuttariṃ[^070-6] padaṃ santaṃ sammappaññāya passati; tañ ca khvassa[^070-14] padaṃ na

sabbena sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ hoti, tassa na sabbena sabbaṃ mānānusayo pahīno hoti, na sabbena sabbaṃ bhavarāgānusayo pahīno hoti, na sabbena sabbaṃ avijjānusayo pahīno hoti. So pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hoti.

Seyyathā pi bhikkhave divasasantatte[^073-4] ayokapāle[^073-5] haññamāne papaṭikā[^073-6] nibbattitvā uppatitvā vipule tiṇapuñje vā kaṭṭhapuñje vā nipateyya, sā tattha aggim pi janeyya dhūmam pi janeyya, aggim pi[^073-7] janetvā dhūmam pi janetvā tam eva vipulaṃ tiṇapuñjaṃ vā kaṭṭhapuñjam[^073-8] vā[^073-8] pariyādiyitvā anāhārā nibbāyeyya[^073-9], evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ paṭipanno hoti[^073-1] ‘no c'; assa[^073-2], no ca me siyā'; . . . pe[^073-3] . . . 8. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ paṭipanno hoti[^073-1] ‘no c'; assa[^073-10], no ca me siyā, na bhavissati, na me bhavissati, yad atthi yaṃ bhūtaṃ, tam pajahāmī'; ti upekhaṃ paṭilabhati. So bhave na rajjati, sambhave[^073-11] na[^073-11] rajjati[^073-11], atthuttariṃ[^073-12] padaṃ santaṃ sammappaññāya[^073-13] passati; tañ ca khvassa padaṃ na sabbena sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ hoti, tassa na sabbena sabbaṃ mānānusayo pahīno hoti, na sabbena sabbaṃ bhavarāgānusayo pahīno hoti, na sabbena sabbaṃ avijjānusayo pahīno hoti. So pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā uddhaṃsoto hoti akaniṭṭhagāmī. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave divasasantatte ayokapāle[^073-14] haññamāne, papaṭikā nibbattitvā uppatitvā mahante[^073-15] tiṇapuñje[^073-16] vā kaṭṭhapuñje[^073-16] vā nipateyya[^073-17], sā tattha aggim pi janeyya dhūmam[^073-18] pi janeyya, aggim pi janetvā dhūmam pi janetvā tam[^073-19] eva[^073-19] mahantaṃ[^073-19]

##### *[PTS Page 074]*

tiṇapuñjaṃ vā kaṭṭhapuñjaṃ vā pariyādiyitvā gaccham[^074-1] pi daheyya dāyam[^074-2] pi[^074-2] daheyya[^074-2], gaccham[^074-1] pi dahitvā[^074-3] dāyam pi dahitvā haritan taṃ vā patthan[^074-4] taṃ vā selan taṃ vā udakan taṃ vā ramaṇīyaṃ vā bhūmibhāgaṃ āgamma anāhārā[^074-5] nibbāyeyya, evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ paṭipanno hoti[^074-6] ‘no c'; assa[^074-7] no ca me siyā' . . . pe[^074-8] . . . So pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā uddhaṃsoto hoti akaniṭṭhagāmī. Imā kho[^074-9] bhikkhave satta purisagatiyo. Katamañ[^074-10] ca bhikkhave anupādā parinibbānaṃ?

9. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ paṭipanno hoti ‘no c'; assa[^074-7], no ca me siyā, na bhavissati, na me bhavissati, yad atthi yaṃ bhūtaṃ, taṃ pajahāmī'; ti, upekhaṃ paṭilabhati. So bhave na rajjati sambhave na rajjati[^074-11], atthuttariṃ[^074-12] padaṃ santaṃ sammappaññāya[^074-13] passati; tañ ca khvassa padaṃ[^074-14] sabbena sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ hoti, tassa sabbena sabbaṃ mānānusayo pahīno hoti, sabbena[^074-15] sabbaṃ bhavarāgānusayo pahīno hoti, sabbena sabbaṃ avijjānusayo pahīno hoti. So āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^074-8] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave[^074-16] anupādā parinibbānaṃ. Imā kho bhikkhave satta purisagatiyo anupādā ca parinibbānan ti. LIII.

1. Evaṃ[^074-9] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate. Atha kho dve devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Gijjha-


<!-- PTS Page 075 -->
<a id="page-075"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 075]*

kūṭaṃ[^075-1] obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho ekā devatā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘etā bhante bhikkhuniyo vimuttā'; ti. Aparā devatā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘etā bhante bhikkhuniyo[^075-2] anupādisesā suvimuttā'; ti. Idam avocuṃ[^075-3] tā[^075-4] devatā. Samanuñño Satthā ahosi. Atha kho tā devatā ‘samanuñño Satthā'; ti Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyiṃsu. Atha kho Bhagavā tassā rattiyā accayena bhikkhū āmantesi ‘imaṃ bhikkhave rattiṃ dve devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Gijjhakūṭaṃ obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho bhikkhave ekā devatā maṃ etad avoca <<etā bhante bhikkhuniyo vimuttā>> ti. Aparā devatā maṃ etad avoca <<etā bhante bhikkhuniyo[^075-2] anupādisesā suvimuttā>> ti. Idam avocuṃ[^075-3] bhikkhave tā devatā, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyiṃsū'; ti.

2. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno[^075-5] Bhagavato avidūre nisinno hoti. Atha kho āyasmato Mahāmoggallānassa etad ahosi ‘katamesānaṃ kho devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sa-upādisese[^075-6] vā sa-upādiseso[^075-7] ti anupādisese[^075-7] vā anupādiseso'; ti. Tena kho pana samayena Tisso nāma bhikkhu adhunā kālakato aññataraṃ Brahmalokaṃ upapanno[^075-8] hoti. Tatrāpi[^075-9] naṃ evaṃ jānanti ‘Tisso Brahmā mahiddhiko mahāṇubhāvo'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno, seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ[^075-10] vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya[^075-11], evam eva Gijjhakūṭe pabbate antarahito tasmiṃ Brahmaloke pāturahosi. Addasā kho Tisso Brahmā āyasmantaṃ Mahāmoggallānaṃ dūrato 'va āgacchantaṃ, disvā


<!-- PTS Page 076 -->
<a id="page-076"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 076]*

āyasmantaṃ Mahāmoggallānaṃ etad avoca ‘ehi kho mārisa Moggallāna, svāgataṃ[^076-1] mārisa Moggallāna, cirassaṃ[^076-2] kho mārisa Moggallāna imaṃ pariyāyamam akāsi, yad[^076-3] idaṃ idhāgamanāya, nisīda mārisa Moggallāna, idam āsanaṃ paññattan'; ti. Nisīdi kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno paññatte āsane. Tisso pi kho Brahmā āyasmantaṃ Mahāmoggallānaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Tissaṃ Brahmānaṃ āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno etad avoca ‘katamesānaṃ kho Tissa devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sa-upādisese[^076-4] vā sa-upādiseso[^076-5] ti anupādisese[^076-6] vā anupādiseso'; ti. ‘Brahmakāyikānaṃ kho mārisa Moggallāna devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sa-upādisese[^076-6] vā sa-upādiseso ti anupādisese[^076-6] vā anupādiseso'; ti. ‘Sabbesaṃ yeva[^076-7] kho Tissa Brahmakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: saupādisese[^076-6] vā sa-upādiseso ti anupādisese[^076-6] vā anupādiseso'; ti?

3. Na kho mārisa Moggallāna sabbesaṃ Brahmakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sa-upādisese vā sa-upādiseso ti anupādisese[^076-6] vā anupādiseso ti. Ye kho[^076-8] te mārisa Moggallāna Brahmakāyikā devā brahmena[^076-9] āyunā santuṭṭhā, brahmena[^076-10] vaṇṇena brahmena[^076-11] sukhena brahmena[^076-11] yasena brahmena ādhipateyyena[^076-12] santuṭṭhā tassa[^076-13] ca uttariṃ[^076-14] nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānanti, tesaṃ[^076-15] na evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sa-upādisese[^076-6] vā sa-upādiseso ti anupādisese[^076-6] vā anupādiseso ti; ye ca[^076-16] kho te mārisa Moggallāna Brahmakāyikā devā brahmena āyunā asantuṭṭhā, brahmena vaṇṇena brahmena sukhena brahmena yasena brahmena ādhipateyyena asantuṭṭhā tassa[^076-17] ca[^076-18] uttariṃ[^076-14] nissaraṇaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 077 -->
<a id="page-077"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 077]*

yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti[^077-1], tesaṃ[^077-2] evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: saupādisese[^077-3] vā sa-upādiseso ti anupādisese[^077-3] vā anupādiseso ti.

4. Idha mārisa Moggallāna bhikkhu ubhato bhāgavimutto hoti, tam enaṃ te devā evaṃ jānanti: ayaṃ kho āyasmā ubhato bhāgavimutto, yāv'; assa kāyo ṭhassati, tāva naṃ dakkhinti[^077-4] devamanussā, kāyassa bhedā na[^077-5] naṃ[^077-5] dakkhinti devamanussā ti. Evam pi[^077-5] kho mārisa Moggallāna tesaṃ devānaṃ[^077-6] ñāṇaṃ hoti: anupādisese[^077-3] vā anupādiseso ti[^077-7].

5. Idha pana mārisa Moggallāna bhikkhu paññāvimutto hoti, tam enaṃ te devā evaṃ jānanti: ayaṃ kho āyasmā paññāvimutto, yāv'; assa kāyo ṭhassati, tāva naṃ dakkhinti devamanussā, kāyassa bhedā[^077-8] na[^077-9] naṃ dakkhinti devamanussā ti. Evam pi kho mārisa Moggallāna tesaṃ devānaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: anupādisese[^077-3] vā anupādiseso ti[^077-7].

6. Idha pana[^077-10] mārisa Moggallāna bhikkhu kāyasakkhi hoti, tam enaṃ te devā evaṃ jānanti[^077-11]: ayaṃ kho āyasmā kāyasakkhi, app eva nāma ayam āyasmā anulomikāni senāsanāni paṭisevamāno kalyāṇamitte bhajamāno indriyāni samannānayamāno, yass'; atthāya kulaputtā samma-d-eva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tad anuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā ti. Evam pi kho mārisa Moggallāna tesaṃ devānaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sa-upādisese vā sa-upādiseso ti[^077-12].

7. Idha pana[^077-10] mārisa Moggallāna bhikkhu diṭṭhippatto hoti . . . pe[^077-13] . . . saddhāvimutto hoti . . .[^077-14] dhammānusārī hoti, tam enaṃ te devā evaṃ jānanti: ayaṃ kho āyasmā


<!-- PTS Page 078 -->
<a id="page-078"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 078]*

dhammānusārī, app eva nāma ayam āyasmā anulomikāni senāsanāni paṭisevamāno kalyāṇamitte bhajamāno indriyāni samannānayamāno, yass'; atthāya kulaputtā samma-d-eva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tad anuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā ti. Evam pi kho mārisa Moggallāna tesaṃ devānaṃ[^078-1] ñāṇaṃ hoti: sa-upādisese vā sa-upādiseso ti.

8. Atha kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno Tissassa Brahmuno bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā, seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya, evam eva Brahmaloke antarahito Gijjhakūṭe pabbate pāturahosi. Atha kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno, yāvatako[^078-2] ahosi Tissena Brahmunā saddhiṃ kathāsallāpo, taṃ sabbaṃ Bhagavato ārocesi. ‘Na hi pana te Moggallāna Tisso Brahmā sattamaṃ animittavihāriṃ[^078-3] puggalaṃ desesī'; ti. ‘Etassa Bhagavā kālo, etassa Sugata kālo, yaṃ Bhagavā sattamaṃ animittavihāriṃ puggalaṃ deseyya, Bhagavato sutvā bhikkhū dhāressantī[^078-4]'; ti. ‘Tena hi Moggallāna suṇāhi[^078-5] sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante' ti kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno Bhagavato paccassosi. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

9. Idha Moggallāna bhikkhu sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ cetosamādhiṃ upasampajja viharati, tam enaṃ te devā evaṃ jānanti: ayaṃ kho āyasmā sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ cetosamādhiṃ upasampajja viharati, app eva nāma ayam āyasmā anulomikāni senāsanāni paṭisevamāno kalyāṇamitte[^078-6] bhajamāno[^078-6] indriyāni samannānayamāno, yass'; atthāya kulaputtā sammad-eva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tad anuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā


<!-- PTS Page 079 -->
<a id="page-079"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 079]*

sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā ti. Evaṃ kho Moggallāna tesaṃ devānaṃ[^079-1] ñāṇaṃ hoti: sa-upādisese vā[^079-2] sa-upādiseso ti. LIV.

1. Evaṃ[^079-3] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Atha kho Sīho senāpati yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Sīho senāpati Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sakkā nu kho bhante sandiṭṭhikaṃ dānaphalaṃ paññāpetun'; ti?

2. Tena hi Sīha taṃ yev'; ettha[^079-4] paṭipucchissāmi, yathā te khameyya, tathā naṃ vyākareyyāsi[^079-5]. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīha? Idh'; assa[^079-6] dve purisā, eko puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, eko puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato[^079-7]. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīha? Kaṃ[^079-8] nu kho arahanto paṭhamaṃ anukampantā anukampeyyuṃ[^079-9]: yo[^079-10] vā[^079-11] so puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, yo vā so puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato ti? ‘Yo so bhante puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, kin taṃ[^079-12] arahanto paṭhamaṃ anukampantā[^079-13] anukampissanti? Yo ca[^079-14] kho so bhante puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato, taṃ yeva arahanto paṭhamaṃ anukampantā anukampeyyuṃ'; .

3. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīha? Kaṃ[^079-15] nu kho arahanto paṭhamaṃ upasaṅkamantā[^079-13] upasaṅkameyyuṃ: yo vā so puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, yo vā so


<!-- PTS Page 080 -->
<a id="page-080"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 080]*

puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato ti? ‘Yo so bhante puriso assadho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, kin taṃ[^080-1] arahanto paṭhamaṃ upasaṅkamantā[^080-2] upasaṅkamissanti? Yo ca kho[^080-3] so bhante puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato, taṃ yeva arahanto paṭhamaṃ upasaṅkamantā[^080-2] upasaṅkameyyuṃ'; .

4. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīha? Kassa nu kho arahanto paṭhamaṃ paṭigaṇhantā[^080-2] paṭigaṇheyyuṃ: yo vā so puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, yo vā so puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato ti? ‘Yo so bhante puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, kin[^080-1] tassa arahanto paṭhamaṃ paṭigaṇhantā[^080-2] paṭigaṇhissanti? Yo ca kho so bhante puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato, tass'; eva[^080-4] arahanto paṭhamaṃ paṭigaṇhantā paṭigaṇheyyuṃ'; .

5. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīha? Kassa nu kho arahanto paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ desentā[^080-5] deseyyuṃ: yo vā so puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, yo vā so puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato ti? ‘Yo so bhante puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, kin[^080-6] tassa arahanto paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ desentā[^080-7] desissanti[^080-8]? Yo ca kho so bhante puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato, tass'; eva arahanto paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ desentā[^080-7] deseyyuṃ'; .

6. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīha? Kassa nu kho kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggaccheyya: yo vā so puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, yo vā so puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato ti? ‘Yo so bhante puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, kin tassa kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggacchissati[^080-9]? Yo ca kho so bhante puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato, tass'; eva kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggaccheyya'; .

7. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīha? Ko nu kho yañ ñad eva parisaṃ upasaṅkameyya, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ,


<!-- PTS Page 081 -->
<a id="page-081"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 081]*

visārado upasaṅkameyya amaṅkubhūto: yo vā so puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, yo vā so puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato ti? ‘Yo so bhante puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, kiṃ so yañ ñad eva parisaṃ upasaṅkamissati, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ, visārado[^081-1] upasaṅkamissati amaṅkubhūto? Yo ca kho so bhante puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato, so yañ ñad eva parisaṃ upasaṅkameyya, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ, visārado upasaṅkameyya amaṅkubhūto'; .

8. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīha? Ko nu kho kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjeyya[^081-2]: yo vā[^081-3] so puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, yo vā so puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato ti? ‘Yo so bhante puriso assaddho maccharī kadariyo paribhāsako, kiṃ so kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjissati[^081-2]? Yo ca kho so bhante puriso saddho dānapati anuppadānarato, so kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjeyya[^081-4]'; .

9. ‘Yānīmāni bhante Bhagavatā cha[^081-5] sandiṭṭhikāni dānaphalāni[^081-6] akkhātāni, nāhaṃ ettha Bhagavato saddhāya[^081-7] gacchāmi, ahaṃ p'; etāni[^081-8] jānāmi. Ahaṃ bhante dāyako dānapati, maṃ arahanto paṭhamaṃ anukampantā anukampanti. Ahaṃ bhante dāyako dānapati, maṃ arahanto paṭhamaṃ upasaṅkamantā upasaṅkamanti. Ahaṃ bhante dāyako dānapati, mayhaṃ arahanto paṭhamaṃ paṭigaṇhantā[^081-9] paṭigaṇhanti. Ahaṃ bhante dāyako dānapati, mayhaṃ arahanto paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ[^081-10] desentā[^081-11] desenti. Ahaṃ bhante dāyako dānapati, mayhaṃ kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggato: Sīho senāpati dāyako kārako saṅghupaṭṭhāko[^081-12]


<!-- PTS Page 082 -->
<a id="page-082"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 082]*

ti. Ahaṃ bhante dāyako dānapati yañ ñad eva parisaṃ upasaṅkamāmi, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ[^082-1] yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ, visārado upasaṅkamāmi amaṅkubhūto. Yānīmāni bhante Bhagavatā cha[^082-2] sandiṭṭhikāni dānaphalāni akkhātāni, nāhaṃ ettha Bhagavato saddhāya[^082-3] gacchāmi, ahaṃ p'; etāni[^082-4] jānāmi. Yañ ca kho maṃ bhante[^082-5] Bhagavā evam āha: dāyako Sīho[^082-6] dānapati kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjatī[^082-7] ti, etāhaṃ na[^082-8] jānāmi, ettha ca panāhaṃ[^082-9] Bhagavato saddhāya[^082-10] gacchāmī'; ti. Evam[^082-11] etaṃ Sīha, evam etaṃ Sīha, dāyako Sīha[^082-12] dānapati kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjatī[^082-7] ti. LV.

1. Cattārīmāni bhikkhave Tathāgatassa arakkheyyāni[^082-13], tīhi[^082-13] ca[^082-13] anupavajjo[^082-14]. Katamāni cattāri Tathāgatassa arakkheyyāni?

2. Parisuddhakāyasamācāro bhikkhave Tathāgato. Natthi Tathāgatassa kāyaduccaritaṃ, yaṃ Tathāgato rakkheyya ‘mā me idaṃ paro aññāsī'; ti. -- Parisuddhavacīsamācāro bhikkhave Tathāgato. Natthi Tathāgatassa vacīduccaritaṃ, yaṃ Tathāgato rakkheyya ‘mā me idaṃ paro aññāsī'; ti. -- Parisuddhamanosamācāro[^082-15] bhikkhave Tathāgato. Natthi Tathāgatassa manoduccaritaṃ, yaṃ Tathāgato rakkheyya ‘mā me idaṃ paro aññāsī'; ti. -- Parisuddhājīvo[^082-16] bhikkhave Tathāgato. Natthi Tathāgatassa micchā-ājīvo[^082-17], yaṃ Tathāgato rakkheyya ‘mā me idaṃ paro aññāsī'; ti. Imāni cattāri Tathāgatassa arakkheyyāni. Katamehi tīhi[^082-18] anupavajjo?


<!-- PTS Page 083 -->
<a id="page-083"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 083]*

3. Svākkhātadhammo[^083-1] bhikkhave Tathāgato. Tatra vata maṃ samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā devo vā Māro vā Brahmā vā koci[^083-2] vā lokasmiṃ sahadhammena paṭicodessati ‘iti pi[^083-3] te[^083-4] na svākkhātadhammo[^083-5]'; ti. Nimittam etaṃ bhikkhave na[^083-6] samanupassāmi[^083-7], etaṃ p'; ahaṃ[^083-8] bhikkhave nimittaṃ asamanupassanto khemappatto abhayappatto vesārajjappatto[^083-9] viharāmi. Supaññattā kho pana me[^083-10] bhikkhave sāvakānaṃ nibbānagāminī paṭipadā, yathā[^083-11] paṭipannā mama sāvakā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharanti[^083-12]. -- Tatra vata maṃ samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā devo vā Māro vā Brahmā vā koci vā lokasmiṃ sahadhammena paṭicodessati[^083-13] ‘iti pi te[^083-14] na supaññattā sāvakānaṃ nibbānagāminī paṭipadā, yathā[^083-15] paṭipannā tava[^083-16] sāvakā[^083-16] āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^083-17] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharantī'; ti. Nimittam etaṃ bhikkhave na[^083-18] samanupassāmi. Etaṃ p'; ahaṃ[^083-8] bhikkhave nimittaṃ asamanupassanto[^083-19] khemappatto abhayappatto vesārajjappatto viharāmi. Anekasatā kho pana me bhikkhave sāvakaparisā āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^083-17] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharanti[^083-20]. -- Tatra vata maṃ samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā devo vā Māro vā Brahmā vā koci vā lokasmiṃ sahadhammena paṭicodessati ‘iti pi te na anekasatā[^083-21] sāvakaparisā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ ditth'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharantī[^083-20]'; ti. Nimittam etaṃ bhikkhave na[^083-22] samanupassāmi, etaṃ p'; ahaṃ[^083-8] bhikkhave


<!-- PTS Page 084 -->
<a id="page-084"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 084]*

nimittaṃ asamanupassanto khemappatto abhayappatto vesārajjappatto viharāmi. Imehi tīhi anupavajjo. Imāni kho bhikkhave cattāri Tathāgatassa arakkheyyāni, imehi ca tīhi anupavajjo ti. LVI.

1. Evaṃ[^084-1] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kimbilāyaṃ[^084-2] viharati Veḷuvane. Atha kho āyasmā Kimbilo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Kimbilo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ko nu kho bhante hetu ko paccayo, yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hotī'; ti? ‘Idha Kimbila Tathāgate parinibbute bhikkhū[^084-3] bhikkhuniyo upāsakā upasikāyo Satthari agāravā viharanti appatissā[^084-4], dhamme agāravā viharanti appatissā, saṅghe agāravā viharanti appatissā, sikkhāya agāravā viharanti appatissā, samādhismiṃ agāravā viharanti appatissā, appamāde agāravā viharanti appatissā, paṭisanthāre[^084-5] agāravā viharanti appatissā. Ayaṃ kho Kimbila hetu ayaṃ paccayo, yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hotī'; ti.

2. ‘Ko pana bhante hetu ko paccayo, yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī'; ti? ‘Idha Kimbila Tathāgate parinibbute bhikkhū bhikkhuniyo upāsakā upāsikāyo Satthari sagāravā viharanti sappatissā[^084-6], dhamme sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, saṅghe sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, sikkhāya sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, samādhismiṃ sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, appamāde sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, paṭisanthāre sagāravā viharanti sappatissā. Ayaṃ kho Kimbila hetu ayaṃ paccayo, yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī'; ti.


<!-- PTS Page 085 -->
<a id="page-085"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 085]*

LVII.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu na cirass'; eva āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^085-1] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyya. Katamehi sattahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu saddho hoti, sīlavā hoti, bahussuto hoti, paṭisallīno hoti, āraddhaviriyo hoti, satimā hoti, paññavā hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu na cirass'; eva āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^085-2] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā ti. LVIII.

1. Evaṃ[^085-3] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Bhaggesu viharati Suṃsumāragire[^085-4] Bhesakaḷāvane Migadāye. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno Magadhesu Kallavāḷamuttagāme[^085-5] pacalāyamāno[^085-6] nisinno hoti. Addasā kho Bhagavā dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena āyasmantaṃ Mahāmoggallānaṃ Magadhesu Kallavāḷamuttagāme[^085-7] pacalāyamānaṃ[^085-6] nisinnaṃ, disvā, seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya, evam eva Bhaggesu Suṃsumāragire Bhesakaḷāvane Migadāye antarahito Magadhesu Kallavāḷamuttagāme[^085-7] āyasmato Mahāmoggallānassa pamukhe[^085-8] pāturahosi. Nisīdi Bhagavā paññatte āsane. Nisajja kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Mahāmoggallānaṃ etad avoca ‘pacalāyasi[^085-9] no tvaṃ Moggallāna, pacalāyasi[^085-10] no tvaṃ Moggallānā'; ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante'; .

2. Tasmā ti ha tvaṃ[^085-11] Moggallāna, yathā saññissa[^085-12] te viharato taṃ middhaṃ okkamati, taṃ saññaṃ manasākāsi[^085-13]


<!-- PTS Page 086 -->
<a id="page-086"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 086]*

taṃ saññaṃ bahulam akāsi[^086-1]: ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ[^086-2] vijjati, yan te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha.

3. No ce te evaṃ viharato[^086-3] taṃ[^086-4] middhaṃ pahīyetha, tato tvaṃ Moggallāna yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ[^086-5] cetasā anuvitakkeyyāsi anuvicāreyyāsi manasānupekkheyyāsi: ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yan te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha.

4. No ce te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha, tato tvaṃ Moggallāna yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthārena sajjhāyaṃ kareyyāsi: ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yan te evaṃ viharato taṃ[^086-6] middhaṃ[^086-6] pahīyetha[^086-6].

5. No ce te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha, tato tvaṃ Moggallāna ubho kaṇṇasotāni āvijeyyāsi[^086-7] pāṇinā[^086-8] gattāni anumajjeyyāsi: ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yan te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha.

6. No ce te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha, tato tvaṃ Moggallāna uṭṭhāyāsanā udakena akkhīni anumajjitvā[^086-9] disā anuvilokeyyāsi nakkhattāni tārakarūpāni ullokeyyāsi: ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yan te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha.

7. No ce te evaṃ viharato tam middhaṃ pahīyetha, tato tvaṃ Moggallāna[^086-10] ālokasaññaṃ manasikareyyāsi {divāsaññaṃ} adhiṭṭheyyāsi[^086-11]; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā. Iti vivaṭena[^086-12] cetasā apariyonaddhena sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāveyyāsi: ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yan te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha.


<!-- PTS Page 087 -->
<a id="page-087"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 087]*

8. No ce te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha, tato tvaṃ Moggallāna pacchāpuresaññī[^087-1] caṅkamaṃ[^087-2] adhiṭṭheyyāsi[^087-3] antogatehi[^087-4] indriyehi abahigatena[^087-5] mānasena: ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yan te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha.

9. No ce te evaṃ viharato taṃ middhaṃ pahīyetha, tato tvaṃ Moggallāna dakkhiṇena passena sīhaseyyaṃ kappeyyāsi pādena[^087-6] pādaṃ accādhāya sato sampajāno uṭṭhānasaññaṃ manasikaritvā[^087-7], paṭibuddhena[^087-8] ca[^087-8] te Moggallāna khippaṃ yeva paccuṭṭhātabbaṃ[^087-9] ‘na seyyasukhaṃ na[^087-10] passasukhaṃ[^087-11] na[^087-12] middhasukhaṃ anuyutto viharissāmī'; ti. Evaṃ hi te Moggallāna sikkhitabbaṃ.

10. Tasmā ti ha Moggallāna evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ ‘na[^087-13] uccāsoṇḍaṃ paggahetvā kulāni upasaṅkamissāmī'; ti. Evaṃ hi te Moggallāna sikkhitabbaṃ. Sace Moggallāna bhikkhu uccāsoṇḍaṃ paggahetvā kulāni upasaṅkamati, santi hi Moggallāna kulesu kiccakaraṇīyāni, yena[^087-14] manussā āgataṃ bhikkhuṃ na {manasikaronti,} {tatra}[^087-15] bhikkhussa evaṃ hoti ‘ko su nāma dāni maṃ imasmiṃ kule paribhindi, virattarūpādānīme mayi manussā'; {ti[^087-16],} {iti 'ssa} alābhena maṅkubhāvo[^087-17], maṅkubhūtassa uddhaccaṃ, uddhatassa asaṃvaro, asaṃvutassa ārā cittaṃ samādhimhā. Tasmā ti ha[^087-18] Moggallāna evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ ‘na viggāhikakathaṃ kathessāmī'; ti. Evaṃ hi te Moggallāna sikkhitabbaṃ. Viggāhikāya Moggallāna kathāya sati kathābāhullaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^087-19], kathābāhulle sati uddhaccaṃ, uddhatassa {asaṃvaro,} asaṃvutassa ārā cittaṃ samādhimhā. Nāhaṃ Moggallāna sabbe h'; eva saṃsaggaṃ[^087-20] vaṇṇayāmi, na[^087-21]


<!-- PTS Page 088 -->
<a id="page-088"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 088]*

panāhaṃ Moggallāna sabbe h'; eva saṃsaggaṃ na[^088-1] vaṇṇayāmi, sagahaṭṭhapabbajitehi[^088-2] kho ahaṃ Moggallāna saṃsaggaṃ na vaṇṇayāmi[^088-3], yāni ca kho tāni senāsanāni appasaddāni appanigghosāni[^088-4] vijanavātāni manussarāhaseyyakāni[^088-5] paṭisallānasāruppāni, tathārūpehi senāsanehi saṃsaggaṃ vaṇṇayāmī'; ti.

11. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kittāvatā nu kho bhante bhikkhu saṃkhittena taṇhāsaṃkhayavimutto hoti accantaniṭṭho accantayogakkhemī accantabrahmacārī accantapariyosāno seṭṭho devamanussānan'; ti? ‘Idha Moggallāna bhikkhuno sutaṃ hoti: sabbe[^088-6] dhammā nālaṃ abhinivesāyā ti, evañ c'; etaṃ Moggallāna bhikkhuno sutaṃ hoti: sabbe dhammā nālaṃ abhinivesāyā ti. So[^088-7] sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānāti, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiññāya sabbaṃ dhammaṃ parijānāti[^088-8], sabbaṃ dhammaṃ pariññāya yaṃ kiñci vedanaṃ vediyati sukhaṃ vā[^088-9] dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā. So tāsu vedanāsu aniccānupassī viharati, virāgānupassī viharati, nirodhānupassī viharati, paṭinissaggānupassī viharati. So tāsu vedanāsu aniccānupassī viharanto virāgānupassī viharanto nirodhānupassī viharanto paṭinissaggānupassī viharanto[^088-7] na ca[^088-9] kiñci loke upādiyati, anupādiyaṃ na paritassati, aparitassaṃ paccattaṃ yeva parinibbāyati; <<khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā>> ti pajānāti. Ettāvatā kho Moggallāna bhikkhu saṃkhittena taṇhāsaṃkhayavimutto hoti accantaniṭṭho accantayogakkhemī accantabrahmacārī accantapariyosāno seṭṭho devamanussānan'; ti. [Mā[^088-10] bhikkhave puññānaṃ bhāyittha, sukhass'; etaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 089 -->
<a id="page-089"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 089]*

bhikkhave adhivacanaṃ, yad idaṃ puññānan[^089-1] ti. Abhijānāmi kho panāhaṃ bhikkhave dīgharattaṃ katānaṃ puññānaṃ dīgharattaṃ iṭṭhaṃ[^089-2] kantaṃ manāpaṃ vipākaṃ paccanubhūtaṃ. Satta vassāni mettacittaṃ[^089-3] bhāvesiṃ[^089-4], satta vassāni mettacittaṃ[^089-3] bhāvetvā satta saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappe na yimaṃ lokaṃ punāgamāsiṃ[^089-5], saṃvaṭṭamāne[^089-6] sudāhaṃ[^089-6] bhikkhave loke Ābhassarupago[^089-7] homi[^089-8], vivaṭṭamāne loke suññaṃ Brahmavimānaṃ upapajjāmi[^089-9]. Tatra sudaṃ bhikkhave Brahmā homi Mahābrahmā abhibhū[^089-10] anabhibhūto[^089-11] aññadatthudaso[^089-12] Vasavattī. Chattiṃsakkhattuṃ kho panāhaṃ bhikkhave Sakko ahosiṃ[^089-13] devānam indo. Anekasattakkhattuṃ[^089-14] rājā ahosiṃ[^089-15] Cakkavattī dhammiko dhammarājā cāturanto[^089-16] vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappatto sattaratanasamannāgato. Tassa mayhaṃ bhikkhave imāni satta ratanāni ahesuṃ, seyyathīdaṃ cakkaratanaṃ hatthiratanaṃ assaratanaṃ maṇiratanaṃ itthiratanaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ, parināyakaratanam eva sattamaṃ. Paro sahassaṃ kho pana me bhikkhave puttā ahesuṃ sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasenappamaddanā[^089-17]. So imaṃ paṭhaviṃ[^089-18] sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍena asatthena dhammena abhivijiya ajjhāvasan ti. Passa puññānaṃ vipākaṃ kusalānaṃ sukhesinaṃ[^089-19]: mettacittaṃ[^089-3] vibhāvetvā[^089-20] satta vassāni bhikkhave[^089-21]


<!-- PTS Page 090 -->
<a id="page-090"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 090]*

satta[^090-1] saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappe na yimaṃ lokaṃ punāgamaṃ[^090-2], saṃvaṭṭamāne[^090-3] lokamhi[^090-4] homi Ābhassarūpago[^090-5], vivaṭṭamāne[^090-6] lokamhi[^090-7] suññaṃ[^090-8] Brahmupago[^090-9] ahuṃ[^090-10], sattakkhattuṃ Mahābrahmā Vasavatti[^090-11] tadā ahuṃ[^090-12], chattiṃsakkhattuṃ devindo devarajjam akārayiṃ, cakkavattī ahuṃ[^090-13] rājā Jambusaṇḍassa[^090-14] issaro. Muddhābhisitto[^090-15] khattiyo manussādhipatī ahuṃ[^090-10] adaṇḍena asatthena vijeyya[^090-16] paṭhaviṃ imaṃ asāhasena[^090-17] dhammena[^090-18] samena {m-anusāsiyaṃ}[^090-19], dhammena rajjaṃ kāretvā asmiṃ*) paṭhavimaṇḍale mahaddhane mahābhoge aḍḍhe[^090-20] ajāyisaṃ[^090-21] kule sabbakāmehi sampanne[^090-22] ratanehi ca sattahi. Buddhā saṅgāhakā loke, tehi[^090-23] etaṃ[^090-23] sudesitaṃ, esa[^090-24] hetu mahantassa, pathabyo[^090-25] yena[^090-26] vuccati. Pahutavittupakaraṇo rājā homi[^090-27] patāpavā, iddhimā yasavā homi[^090-27] Jambusaṇḍassa[^090-28] issaro. Ko sutvā na ppasīdeyya api kaṇhābhijātiyo?


<!-- PTS Page 091 -->
<a id="page-091"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 091]*

Tasmā hi[^091-1] atthakāmena[^091-2] mahattam[^091-3] abhikaṅkhatā saddhammo garukātabbo saraṃ buddhānasāsanan ti[^091-4].] LIX.

1. Ekaṃ[^091-5] samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho Bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivesanaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Tena kho pana samayena Anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivesane manussā uccāsaddā mahāsaddā honti. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ Bhagavā etad avoca ‘kin nu kho[^091-6] te[^091-7] gahapati nivesane manussā uccāsaddā mahāsaddā kevaṭṭā[^091-8] maññe macchavilope[^091-9]'; ti? ‘Ayaṃ bhante Sujātā gharasuṇhā aḍḍhā aḍḍhakulā[^091-10] ānītā, sā neva[^091-11] sassuṃ[^091-12] ādiyati na sasuraṃ[^091-13] ādiyati na sāmikaṃ ādiyati, Bhagavantam pi[^091-14] na sakkaroti na garukaroti na māneti na pūjeti'; ti.

2. Atha kho Bhagavā Sujātaṃ gharasuṇhaṃ āmantesi ‘ehi Sujāte[^091-15]'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho[^091-16] Sujātā gharasuṇhā Bhagavato paṭissutvā[^091-17] yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Sujātaṃ gharasuṇhaṃ Bhagavā


<!-- PTS Page 092 -->
<a id="page-092"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 092]*

etad avoca ‘satta kho imā Sujāte purisassa bhariyā. Katamā satta? Vadhakasamā[^092-1], corīsamā[^092-2], ayyasamā, mātusamā[^092-3], bhaginīsamā, sakhīsamā, dāsīsamā[^092-4]. Imā kho Sujāte satta purisassa bhariyā[^092-5]. Tāsaṃ[^092-6] tvan nu[^092-7] katamā' ti? ‘Na[^092-8] kho ahaṃ bhante imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ[^092-9] vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāmi[^092-10]. Sādhu me bhante Bhagavā tathā dhammaṃ desetu, yathāhaṃ[^092-11] imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa vitthārena atthaṃ ājāneyyan[^092-12]'; ti. ‘Tena hi Sujāte suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho Sujātā gharasuṇhā Bhagavato paccassosi. Bhagavā etad avoca:-- Paduṭṭhacittā ahitānukampinī aññesu rattā atimaññate patiṃ dhanena kītassa vadhāya ussukā, yā evarūpā purisassa bhariyā ‘vadhakā[^092-13] ca bhariyā'; ti ca[^092-14] sā pavuccati[^092-15]. Yaṃ itthiyā vindati sāmiko dhanaṃ sippaṃ vaṇijjañ ca kasim adhiṭṭhahaṃ[^092-16] appam pi tasmā[^092-17] apahātum[^092-18] icchati, yā evarūpā purisassa bhariyā ‘corī[^092-19] ca[^092-20] bhariyā'; ti ca sā pavuccati. Akammakāmā alasā mahagghasā pharusā[^092-4] ca caṇḍi[^092-21] duruttavādinī[^092-22] uṭṭhāyakānaṃ[^092-23] abhibhuyya vattati, yā evarūpā purisassa bhariyā ‘ayyā ca bhariyā[^092-24]'; ti ca sā pavuccati.


<!-- PTS Page 093 -->
<a id="page-093"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 093]*

Yā sabbadā hoti hitānukampinī mātā[^093-1] va puttaṃ[^093-2] anurakkhate patiṃ tato dhanaṃ sambhatam assa rakkhati, yā evarūpā purisassa bhariyā ‘mātā ca bhariyā'; ti ca sā pavuccati. Yathā pi jeṭṭhā bhaginī kaniṭṭhakā[^093-3] sagāravā hoti sakamhi[^093-4] sāmike hirīmanā[^093-5] bhattuvasānuvattinī[^093-6], yā evarūpā purisassa bhariyā ‘bhaginī ca bhariyā'; ti ca sā pavuccati. Yā cīdha disvāna patiṃ pamodati sakhī sakhāraṃ va cirassam[^093-7] āgataṃ koleyyakā sīlavatī patibbatā, yā evarūpā purisassa bhariyā ‘sakhī ca bhariyā'; ti ca sā pavuccati. Akkuddhasantā[^093-8] vadhadaṇḍatajjitā aduṭṭhacittā[^093-9] patino titikkhati {akkodhanā} bhattuvasānuvattinī, yā evarūpā purisassa bhariyā ‘dāsī ca bhariyā'; ti ca sā pavuccati. Yā cīdha bhariyā ‘vadhakā[^093-10]'; ti vuccati ‘corī[^093-11] ca ayyā'; ti ca sā[^093-12] pavuccati dussīlarūpā pharusā anādarā kāyassa bhedā nirayaṃ vajanti tā. Yā cīdha ‘mātā bhaginī sakhī'; ti[^093-13] ca ‘dāsī ca[^093-14] bhariyā'; ti ca sā[^093-12] pavuccati sīle ṭhitattā cirarattasaṃvutā kāyassa bhedā sugatiṃ vajanti tā ti. Imā kho Sujāte satta purisassa bhariyā. Tāsam tvaṃ katamā ti?


<!-- PTS Page 094 -->
<a id="page-094"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 094]*

Ajja-t-agge maṃ bhante Bhagavā dāsīsamaṃ sāmikassa bhariyaṃ dhāretū ti. LX.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave dhammā sapattakantā sapattakaraṇā[^094-1] kodhanaṃ āgacchanti[^094-2] itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā. Katame satta?

2. Idha bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa evaṃ icchati ‘aho vatāyaṃ dubbaṇṇo assā'; ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Na bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa vaṇṇavatāya[^094-3] nandati. Kodhano 'yaṃ[^094-4] bhikkhave purisapuggalo kodhābhibhūto kodhapareto[^094-5], kiñ cāpi so[^094-6] hoti sunahāto[^094-7] suvilitto kappitakesamassu odātavatthavasano[^094-8], atha kho so dubbaṇṇo ca hoti kodhābhibhūto. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo dhammo sapattakanto sapattakaraṇo[^094-1] kodhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa evaṃ icchati ‘aho vatāyaṃ dukkhaṃ sayeyyā'; ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Na bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa sukhaseyyāya nandati. Kodhano 'yaṃ bhikkhave purisapuggalo kodhābhibhūto kodhapareto, kiñ cāpi so pallaṅke seti gonakatthate paṭikatthate[^094-9] paṭalikatthate[^094-10] kadalimigapavarapaccattharaṇe[^094-11] sa-uttaracchade[^094-12] ubhato-lohitakūpadhāne, atha kho so dukkhaṃ[^094-13] yeva seti[^094-14] kodhābhibhūto. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo dhammo sapattakanto sapattakaraṇo[^094-1] kodhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa evaṃ icchati ‘aho vatāyaṃ na pacurattho[^094-15] assā'; ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Na bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa pacuratthatāya


<!-- PTS Page 095 -->
<a id="page-095"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 095]*

nandati. Kodhano 'yaṃ[^095-1] bhikkhave purisapuggalo kodhābhibhūto kodhapareto anatthaṃ pi gahetvā ‘attho me gahito'; ti maññati, atthaṃ pi gahetvā ‘anattho me gahito[^095-2]' ti maññati. Tass'; ime dhammā aññam aññavipaccanīkā gahitā dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti kodhābhibhūtassa. Ayaṃ bhikkhave tatiyo dhammo sapattakanto sapattakaraṇo[^095-3] kodhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa evaṃ icchati ‘aho vatāyaṃ na bhogavā assā'; ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Na bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa bhogavatāya nandati. Kodhanassa bhikkhave purisapuggalassa kodhābhibhūtassa kodhaparetassa, ye pi 'ssa[^095-4] te honti bhogā uṭṭhānaviriyādhigatā bāhābalaparicitā sedāvakkhittā dhammikā dhammaladdhā, te pi rājāno[^095-5] rājakosaṃ pavesenti kodhābhibhūtassa. Ayaṃ bhikkhave catuttho dhammo sapattakanto sapattakaraṇo[^095-3] kodhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa evaṃ icchati ‘aho vatāyaṃ na yasavā assā'; ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Na bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa yasavatāya[^095-6] nandati. Kodhano 'yaṃ[^095-7] bhikkhave purisapuggalo kodhābhibhūto kodhapareto, yo pi 'ssa so[^095-8] hoti yaso appamādādhigato, tamhā pi dhaṃsati kodhābhibhūto. Ayam bhikkhave pañcamo dhammo sapattakanto sapattakaraṇo[^095-3] kodhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa evaṃ icchati ‘aho vatāyaṃ na mittavā assā'; ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Na bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa mittavatāya nandati. Kodhanaṃ[^095-9] bhikkhave purisapuggalaṃ[^095-10] kodhābhibhūtaṃ[^095-11] kodhaparetaṃ[^095-11], ye pi 'ssa te honti mittāmaccā ñātisālohitā, te pi ārakā[^095-12] parivajjenti kodhābhibhūtaṃ. Ayaṃ bhikkhave chaṭṭho[^095-13] dhammo sapattakanto sapattakaraṇo[^095-3] kodhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā.


<!-- PTS Page 096 -->
<a id="page-096"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 096]*

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa evaṃ icchati ‘aho vatāyaṃ[^096-1] kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjeyyā[^096-2]'; ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Na bhikkhave sapatto sapattassa sugatigamanena nandati. Kodhano 'yaṃ[^096-3] bhikkhave purisapuggalo kodhābhibhūto kodhapareto kāyena duccaritaṃ carati vācāya[^096-4] duccaritaṃ carati manasā duccaritaṃ carati. So kāyena duccaritaṃ caritvā . . . pe[^096-5] . . . kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^096-6] kodhābhibhūto. Ayaṃ bhikkhave sattamo dhammo sapattakanto sapattakaraṇo[^096-7] kodhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā. Ime kho bhikkhave satta dhammā sapattakantā sapattakaraṇā[^096-7] kodhanam āgacchanti[^096-8] itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā ti. Kodhano dubbaṇṇo hoti atho[^096-9] dukkhaṃ pi seti so, atho[^096-10] atthaṃ gahetvāna[^096-11] anatthaṃ[^096-12] adhipajjati[^096-13], tato kāyena vācāya vadhaṃ[^096-14] katvāna kodhano kodhābhibhūto puriso dhanajāniṃ[^096-15] nigacchati, kodhasammadasammatto[^096-16] āyasakyaṃ[^096-17] nigacchati, ñātimittā[^096-18] suhajjā ca[^096-19] parivajjenti kodhanaṃ. Anatthajanano kodho, kodho[^096-12] cittappakopano[^096-20], bhayam antarato jātaṃ[^096-21], taṃ jano[^096-22] nāvabujjhati. Kuddho atthaṃ na jānāti[^096-23], kuddho dhammaṃ na passati, andhatamaṃ[^096-24] tadā hoti, yaṃ kodho sahate naraṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 097 -->
<a id="page-097"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 097]*

Yaṃ kuddho uparodheti[^097-1] sukaraṃ viya[^097-2] dukkaraṃ[^097-3], pacchā so vigate kodhe aggidaḍḍho[^097-4] va tappati. Dummaṅkuyaṃ[^097-5] padasseti[^097-6] dhūmaggimi[^097-7] va pāvako[^097-8], yato patāyati[^097-9] kodho yena kujjhanti[^097-10] mānavā[^097-11]. Nāssa[^097-12] hiri[^097-13] na ottappaṃ[^097-14] na {vācā[^097-15],[^097-16]} hoti gāravo kodhena abhibhūtassa na dīpaṃ[^097-17] hoti kiñcanaṃ[^097-18]. Tapanīyāni kammāni yāni dhammehi ārakā, tāni ārocayissāmi, taṃ suṇātha yathākathaṃ: kuddho hi pitaraṃ hanti, kuddho[^097-19] hanti samātaraṃ, kuddho hi brāhmaṇaṃ hanti, hanti kuddho puthujjanaṃ; yāya[^097-20] mātu bhato poso imaṃ lokaṃ avekkhati, tam pi pāṇadadiṃ[^097-21] santiṃ[^097-22] hanti kuddho puthujjano. Attupamā[^097-23] hi te sattā, attā hi paramaṃ piyo, hanti kuddho puthuttānaṃ[^097-24] nānārūpesu mucchito[^097-25]. Asinā hanti attānaṃ, visaṃ[^097-26] khādanti mucchitā, rajjuyā baddhā[^097-27] mīyanti[^097-28] pabbatām api kandare[^097-29].


<!-- PTS Page 098 -->
<a id="page-098"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 098]*

Bhūnahaccāni[^098-1] kammāni attamāraṇiyāni[^098-2] ca karontā[^098-3] nāvabujjhanti[^098-4], kodhajāto parābhavo. Itāyaṃ kodharūpena[^098-5] maccupāso[^098-6] guhāsayo, taṃ[^098-7] damena[^098-7] samucchinde[^098-8] paññāviriyena diṭṭhiyā, ekam[^098-9] ekaṃ[^098-9] akusalaṃ samucchindetha[^098-10] paṇḍito[^098-11]. Tath'; eva dhamme sikkhetha[^098-12], mā no dummaṅkuyaṃ ahu: Vītakodhā anāyāsā[^098-13] vītalobhā[^098-14] anissukā[^098-15] dantā kodhaṃ pahatvāna[^098-16] parinibbiṃsu[^098-17] anāsavā[^098-18] ti. Avyākatavaggo[^098-19] chaṭṭho[^098-20]. Tass'; uddānaṃ: Avyākato[^098-21] purisagati Tissa[^098-22] Sīha[^098-22] rakkhitapañcamaṃ[^098-23]. Kimbila[^098-24] satta[^098-25] pacalā[^098-26] sattabhariyā[^098-27] kodhanā[^098-28] ti[^098-29].

##### *[PTS Page 099]*

LXI.

1. Hirottappe bhikkhave asati hirottappavipannassa hatupaniso hoti indriyasaṃvaro, indriyasaṃvare asati indriyasaṃvaravipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti sīlaṃ, sīle asati sīlavipannassa hatupaniso hoti sammāsamādhi[^099-1], sammāsamādhimhi[^099-2] asati sammāsamādhivipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipannassa hatupaniso hoti nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge asati nibbidāvirāgavipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rukkho sākhāpalāsavipanno, tassa papaṭikā[^099-3] pi[^099-4] na[^099-5] pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho bhikkhave hirottappe asati hirottappavipannassa hatupaniso hoti . . . pe[^099-6] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ.

2. Hirottappe bhikkhave sati hirottappasampannassa upanisasampanno[^099-7] hoti indriyasaṃvaro, indriyasaṃvare sati indriyasaṃvarasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti sīlaṃ, sīle sati sīlasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti sammāsamādhi, sammāsamādhimhi[^099-2] sati sammāsamādhisampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge sati nibbidāvirāgasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rukkho sākhāpalāsasampanno, tassa papaṭikā[^099-8] pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho bhikkhave hirottappe sati hirottappasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti . . . pe[^099-9] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanan ti.

##### *[PTS Page 100]*

LXII.

1. Evaṃ[^100-1] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Ambapālivane. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante[^100-2] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Aniccā bhikkhave saṅkhārā, adhuvā bhikkhave saṅkhārā, anassāsikā bhikkhave saṅkhārā, yāvañ c'; idaṃ bhikkhave alam eva sabbasaṅkhāresu nibbindituṃ alaṃ[^100-3] virajjituṃ[^100-3] alaṃ vimuccituṃ. Sineru bhikkhave pabbatarājā caturāsītiyojanasahassāni āyāmena[^100-4] caturāsītiyojanasahassāni[^100-4] vitthārena caturāsītiyojanasahassāni mahāsamudde ajjhogāḷho caturāsītiyojanasahassāni mahāsamuddā[^100-5] accuggato. Hoti[^100-6] kho[^100-6] so bhikkhave samayo, yaṃ[^100-7] bahūni[^100-6] vassāni[^100-6] bahūni vassasatāni bahūni[^100-6] vassasahassāni[^100-6] bahūni vassasatasahassāni devo na vassati, deve kho pana bhikkhave avassante ye keci 'me bījagāmabhūtagāma[^100-8]- osadhitiṇavanappatayo[^100-9], te ussussanti[^100-10] vissussanti[^100-11] na bhavanti. Evaṃ aniccā[^100-12] bhikkhave saṅkhārā, evaṃ adhuvā bhikkhave saṅkhārā[^100-13], evaṃ anassāsikā bhikkhave saṅkhārā, yāvañ c'; idaṃ bhikkhave alam eva sabbasaṅkhāresu nibbindituṃ alaṃ virajjituṃ alaṃ vimuccituṃ. Hoti kho[^100-14] so bhikkhave samayo, yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhuno accayena dutiyo suriyo pātubhavati.

3. Dutiyassa bhikkhave suriyassa pātubhāvā yā kāci[^100-15] kunnadiyo kussubbhā[^100-16], tā ussussanti vissussanti[^100-17] na


<!-- PTS Page 101 -->
<a id="page-101"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 101]*

bhavanti. Evaṃ aniccā bhikkhave saṅkhārā . . . pe[^101-1] . . . alaṃ vimuccituṃ. Hoti kho[^101-2] so bhikkhave samayo, yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhuno accayena tatiyo suriyo pātubhavati.

4. Tatiyassa bhikkhave suriyassa pātubhāvā yā kāci[^101-3] mahānadiyo, seyyathīdaṃ Gaṅgā Yamunā Aciravatī Sarabhū Mahī[^101-4], tā[^101-5] ussussanti[^101-6] vissussanti[^101-7] na bhavanti. Evaṃ aniccā bhikkhave saṅkhārā . . .[^101-8] alaṃ vimuccituṃ. Hoti kho[^101-2] so bhikkhave samayo, yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhuno accayena catuttho suriyo pātubhavati.

5. Catutthassa bhikkhave suriyassa pātubhāvā[^101-9] ye te mahāsarā, yato imā mahānadiyo sambhavanti[^101-10], seyyathīdaṃ[^101-11] Anotattā Sīhapapātā Rathakārā Kaṇṇamuṇḍā Kuṇālā Chaddantā Mandākini, tā[^101-12] ussussanti[^101-13] vissussanti[^101-14] na bhavanti. Evaṃ aniccā bhikkhave saṅkhārā . . .[^101-8] alaṃ vimuccituṃ. Hoti kho[^101-15] so bhikkhave samayo, yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhuno accayena pañcamo suriyo pātubhavati.

6. Pañcamassa bhikkhave suriyassa pātubhāvā yojanasatikāni pi mahāsamudde udakāni ogacchanti, dviyojanasatikāni pi mahāsamudde[^101-16] udakāni[^101-16] ogacchanti[^101-16], tiyojanasatikāni pi mahāsamudde[^101-17] udakāni[^101-17] ogacchanti[^101-17] . . . pe[^101-18] . . . sattayojanasatikāni pi mahāsamudde udakāni ogacchanti; sattatālam[^101-19] pi mahāsamudde udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, chatālam pi pañcatālam pi catutālam pi titālam pi dvitālam

<a id="page-102"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 102]*

pi tālamattam pi mahāsamudde udakaṃ saṇṭhāti; sattaporisam[^102-1] pi mahāsamudde udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, chaporisam pi pañcaporisam pi catuporisam pi tiporisam pi dviporisam pi porisamattam[^102-2] pi aḍḍhaporisam[^102-3] pi[^102-3] kaṭimattam[^102-3] pi[^102-3] jānukamattam[^102-4] pi gopphakamattam pi mahāsamudde udakaṃ saṇṭhāti. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave saradasamaye thullaphusitake[^102-5] deve vassante tattha tattha gopadesu[^102-6] udakāni ṭhitāni honti, evam eva kho bhikkhave tattha tattha gopadamattāni[^102-7] mahāsamudde udakāni ṭhitāni honti. Pañcamassa bhikkhave suriyassa pātubhāvā aṅgulipabbamattam[^102-8] pi mahāsamudde udakaṃ na hoti. Evaṃ aniccā bhikkhave saṅkhārā . . .[^102-9] alaṃ vimuccituṃ. Hoti kho[^102-10] so bhikkhave samayo, yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhuno accayena chaṭṭho suriyo pātubhavati.

7. Chaṭṭhassa bhikkhave suriyassa pātubhāvā ayañ ca mahāpaṭhavī Sineru ca[^102-11] pabbatarājā dhūpāyanti[^102-12] sandhūpāyanti[^102-13] sampadhūpāyanti[^102-14]. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave kumbhakārapāko ālimpito[^102-15] paṭhamaṃ[^102-16] dhūpeti[^102-17] sandhūpeti[^102-18] sampadhūpeti[^102-19], evam eva kho bhikkhave chaṭṭhassa suriyassa pātubhāvā ayañ ca mahāpaṭhavī Sineru ca pabbatarājā dhūpāyanti[^102-20] sandhūpāyanti[^102-13] sampadhūpāyanti[^102-14]. Evaṃ aniccā bhikkhave saṅkhārā . . .[^102-21] alaṃ vimuccituṃ. Hoti kho[^102-10] so bhikkhave samayo, yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhuno accayena sattamo suriyo pātubhavati.
