8. Sattamassa bhikkhave suriyassa pātubhāvā ayañ ca mahāpaṭhavī[^103-1] Sineru ca[^103-2] pabbatarājā ādippanti[^103-3] pajjalanti ekajālā[^103-4] bhavanti; imissā ca bhikkhave mahāpaṭhaviyā Sinerussa ca pabbatarājassa jhāyamānānaṃ dayhamānānaṃ[^103-5] acci[^103-6] vātena khittā yāva Brahmalokā pi gacchati[^103-7], Sinerussa ca[^103-8] bhikkhave pabbatarājassa jhāyamānassa dayhamānassa[^103-2] vinassamānassa[^103-9] mahatā tejokhandhena abhibhūtassa yojanasatikāni pi kūṭāni palujjanti, dviyojanasatikāni pi . . .[^103-10] tiyojanasatikāni pi . . .[^103-10] catuyojanasatikāni pi . . .[^103-10] pañcayojanasatikāni pi kūṭāni palujjanti; imissā ca bhikkhave mahāpaṭhaviyā Sinerussa ca pabbatarājassa jhāyamānānaṃ dayhamānānaṃ[^103-11] n'; eva chārikā paññāyati na masi[^103-12]. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave sappissa vā telassa vā jhāyamānassa[^103-13] dayhamānassa n'; eva chārikā paññāyati na masi[^103-14], evam eva kho bhikkhave imissā ca mahāpaṭhaviyā Sinerussa ca[^103-2] pabbatarājassa jhāyamānānaṃ dayhamānānaṃ[^103-11] n'; eva chārikā paññāyati na masi[^103-15]. Evaṃ aniccā bhikkhave saṅkhārā, evaṃ adhuvā bhikkhave saṅkhārā, evaṃ anassāsikā bhikkhave saṅkhārā, yāvañ c'; idaṃ bhikkhave alam eva sabbasaṅkhāresu nibbindituṃ alaṃ virajjituṃ alaṃ vimuccituṃ. Tatra bhikkhave ko mantā ko saddhātā[^103-16] ‘ayañ ca paṭhavī Sineru ca[^103-17] pabbatarājā dayhissanti vinassissanti[^103-18] na bhavissantī'; ti[^103-19] aññatra diṭṭhapadehi?

9. Bhūtapubbaṃ bhikkhave Sunetto nāma satthā ahosi titthakaro kāmesu vītarāgo. Sunettassa kho pana bhik-


<!-- PTS Page 104 -->
<a id="page-104"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 104]*

khave satthuno anekāni sāvakasatāni ahesuṃ. Sunetto[^104-1] satthā sāvakānaṃ Brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desesi[^104-2]. Ye kho pana bhikkhave Sunettassa satthuno Brahmalokasahavyatāya[^104-3] dhammaṃ desentassa sabbena sabbaṃ sāsanaṃ ājāniṃsu[^104-4], te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ Brahmalokaṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5]. Ye na[^104-6] sabbena sabbaṃ sāsanaṃ ājāniṃsu[^104-7], te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā app ekacce Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5], app ekacce Nimmānaratīnaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5], app ekacce Tusitānaṃ[^104-8] devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5], app ekacce Yāmānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5], app ekacce Tāvatiṃsānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5], app ekacce Cātummahārājikānaṃ[^104-9] devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5], app ekacce khattiyamahāsālānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5], app ekacce brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5], app ekacce gahapatimahāsālānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjiṃsu[^104-5].

10. Atha kho bhikkhave Sunettassa satthuno etad ahosi ‘na kho pan'[^104-10] etaṃ[^104-10] naṭirūpaṃ, yo 'haṃ[^104-11] sāvakānaṃ samasamagatiyo[^104-12] assaṃ abhisamparāyaṃ[^104-13], yan nūnāhaṃ uttariṃ[^104-14] mettaṃ[^104-15] bhāveyyan'; ti. Atha kho bhikkhave Sunetto satthā satta vassāni mettacittaṃ[^104-16] bhāvesi, satta[^104-17] vassāni mettacittaṃ[^104-16] bhāvetvā satta saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappe na yimaṃ lokaṃ punar[^104-18] āgamāsi[^104-18], saṃvaṭṭamāne[^104-19] sudaṃ[^104-19]


<!-- PTS Page 105 -->
<a id="page-105"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 105]*

bhikkhave loke Ābhassarupago[^105-1] hoti[^105-2], vivaṭṭamāne loke suññaṃ Brahmavimānaṃ upapajjati[^105-3]. Tatra sudaṃ bhikkhave Brahmā hoti Mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūto aññadatthudaso vasavattī, chattiṃsakkhattuṃ[^105-4] kho pana bhikkhave Sakko ahosi devānam indo, anekasatakkhattuṃ[^105-5] rājā ahosi Cakkavattī dhammiko dhammarājā[^105-6] cāturanto[^105-7] vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappatto sattaratanasamannāgato. Paro sahassaṃ kho pan'; assa puttā ahesuṃ sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasenappamaddanā. So imaṃ paṭhaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍena asatthena dhammena abhivijiya[^105-8] ajjhāvasi[^105-9]. So hi nāma bhikkhave Sunetto satthā evaṃdīghāyuko samāno evaṃciraṭṭhiko aparimutto ahosi jātiyā jarāya[^105-10] maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi, aparimutto dukkhasmā ti vadāmi. Taṃ kissa hetu? Catunnaṃ dhammānaṃ ananubodhā appaṭivedhā. Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ?

11. Ariyassa bhikkhave[^105-11] sīlassa ananubodhā appaṭivedhā, ariyassa[^105-12] samādhissa ananubodhā[^105-13] appaṭivedhā[^105-13], ariyāya paññāya ananubodhā appaṭivedhā, ariyāya vimuttiyā ananubodhā appaṭivedhā. Tayidaṃ[^105-14] bhikkhave ariyaṃ sīlaṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, ariyo samādhi anubuddho[^105-15] paṭividdho, ariyā paññā anubuddhā[^105-16] paṭividdhā, ariyā vimutti anubuddhā[^105-16] patividdhā. Ucchinnā bhavataṇhā, khīṇā bhavanetti, natthi dāni punabbhavo ti.


<!-- PTS Page 106 -->
<a id="page-106"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 106]*

Idam avoca Bhagavā, idaṃ vatvā[^106-1] Sugato athāparaṃ etad avoca Satthā: Sīlaṃ samādhi paññā ca vimutti ca anuttarā, anubuddhā ime dhammā Gotamena[^106-2] yasassinā[^106-3]. Iti buddho abhiññāya dhammam akkhāsi bhikkhunaṃ[^106-4] dukkhass'; antakaro Satthā cakkhumā parinibbuto ti. LXIII.

1. Yato kho bhikkhave rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ sattahi nagaraparikkhārehi suparikkhittaṃ[^106-5] hoti catunnañ ca āhārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī: idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ akaraṇīyaṃ bāhirehi paccatthikehi paccāmittehi. Katamehi sattahi nagaraparikkhārehi suparikkhittaṃ[^106-6] hoti?

2. Idha bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare esikā hoti gambhīranemā sunikhātā acalā asampavedhi[^106-7]. Iminā paṭhamena nagaraparikkhārena suparikkhittaṃ[^106-8] hoti rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā[^106-9] bāhirānaṃ[^106-9] paṭighātāya.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare parikhā[^106-10] hoti gambhīrā c'; eva vitthatā ca. Iminā dutiyena nagaraparikkhārena suparikkhittaṃ[^106-11] hoti rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya.


<!-- PTS Page 107 -->
<a id="page-107"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 107]*

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare[^107-1] anupariyāyapatho[^107-2] hoti ucco c'; eva vitthato ca. Iminā tatiyena nagaraparikkhārena suparikkhittaṃ[^107-3] hoti rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahuṃ[^107-4] āvudhaṃ sanniciṭaṃ hoti salākañ[^107-5] c'; eva jevanīyañ[^107-6] ca. Iminā catutthena nagaraparikkhārena suparikkhittaṃ[^107-3] hoti rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahu[^107-7] balakāyo paṭivasati, seyyathīdaṃ hatthārohā assārohā rathikā dhanuggahā celakā[^107-8] calakā piṇḍadāyikā[^107-9] uggā rājaputtā pakkhandino[^107-10] mahānāgā sūrā[^107-11] cammayodhino dāsakaputtā[^107-12]. Iminā pañcamena nagaraparikkhārena suparikkhittaṃ[^107-3] hoti rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare dovāriko hoti paṇḍito vyatto medhāvī aññātānaṃ nivāretā ñātānaṃ pavesetā. Iminā chaṭṭhena nagaraparikkhārena suparikkhittaṃ[^107-13] hoti rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare pākāro hoti ucco c'; eva vitthato ca vāsanalepanasampanno ca[^107-14]. Iminā sattamena nagaraparikkhārena suparikkhittaṃ[^107-3] hoti rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya.


<!-- PTS Page 108 -->
<a id="page-108"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 108]*

Imehi sattahi nagaraparikkhārehi suparikkhittaṃ[^108-1] hoti. Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ āhārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī[^108-2]?

9. Idha bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahuṃ[^108-3] tiṇakaṭṭhodakaṃ[^108-4] sannicitaṃ hoti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā[^108-5] aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya.

10. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahuṃ[^108-3] sāliyavakaṃ sannicitaṃ hoti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya.

11. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahuṃ[^108-6] tilamuggamāsāparaṇṇaṃ[^108-7] sannicitaṃ hoti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya.

12. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahuṃ[^108-8] bhesajjaṃ sannicitaṃ hoti, seyyathīdaṃ sappi navanītaṃ telaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ loṇaṃ, abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya. Imesaṃ[^108-9] catunnaṃ āhārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī[^108-2] akasiralābhī. Yato kho bhikkhave rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ[^108-10] imehi[^108-11] sattahi nagaraparikkhārehi suparikkhittaṃ[^108-12] hoti imesañ[^108-11] ca[^108-13] catunnaṃ[^108-14] āhārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī: idaṃ vuccati[^108-15] bhikkhave rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ akaraṇīyaṃ bāhirehi paccatthikehi paccāmittehi. Evam eva kho bhikkhave yato ariyasāvako sattahi saddhammehi[^108-16] samannāgato hoti catunnañ ca[^108-17]


<!-- PTS Page 109 -->
<a id="page-109"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 109]*

jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^109-1] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti[^109-2] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī: ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ariyasāvako akaraṇīyo Mārassa akaraṇīyo[^109-3] pāpimato. Katamehi sattahi saddhammehi samannāgato hoti[^109-3]?

13. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare esikā hoti gambhīranemā[^109-4] sunikhātā acalā asampavedhi abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako saddho hoti, saddhati Tathāgatassa bodhiṃ ‘iti pi so Bhagavā[^109-5] arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Saddhāsiko[^109-6] bhikkhave ariyasāvako akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāveti; sāvajjaṃ pajahati, anavajjaṃ bhāveti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati. Iminā paṭhamena saddhammena samannāgato hoti.

14. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare parikhā hoti gambhīrā c'; eva vitthatā ca abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako hirimā hoti, hiriyati[^109-7] kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena, hiriyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Hiriparikho[^109-8] bhikkhave ariyasāvako akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāveti; sāvajjaṃ pajahati, anavajjaṃ bhāveti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati. Iminā dutiyena saddhammena samannāgato hoti[^109-9].

15. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare anupariyāyapatho hoti ucco c'; eva vitthato ca abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako ottappī hoti, ottappati kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena, ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Ottappapariyāyapatho[^109-10] bhikkhave ariyasāvako akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 110 -->
<a id="page-110"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 110]*

bhāveti; sāvajjaṃ pajahati, anavajjaṃ bhāveti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati. Iminā tatiyena saddhammena samannāgato hoti.

16. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahuṃ[^110-1] āvudhaṃ sannicitaṃ hoti salākañ c'; eva jevanīyañ[^110-2] ca abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako bahussuto hoti[^110-3] sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Sutāvudho[^110-4] bhikkhave ariyasāvako akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāveti; sāvajjaṃ pajahati, anavajjaṃ bhāveti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati. Iminā catutthena saddhammena samannāgato hoti.

17. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahu balakāyo paṭivasati, seyyathīdaṃ hatthārohā assārohā[^110-5] rathikā dhanuggahā celakā calakā piṇḍadāyikā[^110-6] uggā rājaputtā pakkhandino[^110-7] mahānāgā sūrā[^110-8] cammayodhino dāsakaputtā[^110-9] abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Viriyabalakāyo[^110-10] bhikkhave ariyasāvako akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāveti; sāvajjaṃ pajahati, anavajjaṃ bhāveti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati. Iminā pañcamena saddhammena samannāgato hoti.

18. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare dovāriko hoti paṇḍito vyatto medhāvī aññātānaṃ nivāretā ñātānaṃ pavesetā[^110-11] abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 111 -->
<a id="page-111"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 111]*

paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako satimā hoti paramena satinepakkena samannāgato cirakatam pi cirabhāsitam pi saritā anussaritā[^111-1]. Satidovāriko[^111-2] bhikkhave ariyasāvako akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāveti; sāvajjaṃ pajahati, anavajjaṃ bhāveti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati. Iminā chaṭṭhena saddhammena samannāgato hoti.

19. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare pākāro[^111-3] hoti ucco c'; eva vitthato ca vāsanalepanasampanno[^111-4] ca abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako paññavā hoti, udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Paññāvāsanalepanasampanno[^111-5] bhikkhave ariyasāvako akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāveti; sāvajjaṃ pajahati, anavajjaṃ bhāveti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati. Iminā sattamena saddhammena samannāgato hoti. Imehi sattahi saddhammehi samannāgato hoti. Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^111-6] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī[^111-7] akasiralābhī?

20. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahuṃ[^111-8] tiṇakaṭṭhodakaṃ sannicitaṃ hoti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako vivicc'; eva kāmehi[^111-9] vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ[^111-10] jhānaṃ[^111-10] upasampajja viharati attano ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya okkamanāya nibbānassa.

21. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare


<!-- PTS Page 112 -->
<a id="page-112"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 112]*

bahuṃ[^112-1] sāliyavakaṃ sannicitaṃ hoti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya[^112-2] bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā[^112-3] ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati attano ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya okkamanāya nibbānassa.

22. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahuṃ[^112-4] tilamuggamāsāparaṇṇaṃ[^112-5] sannicitaṃ hoti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako pītiyā ca virāgā[^112-3] upekhako ca viharati sato sampajāno sukhañ ca kāyena paṭisaṃvedeti yan taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti upekhako satimā sukhavihārī ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati attano ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya okkamanāya nibbānassa.

23. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rañño paccantime nagare bahuṃ[^112-6] bhesajjaṃ sannicitaṃ hoti, seyyathīdaṃ sappi navanītaṃ telaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ[^112-7] loṇaṃ, abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya[^112-8] bāhirānaṃ[^112-9] paṭighātāya, evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako sukhassa ca pahānā[^112-10] dukkhassa ca pahānā pubb'; eva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upekhāsatipārisuddhiṃ[^112-11] catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati attano ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya okkamanāya nibbānassa. Imesaṃ catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^112-12] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī[^112-13].


<!-- PTS Page 113 -->
<a id="page-113"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 113]*

Yato kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako imehi sattahi saddhammehi[^113-1] samannāgato hoti imesañ ca[^113-2] catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī: ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ariyasāvako akaraṇīyo Mārassa akaraṇīyo pāpimato ti. LXIV.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^113-3] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi sattahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammaññū ca hoti atthaññū ca[^113-2] attaññū[^113-4] ca[^113-4] mattaññū ca kālaññū ca parisaññū ca puggalaparoparaññū[^113-5] ca. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammaññū hoti?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammaṃ jānāti[^113-6]: suttaṃ geyyaṃ veyyākaraṇaṃ gāthaṃ udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vedallaṃ. No ce bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammaṃ jāneyya: suttaṃ geyyaṃ[^113-7] . . .[^113-8]abbhutadhammaṃ[^113-9] vedallaṃ, na yidha dhammaññū ti vucceyya; yasmā ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammaṃ jānāti: suttaṃ geyyaṃ[^113-7] . . .[^113-8] abbhutadhammaṃ[^113-7] vedallaṃ, tasmā[^113-10] dhammaññū ti[^113-11] vuccati. Iti dhammaññū. Atthaññū ca kathaṃ hoti?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu tassa tass'; eva bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa attho, ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa attho'; ti. No ce bhikkhave bhikkhu tassa tass' eva bhāsitassa atthaṃ jāneyya ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa attho, ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa attho'; ti, na yidha atthaññū ti vucceyya; yasmā ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu tassa tass' eva bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa attho,


<!-- PTS Page 114 -->
<a id="page-114"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 114]*

ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa attho'; ti, tasmā atthaññū ti vuccati. Iti dhammaññū, atthaññū. Attaññū[^114-1] ca kathaṃ hoti?

5. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu attānaṃ jānāti ‘ettako 'mhi saddhāya sīlena sutena cāgena paññāya paṭibhānenā[^114-2]'; ti. No ce bhikkhave bhikkhu attānaṃ jāneyya ‘ettako'; mhi saddhāya sīlena sutena cāgena paññāya paṭibhānenā'; ti, na yidha attaññū ti vucceyya; yasmā ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu attānaṃ jānāti ‘ettako'; mhi saddhāya sīlena sutena cāgena paññāya paṭibhānenā'; ti, tasmā attaññū ti vuccati. Iti dhammaññū, atthaññū[^114-3], attaññū[^114-4]. Mattaññū ca kathaṃ hoti?

6. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu mattaṃ jānāti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ paṭiggahaṇāya. No ce bhikkhave bhikkhu mattaṃ jāneyya cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ paṭiggahaṇāya, na yidha mattaññū ti vucceyya; yasmā ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu mattaṃ jānāti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ paṭiggahaṇāya, tasmā mattaññū ti vuccati. Iti dhammaññū, atthaññū[^114-3], attaññū[^114-1], mattaññū. Kālaññū ca kathaṃ hoti?

7. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kālaṃ jānāti ‘ayaṃ kālo uddesassa, ayaṃ kālo paripucchāya, ayaṃ kālo yogassa. ayaṃ kālo paṭisallānāyā[^114-5]'; ti. No ce bhikkhave bhikkhu kālaṃ jāneyya ‘ayaṃ kālo uddesassa, ayaṃ kālo paripucchāya, ayaṃ kālo yogassa, ayaṃ kālo paṭisallānāyā'; ti, na yidha kālaññū ti vucceyya; yasmā ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu kālaṃ jānāti ‘ayaṃ kālo uddesassa, ayaṃ kālo paripucchāya, ayaṃ kālo yogassa, ayaṃ kālo paṭisallānāyā' ti, tasmā kālaññū ti vuccati. Iti dhammaññū, atthaññū, attaññū, mattaññū, kālaññū. Parisaññū ca kathaṃ hoti?

8. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu parisaṃ jānāti ‘ayaṃ khattiyaparisā, ayaṃ brāhmaṇaparisā, ayaṃ gahapatiparisā, ayaṃ samaṇaparisā; tattha evaṃ upasaṅkamitabbaṃ, evaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 115 -->
<a id="page-115"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 115]*

ṭhātabbaṃ[^115-1], evaṃ nisīditabbaṃ, evaṃ bhāsitabbaṃ, evaṃ tuṇhībhavitabban'; ti. No ce bhikkhave bhikkhu parisaṃ jāneyya ‘ayaṃ khattiyaparisā[^115-2], ayaṃ brāhmaṇaparisā, ayaṃ gahapatiparisā, ayaṃ samaṇaparisā; tattha evaṃ upasaṅkamitabbaṃ, evaṃ ṭhātabbaṃ, evaṃ nisīditabbaṃ, evaṃ bhāsitabbaṃ, evaṃ tuṇhībhavitabban'; ti, na yidha parisaññū ti vucceyya; yasmā ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu parisaṃ jānāti ‘ayaṃ khattiyaparisā[^115-3], ayaṃ brāhmaṇaparisā, ayaṃ gahapatiparisā, ayaṃ samaṇaparisā; tattha evaṃ upasaṅkamitabbaṃ, evaṃ ṭhātabbaṃ, evaṃ nisīditabbaṃ, evaṃ bhāsitabbaṃ, evaṃ tuṇhībhavitabban'; ti, tasmā parisaññū ti vuccati. Iti dhammaññū, atthaññū, attaññū, mattaññū, kālaññū, parisaññū. Puggalaparoparaññū[^115-4] ca kathaṃ hoti?

9. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno dvayena[^115-5] puggalā viditā honti: dve puggalā, eko ariyānaṃ dassanakāmo[^115-6], eko ariyānaṃ na[^115-7] dassanakāmo[^115-8]. Yvāyaṃ puggalo ariyānaṃ na dassanakāmo, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena[^115-9] gārayho. Yvāyaṃ puggalo ariyānaṃ dassanakāmo, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena[^115-10] pāsaṃso. Dve puggalā ariyānaṃ dassanakāmā: eko[^115-11] saddhammaṃ sotukāmo, eko saddhammaṃ na sotukāmo[^115-12]. Yvāyaṃ puggalo saddhammaṃ na sotukāmo, evaṃ so ten' aṅgena gārayho. Yvāyaṃ puggalo saddhammaṃ sotukāmo, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Dve[^115-13] puggalā saddhammaṃ sotukāmā: eko ohitasoto dhammaṃ suṇāti, eko anohitasoto dhammaṃ suṇāti. Yvāyaṃ[^115-14] puggalo anohitasoto dhammaṃ suṇāti, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Yvāyaṃ[^115-15] puggalo


<!-- PTS Page 116 -->
<a id="page-116"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 116]*

ohitasoto dhammaṃ suṇāti, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Dve puggalā ohitasotā dhammaṃ suṇanti[^116-1]: eko sutvā dhammaṃ dhāreti, eko sutvā dhammaṃ na dhāreti. Yvāyaṃ[^116-2] puggalo sutvā dhammaṃ na[^116-3] dhāreti, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Yvāyaṃ[^116-2] puggalo sutvā dhammaṃ dhāreti, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Dve puggalā sutvā dhammaṃ dhārenti: eko dhatānaṃ[^116-4] dhammānaṃ atthaṃ[^116-5] upaparikkhati, eko dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ na[^116-6] upaparikkhati. Yvāyaṃ[^116-7] puggalo dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ na upaparikkhati, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Yvāyaṃ[^116-2] puggalo dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Dve puggalā dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhanti: eko attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno, eko[^116-8] na[^116-9] attham aññāya dhammam aññāya[^116-10] dhammānudhammapaṭipanno. Yvāyaṃ[^116-2] puggalo na[^116-9] attham aññāya dhammam aññāya[^116-10] dhammānudhammapaṭipanno, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Yvāyaṃ[^116-2] puggalo attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Dve puggalā attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipannā: eko attahitāya paṭipanno no parahitāya, eko attahitāya ca paṭipanno parahitāya ca[^116-11] Yvāyaṃ[^116-2] puggalo attahitāya paṭipanno, no[^116-12] parahitāya, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Yvāyaṃ puggalo attahitāya ca paṭipanno parahitāya ca, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Evaṃ kho[^116-6] bhikkhave bhikkhuno[^116-13] dvayena puggalā viditā honti. Evaṃ kho[^116-14] bhikkhave bhikkhu puggalaparoparaññū[^116-15] hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 117 -->
<a id="page-117"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 117]*

Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti[^117-1] . . . pe[^117-2] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. LXV.

1. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ[^117-3] pāricchattako koviḷāro[^117-4] paṇḍupalāso hoti, attamanā bhikkhave devā Tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samaye honti ‘paṇḍupalāso dāni pāricchattako koviḷāro, na cirass'; eva dāni sattapalāso[^117-5] bhavissatī[^117-6]'; ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro sattapalāso[^117-7] hoti, attamanā bhikkhave devā Tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samaye honti ‘sattapalāso[^117-8] dāni pāricchattako koviḷāro, na cirass'; eva dāni jālakajāto[^117-9] bhavissatī[^117-6]'; ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro jālakajāto hoti, attamanā bhikkhave devā Tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samaye honti ‘jālakajāto[^117-10] dāni pāricchattako koviḷāro, na cirass' eva dāni khārakajāto[^117-11] bhavissatī'; ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro khārakajāto hoti, attamanā bhikkhave devā Tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samaye honti ‘khārakajāto dāni paricchattako koviḷāro, na cirass'; eva dāni kuḍumalakajāto bhavissatī' ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro kuḍumalakajāto[^117-12] hoti, attamanā bhikkhave devā Tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samaye honti ‘kuḍumalakajāto[^117-12] dāni[^117-13] pāricchattako koviḷāro, na cirass'; eva dāni


<!-- PTS Page 118 -->
<a id="page-118"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 118]*

kokāsakajāto[^118-1] bhavissatī'; ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro kokāsakajāto[^118-2] hoti, attamanā bhikkhave devā Tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samaye honti ‘kokāsakajāto dāni pāricchattako koviḷāro, na cirass'; eva dāni sabbaphāliphullo[^118-3] bhavissatī'; ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako kovilāro sabbaphāliphullo[^118-3] hoti, attamanā bhikkhave devā Tāvatiṃsā pāricchattakassa koviḷārassa mūle dibbe cattāro māse pañcahi kāmaguṇehi samappitā samaṅgibhūtā[^118-4] paricārenti[^118-5]. Sabbaphāliphullassa kho pana bhikkhave pāricchattakassa koviḷārassa samantā paññāsayojanāni ābhāya phuṭaṃ[^118-6] hoti. Anuvātaṃ[^118-7] yojanasataṃ gandho gacchati. Ayam ānubhāvo pāricchattakassa koviḷārassa.

2. Evam eva kho bhikkhave yasmiṃ samaye ariyasāvako agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjāya ceteti, paṇḍupalāso bhikkhave ariyasāvako tasmiṃ samaye hoti devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye ariyasāvako[^118-8] kesamassuṃ ohāretvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajito hoti, sattapalāso[^118-9] bhikkhave ariyasāvako tasmiṃ samaye hoti devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye ariyasāvako vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . . pe[^118-10] . . . paṭhamaṃ[^118-11] jhānaṃ[^118-11] upasampajja viharati, jālakajāto bhikkhave ariyasāvako tasmiṃ samaye hoti devānaṃ[^118-12] Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye ariyasāvako vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . . pe[^118-10] . . . dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, khārakajāto bhikkhave ariyasāvako tasmiṃ samaye hoti[^118-13] devānaṃ[^118-12] Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave

samaye ariyasāvako pītiyā ca virāgā . . . pe[^119-1] . . . tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, kuḍumalakajāto bhikkhave ariyasāvako tasmiṃ samaye hoti devānaṃ[^119-2] Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye ariyasāvako sukhassa ca pahānā[^119-3] . . . pe[^119-4] . . . catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, kokāsakajāto bhikkhave ariyasāvako tasmiṃ samaye hoti devānaṃ[^119-2] Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye ariyasāvako āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^119-5] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati, sabbaphāliphullo bhikkhave ariyasāvako tasmiṃ samaye hoti devānaṃ[^119-2] Tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattako koviḷāro. Tasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye Bhummā devā saddam anussāventi[^119-6] ‘eso itthannāmo āyasmā itthannāmassa āyasmato saddhivihārī[^119-7] amukamhā gāmā vā nigamā vā agārasmā[^119-8] anagāriyaṃ pabbajito āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^119-5] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharatī'; ti. Bhummānaṃ devānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā Cātummahārājikā[^119-9] devā . . .[^119-10] Tāvatiṃsā devā . . . Yāmā devā . . . Tusitā devā . . . Nimmānaratī devā . . . Paranimmitavasavattī devā . . . Brahmakāyikā devā saddam anussāventi[^119-11] ‘eso itthannāmo āyasmā itthannāmassa āyasmato saddhivihārī[^119-7] amukamhā[^119-12] gāmā vā nigamā vā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajito āsavānaṃ khayā[^119-13] anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ ditth'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharatī'; ti. Iti ha

##### *[PTS Page 120]*

tena khaṇena[^120-1] tena muhuttena yāva[^120-2] Brahmalokā saddo abbhuggacchati. Ayam ānubhāvo khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno ti. LXVI.

1. Atha kho āyasmato Sāriputtassa rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi ‘kin nu kho bhikkhu sakkatvā garukatvā[^120-3] upanissāya viharanto akusalaṃ pajaheyya kusalaṃ bhāveyyā'; ti? Atha kho āyasmato Sāriputtassa etad ahosi ‘Satthāraṃ kho bhikkhu sakkatvā garukatvā upanissāya viharanto akusalaṃ pajaheyya kusalaṃ bhāveyya, dhammaṃ kho bhikkhu[^120-4] . . .[^120-5] saṅghaṃ kho bhikkhu[^120-4] . . .[^120-5] sikkhaṃ kho bhikkhu[^120-4] . . .[^120-5] samādhiṃ kho bhikkhu[^120-4] . . .[^120-5] appamādaṃ kho bhikkhu[^120-4] . . .[^120-5] paṭisanthāraṃ[^120-6] kho bhikkhu sakkatvā garukatvā upanissāya viharanto akusalaṃ pajaheyya kusalaṃ bhāveyyā'; ti. Atha kho āyasmato Sāriputtassa etad ahosi ‘ime kho me[^120-7] dhammā parisuddhā pariyodātā; yan nūnāhaṃ ime dhamme gantvā[^120-8] Bhagavato āroceyyaṃ[^120-9], evaṃ me[^120-10] ime dhammā parisuddhā c'; eva bhavissanti parisuddhasaṃkhātatarā ca. Seyyathā pi nāma puriso suvaṇṇanikkhaṃ adhigaccheyya parisuddhaṃ pariyodātaṃ, tassa evam assa <<ayaṃ kho me suvaṇṇanikkho parisuddho pariyodāto; yan nūnāhaṃ imaṃ suvaṇṇanikkhaṃ gantvā kammārānaṃ[^120-11] dasseyyaṃ, evaṃ me ayaṃ suvaṇṇanikkho kammāragato[^120-12] parisuddho c'; eva bhavissati parisuddhasaṃkhātataro[^120-13] cā[^120-14]>> ti: evam eva me[^120-15] ime dhammā parisuddhā pariyodātā; yan nūnāhaṃ ime dhamme gantvā Bhagavato āroceyyaṃ, evaṃ me[^120-10] ime dhammā parisuddhā c'; eva bhavissanti parisuddhasaṃkhātatarā cā'; ti. Atha kho

āyasmā Sāriputto sāyaṇhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Idha mayhaṃ bhante rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi ‘kin nu kho bhikkhu sakkatvā garukatvā upanissāya viharanto akusalaṃ pajaheyya kusalaṃ bhāveyyā'; ti? Tassa[^121-1] mayhaṃ bhante etad ahosi ‘Satthāraṃ kho bhikkhu[^121-2] sakkatvā garukatvā upanissāya viharanto akusalaṃ pajaheyya kusalaṃ bhāveyya, dhammaṃ kho bhikkhu[^121-3] . . .[^121-4] saṅghaṃ[^121-5] kho bhikkhu[^121-3] . . . sikkhaṃ kho bhikkhu[^121-3] . . . samādhiṃ kho bhikkhu[^121-3] . . . appamādaṃ kho bhikkhu[^121-3] . . . paṭisanthāraṃ kho bhikkhu sakkatvā[^121-6] garukatvā upanissāya viharanto akusalaṃ pajaheyya kusalaṃ bhāveyyā'; ti. Tassa[^121-1] mayhaṃ bhante etad ahosi ‘ime kho me[^121-7] dhammā parisuddhā pariyodātā; yan nūnāhaṃ ime dhamme gantvā Bhagavato āroceyyaṃ, evaṃ me[^121-8] ime dhammā parisuddhā c'; eva bhavissanti parisuddhasaṃkhātatarā ca. Seyyathā pi nāma puriso suvaṇṇanikkhaṃ adhigaccheyya parisuddhaṃ pariyodātaṃ, tassa evam assa <<ayaṃ kho me suvaṇṇanikkho parisuddho pariyodāto; yan nūnāhaṃ imaṃ suvaṇṇanikkhaṃ gantvā kammārānaṃ[^121-9] dasseyyaṃ, evaṃ me ayaṃ suvaṇṇanikkho kammāragato parisuddho c'; eva bhavissati parisuddhasaṃkhātataro cā[^121-10]>> ti: evam eva[^121-11] me[^121-12] ime dhammā parisuddhā pariyodātā; yan nūnāhaṃ ime dhamme gantvā Bhagavato āroceyyaṃ, evaṃ me[^121-13] ime dhammā parisuddhā c'; eva bhavissanti

parisuddhasaṃkhātatarā cā'; ti. ‘Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta, Satthāraṃ kho Sāriputta bhikkhu sakkatvā garukatvā upanissāya viharanto akusalaṃ pajaheyya kusalaṃ bhāveyya, dhammaṃ kho Sāriputta[^122-1] bhikkhu[^122-2] . . . saṅghaṃ kho Sāriputta[^122-3] bhikkhu[^122-3] . . .[^122-4] sikkhaṃ kho Sāriputta[^122-3] bhikkhu[^122-3] . . . samādhiṃ kho Sāriputta[^122-3] bhikkhu[^122-3] . . . appamādaṃ kho Sāriputta[^122-3] bhikkhu[^122-3] . . . paṭisanthāraṃ[^122-5] kho Sāriputta bhikkhu sakkatvā garukatvā upanissāya viharanto akusalaṃ pajaheyya kusalaṃ bhāveyyā'; ti. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

3. Imassa kho ahaṃ bhante Bhagavatā[^122-6] saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāmi[^122-7]. So vata bhante bhikkhu Satthari agāravo dhamme sagāravo bhavissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati; yo so bhante bhikkhu Satthari agāravo, dhamme pi so agāravo[^122-8]. So vata bhante bhikkhu Satthari agāravo dhamme agāravo saṅghe sagāravo bhavissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati; yo so bhante bhikkhu Satthari agāravo dhamme agāravo, saṅghe pi so agāravo[^122-8]. So vata bhante bhikkhu Satthari agāravo dhamme agāravo saṅghe agāravo sikkhāya sagāravo bhavissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati; yo so bhante bhikkhu Satthari agāravo dhamme agāravo saṅghe agāravo, sikkhāya pi so agāravo. So vata bhante bhikkhu Satthari agāravo dhamme agāravo saṅghe agāravo sikkhāya agāravo samādhismiṃ sagāravo bhavissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati; yo so bhante bhikkhu Satthari agāravo dhamme agāravo saṅghe agāravo sikkhāya agāravo, samādhismim pi so agāravo. 4. Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta, sādhu[^124-2] kho[^124-2] tvaṃ[^124-2] Sāriputta[^124-2] imassa mayā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāsi[^124-3]. So vata Sāriputta bhikkhu Satthari agāravo dhamme sagāravo bhavissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati[^124-4]; yo so Sāriputta bhikkhu Satthari agāravo, dhamme pi[^124-5] so[^124-5] agāravo[^124-6] . . .[^124-7] So vata Sāriputta bhikkhu Satthari agāravo . . . dhamme agāravo . . . saṅghe[^124-7] agāravo[^124-7] . . . sikkhāya agāravo . . . samādhismiṃ agāravo . . . appamāde[^124-8] agāravo paṭisanthāre sagāravo bhavissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati; yo so Sāriputta bhikkhu Satthari agāravo dhamme agāravo saṅghe agāravo sikkhāya agāravo samādhismiṃ agāravo appamāde agāravo, paṭisanthāre pi so agāravo. So vata Sāriputta bhikkhu Satthari sagāravo dhamme agāravo bhavissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati[^124-9]; yo so Sāriputta bhikkhu Satthari sagāravo, dhamme pi so sagā-

##### *[PTS Page 125]*

ravo[^125-1] . . .[^125-2] So vata Sāriputta bhikkhu Satthari sagāravo . . . dhamme sagāravo[^125-3] . . . saṅghe sagāravo[^125-4] . . . sikkhāya sagāravo . . . samādhismiṃ sagāravo . . . appamāde sagāravo paṭisanthāre agāravo[^125-5] bhavissatī ti n'; etaṃ[^125-6] ṭhānaṃ vijjati; yo so Sāriputta bhikkhu Satthari sagāravo[^125-7] dhamme[^125-8] sagāravo[^125-9] saṅghe sagāravo sikkhāya sagāravo samādhismiṃ sagāravo appamāde sagāravo, paṭisanthāre pi so sagāravo. Imassa kho Sāriputta mayā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena attho daṭṭhabbo ti. LXVII.

1. Bhāvanaṃ ananuyuttassa bhikkhave bhikkhuno viharato kiñcāpi evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata me anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimucceyyā'; ti, atha khvāssa[^125-10] neva anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccati. Taṃ kissa hetu? ‘Abhāvitattā ti 'ssa vacanīyaṃ. Kissa abhāvitattā? Catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ catunnaṃ sammappadhānānaṃ[^125-11] catunnaṃ iddhipādānaṃ pañcannaṃ indriyānaṃ pañcannaṃ balānaṃ sattannaṃ bojjhaṅgānaṃ ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave kukkuṭiya aṇḍāni[^125-12] aṭṭha vā dasa vā[^125-13] dvādasa vā, tān'; assu kukkuṭiyā na sammāadhisayitāni na[^125-14] sammāpariseditāni[^125-14] na sammāparibhā-


<!-- PTS Page 126 -->
<a id="page-126"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 126]*

vitāni, kiñcāpi tassā kukkuṭiyā evaṃ icchā uppajjheyya ‘aho vata me kukkuṭapotakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakena vā aṇḍakosaṃ padāletvā sotthinā abhinibbijjeyyun[^126-1]'; ti; atha kho abhabbā 'va[^126-2] te kukkuṭapotakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakena vā aṇḍakosaṃ pādaletvā sotthinā abhinibbijjituṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā h'; amūni[^126-3] bhikkhave kukkuṭiyā aṇḍāni na sammā-adhisayitāni na sammāpariseditāni[^126-4] na sammāparibhāvitāni. Evam eva kho bhikkhave bhāvanaṃ ananuyuttassa bhikkhuno viharato kiñcāpi evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata me anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimucceyyā'; ti, atha khvāssa neva anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccati. Taṃ kissa hetu?  Abhāvitattā ti 'ssa vacanīyaṃ. Kissa abhāvitattā? Catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ . . . pe[^126-5] . . . ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa.

2. Bhāvanaṃ anuyuttassa bhikkhave bhikkhuno viharato kiñcāpi na evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata me anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimucceyyā'; ti, atha khvāssa anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccati. Taṃ kissa hetu? Bhāvitattā ti 'ssa vacanīyaṃ. Kissa bhāvitattā? Catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ . . . pe[^126-5] . . . ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave kukkuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā, tān'; assu kukkuṭiyā sammā-adhisayitāni sammāpariseditāni sammāparibhāvitāni, kiñcāpi tassā[^126-6] kukkuṭiyā na evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata me kukkuṭapotakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakena vā aṇḍakosaṃ padāletvā sotthinā abhinibbijjeyyun'; ti; atha kho bhabbā 'va te kukkuṭapotakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakena vā aṇḍakosaṃ padāletvā sotthinā abhinibbijjituṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā h'; amūni[^126-7] bhikkhave kukkuṭiyā aṇḍāni sammā-adhisayitāni sammāpariseditāni sammāparibhāvitāni. Evam eva kho bhikkhave bhāvanaṃ anuyuttassa bhikkhuno viharato kiñcāpi na evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho


<!-- PTS Page 127 -->
<a id="page-127"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 127]*

vata me anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimucceyyā'; ti, atha khvāssa anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccati. Taṃ kissa hetu?  Bhāvitattā ti 'ssa vacanīyaṃ. Kissa bhāvitattā? Catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ . . . pe[^127-1] . . . ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa.

3. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave palagaṇḍassa[^127-2] vā palagaṇḍantevāsikassa[^127-3] vā dissante[^127-4] 'va[^127-5] vāsijaṭe[^127-6] aṅgulipadāni[^127-7] dissanti aṅguṭṭhapadaṃ[^127-8], no ca khvāssa evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti ‘ettakaṃ[^127-9] me ajja[^127-10] vāsijaṭassa khīṇaṃ, ettakaṃ[^127-11] hiyyo, ettakaṃ[^127-11] pare'; ti, atha khvāssa khīṇe khīṇante[^127-12] 'va[^127-12] ñāṇaṃ hoti: evam eva kho bhikkhave bhāvanaṃ anuyuttassa bhikkhuno viharato kiñcāpi na evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti ‘ettakaṃ[^127-9] me ajja āsavānaṃ khīṇaṃ, ettakaṃ[^127-11] hiyyo, ettakaṃ[^127-11] pare'; ti, atha khvāssa khīṇe khīṇante[^127-13] 'va[^127-13] ñāṇaṃ hoti. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave sāmuddikāya nāvāya vettabandhanabaddhāya[^127-14] chammāsāni[^127-15] udake pariyādāya[^127-16] hemantikena thale[^127-17] ukkhittāya vātātapaparetāni bandhanāni, tāni[^127-18] pāvussakena[^127-19] meghena abhippavuṭṭhāni[^127-20] appakasiren'; eva paṭippassambhanti[^127-21] pūtikāni bhavanti: evam eva kho bhikkhave bhāvanaṃ anuyuttassa bhikkhuno viharato appakasiren'; eva saññojanāni paṭippassambhanti pūtikāni[^127-22] bhavantī[^127-22] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 128 -->
<a id="page-128"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 128]*

LXVIII.

1. Evaṃ[^128-1] me[^128-1] sutaṃ[^128-1]. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kosalesu cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṃ. Addasā kho Bhagavā addhānamaggapaṭipanno aññatarasmiṃ padese mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampajjalitaṃ sajotibhūtaṃ[^128-2], disvā[^128-3] maggā okkamma aññatarasmiṃ rukkhamūle paññatte āsane nisīdi. Nisajja kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi ‘passatha no tumhe bhikkhave amuṃ mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampajjalitaṃ sajotibhūtan'; ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante'; . Taṃ kiṃ maññatha bhikkhave, katamaṃ nu kho varaṃ: yaṃ amuṃ mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampajjalitaṃ sajotibhūtaṃ āliṅgitvā[^128-4] upanisīdeyya vā upanipajjeyya vā, yaṃ vā[^128-5] khattiyakaññaṃ vā brāhmaṇakaññaṃ vā gahapatikaññaṃ vā mudutalunahatthapādaṃ[^128-6] āliṅgitvā upanisīdeyya vā upanipajjeyya vā ti? ‘Etad eva bhante varaṃ: yaṃ khattiyakaññaṃ vā brāhmaṇakaññaṃ vā gahapatikaññaṃ vā mudutalunahatthapādaṃ āliṅgitvā upanisīdeyya vā upanipajjeyya vā. Dukkhaṃ h'; etaṃ bhante, yaṃ amuṃ mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampajjalitaṃ sajotibhūtaṃ āliṅgitvā upanisīdeyya vā upanipajjeyya vā'; ti. Ārocayāmi vo bhikkhave, paṭivedayāmi vo bhikkhave, yathā etad eva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa pāpadhammassa asucisaṅkassarasamācārassa[^128-7] paṭicchannakammantassa assamaṇassa samaṇapaṭiññassa abrahmacārissa[^128-8] brahmacāripaṭiññassa[^128-8] antoputissa[^128-9] avassutassa kasambujātassa, yaṃ amuṃ mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampajjalitaṃ sajotibhūtaṃ āliṅgitvā upanisīdeyya vā upanipajjeyya vā. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tato nidānaṃ hi[^128-10] so bhikkhave mara-


<!-- PTS Page 129 -->
<a id="page-129"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 129]*

ṇaṃ vā nigaccheyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tveva tappaccayā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjeyya[^129-1]. Yañ ca kho so bhikkhave dussīlo pāpadhammo asucisaṅkassarasamācāro . . . pe[^129-2] . . . kasambujāto khattiyakaññaṃ vā brāhmaṇakaññaṃ vā gahapatikaññaṃ vā mudutalunahatthapādaṃ āliṅgitvā upanisīdati vā upanipajjati vā, taṃ hi 'ssa[^129-3] bhikkhave hoti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^129-1].

2. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha bhikkhave, katamaṃ nu kho varaṃ: yaṃ balavā puriso daḷhāya vālarajjuyā ubho jaṅghe[^129-4] veṭhetvā[^129-5] ghaṃseyya[^129-6], sā chaviṃ chindeyya, chaviṃ chetvā cammaṃ chindeyya, cammaṃ chetvā maṃsaṃ chindeyya, maṃsaṃ chetvā nahāruṃ chindeyya, nahāruṃ chetvā aṭṭhiṃ chindeyya, aṭṭhiṃ chetvā aṭṭhimiñjaṃ āhacca tiṭṭheyya, yaṃ vā[^129-7] khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā abhivādanaṃ sādiyeyyā[^129-8] ti? ‘Etad eva bhante varaṃ: yaṃ[^129-9] khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā abhivādanaṃ sādiyeyya[^129-10]. Dukkhaṃ h'; etaṃ bhante, yaṃ balavā puriso daḷhāya vālarajjuyā . . . pe[^129-11] . . . aṭṭhimiñjaṃ āhacca tiṭṭheyyā'; ti. Ārocayāmi vo bhikkhave, paṭivedayāmi vo bhikkhave, yathā etad eva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa[^129-12] . . . pe[^129-13] . . . kasambujātassa, yaṃ[^129-14] balavā puriso daḷhāya vālarajjuyā ubho jaṅghe[^129-4] veṭhetvā[^129-5] . . . pe[^129-11] . . . aṭṭhimiñjaṃ āhacca tiṭṭheyya[^129-15]. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tato nidānaṃ hi so bhikkhave maraṇaṃ vā nigaccheyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tveva tappaccayā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjeyya[^129-1]. Yañ


<!-- PTS Page 130 -->
<a id="page-130"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 130]*

ca kho so bhikkhave dussīlo . . . pe[^130-1] . . . kasambujāto khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā abhivādanaṃ sādiyati[^130-2], taṃ hi 'ssa[^130-3] bhikkhave hoti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^130-4].

3. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha bhikkhave, katamaṃ nu kho varaṃ: yaṃ balavā puriso tiṇhāya sattiyā teladhotāya paccorasmiṃ pahareyya[^130-5], yaṃ vā[^130-6] khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā añjalikammaṃ sādiyeyyā ti? ‘Etad eva bhante varaṃ: yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā añjalikammaṃ sādiyeyya. Dukkhaṃ h'; etaṃ bhante, yaṃ balavā puriso tiṇhāya sattiyā teladhotāya paccorasmiṃ pahareyyā'; ti. Ārocayāmi vo bhikkhave, paṭivedayāmi vo bhikkhave, yathā etad eva[^130-7] tassa[^130-7] varaṃ dussīlassa . . . pe[^130-1] . . . kasambujātassa, yaṃ balavā puriso tiṇhāya sattiyā teladhotāya paccorasmiṃ pahareyya. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tato nidānaṃ hi[^130-8] so[^130-8] bhikkhave maraṇaṃ vā nigaccheyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tveva tappaccayā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjeyya[^130-9]. Yañ ca kho so bhikkhave dussīlo pāpadhammo[^130-10] . . . pe[^130-6] . . . kasambujāto khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ[^130-8] vā[^130-8] gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā añjalikammaṃ sādiyati, taṃ hi'; ssa[^130-11] bhikkhave hoti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya, kāyassa bheda parammaraṇa apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^130-9].

4. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha bhikkhave, katamaṃ nu kho varaṃ: yaṃ balavā puriso tattena ayopaṭṭena ādittena


<!-- PTS Page 131 -->
<a id="page-131"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 131]*

sampajjalitena sajotibhūtena kāyaṃ sampaliveṭheyya[^131-1], yaṃ vā[^131-2] khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādeyyaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñjeyyā ti? ‘Etad eva bhante varaṃ: yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā . . . pe[^131-3] . . . saddhādeyyaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñjeyya. Dukkhaṃ h'; etaṃ bhante, yaṃ balavā puriso tattena ayopaṭṭena ādittena[^131-4] sampajjalitena sajotibhūtena kāyaṃ sampaliveṭṭheyyā[^131-5]'; ti. Ārocayāmi vo bhikkhave, paṭivedayāmi vo bhikkhave, yathā etad eva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa . . . pe[^131-6] . . . kasambujātassa, yaṃ[^131-7] balavā puriso tattena ayopaṭṭena ādittena sampajjalitena sajotibhūtena kāyaṃ sampaliveṭheyya[^131-5]. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tato nidānaṃ hi so bhikkhave maraṇaṃ vā nigaccheyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tveva tappaccayā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjeyya[^131-8]. Yañ ca kho so bhikkhave dussīlo[^131-9] . . . pe[^131-6] . . . kasambujāto khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādeyyaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñjati, taṃ hi 'ssa[^131-10] bhikkhave hoti dīgharattam ahitāya dukkhāya, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^131-11].

5. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha bhikkhave, katamaṃ nu kho varaṃ: yaṃ balavā puriso tattena ayosaṅkunā ādittena[^131-12] sampajjalitena[^131-12] sajotibhūtena[^131-12] mukhaṃ vivaritvā tattaṃ lohagulaṃ[^131-13] ādittaṃ sampajjalitaṃ sajotibhūtaṃ mukhe pakkhipeyya, taṃ tassa oṭṭham pi daheyya mukham pi daheyya jivham pi daheyya kaṇṭham pi daheyya udaram[^131-14]

Yañ ca kho so bhikkhave dussīlo pāpadhammo[^132-8] . . . pe[^132-11] . . . kasambujāto khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādeyyaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñjati, taṃ hi 'ssa[^132-14] bhikkhave hoti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^132-15].

6. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha bhikkhave, katamaṃ nu kho varaṃ: yaṃ balavā puriso sīse vā gahetvā khandhe vā

gahetvā tattaṃ ayomañcaṃ vā ayopīṭhaṃ vā ādittaṃ[^133-1] sampajjalitaṃ[^133-1] sajotibhūtaṃ[^133-1] abhinisīdāpeyya vā abhinipajjāpeyya vā, yaṃ vā[^133-2] khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādeyyaṃ mañcapīṭhaṃ[^133-3] paribhuñjeyyā ti? ‘Etad eva bhante varaṃ: yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādeyyaṃ mañcapīthaṃ paribhuñjeyya. Dukkhaṃ h'; etaṃ bhante, yaṃ balavā puriso sīse vā gahetvā khandhe vā gahetvā . . . pe[^133-4] . . . abhinipajjāpeyya vā'; ti. Tato nidānaṃ hi so bhikkhave maraṇaṃ vā nigaccheyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tveva tappaccayā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjeyya[^133-8]. Yañ ca kho so bhikkhave dussīlo pāpadhammo[^133-9] . . . pe[^133-6] . . . kasambujāto khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādeyyaṃ mañcapīṭhaṃ paribhuñjati, taṃ hi 'ssa[^133-10] bhikkhave hoti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^133-8].

7. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha bhikkhave, katamaṃ nu kho varaṃ: yaṃ balavā puriso uddhapādaṃ[^133-11] adhosiraṃ gahetvā tattāya lohakumbhiyā pakkhipeyya ādittāya sampajjalitāya sajotibhūtāya, so tattha phenuddehakaṃ[^133-12] paccamāno sakim pi uddhaṃ gaccheyya sakim pi adho gaccheyya sakim pi tiriyaṃ gaccheyya, yaṃ vā[^133-13] khattiyamahāsālānaṃ

vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādeyyaṃ vihāraṃ paribhuñjeyyā ti? ‘Etad eva bhante varaṃ: yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādeyyaṃ vihāraṃ paribhuñjeyya. Dukkhaṃ h'; etaṃ bhante, yaṃ balavā puriso uddhapādaṃ[^134-1] adhosiraṃ gahetvā tattāya lohakumbhiyā pakkhipeyya ādittāya sampajjalitāya sajotibhūtāya, so tattha phenuddehakaṃ paccamāno sakim[^134-2] pi[^134-2] uddhaṃ[^134-2] gaccheyya[^134-2] sakiṃ pi adho gaccheyya sakim pi tiriyaṃ gaccheyyā'; ti. Ārocayāmi vo bhikkhave, paṭivedayāmi[^134-3] vo bhikkhave, yathā etad eva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa pāpadhammassa[^134-4] . . . pe[^134-5] . . . kasambujātassa, yam[^134-2] balavā puriso uddhapādaṃ[^134-1] adhosiraṃ gahetvā . . . pe[^134-6] . . . sakim pi tiriyaṃ gaccheyya. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tato nidānaṃ hi so bhikkhave maraṇaṃ vā nigaccheyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tveva tappaccayā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjeyya[^134-7]. Yañ ca kho so bhikkhave dussīlo pāpadhammo[^134-4] . . . pe[^134-5] . . . kasambujāto khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādeyyaṃ vihāraṃ paribhuñjati, taṃ hi 'ssa[^134-8] bhikkhave hoti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^134-7]. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:-- Yesañ ca mayaṃ paribhuñjāma cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikhārānaṃ[^134-9], tesan[^134-10] te[^134-11] kārā[^134-12] mahapphalā bhavissanti mahānisaṃsā, amhākañ c'; evāyaṃ pabbajjā avañjhā bhavissati saphalā sa-udrayā[^134-13] ti. Evaṃ hi vo[^134-14] bhikkhave sikkhitabbaṃ.

8. Attatthaṃ[^134-15] vā bhikkhave sampassamānena alam eva appamādena sampādetuṃ, paratthaṃ vā bhikkhave sam-


<!-- PTS Page 135 -->
<a id="page-135"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 135]*

passamānena alam eva appamādena sampādetuṃ, ubhayatthaṃ vā bhikkhave sampassamānena alam eva appamādena sampādetun ti. Idam avoca Bhagavā[^135-1]. Imasmiñ ca pana veyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhaññamāne saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ uṇhaṃ lohitaṃ mukhato uggañchi[^135-2], saṭṭhimattā bhikkhū sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattiṃsu[^135-3] ‘dukkaraṃ Bhagavā sudukkaraṃ Bhagavā'; ti, saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavehi cittāni vimucciṃsū ti. LXIX.

1. Bhūtapubbaṃ bhikkhave Sunetto nāma satthā ahosi titthakaro kāmesu vītarāgo. Sunettassa kho pana bhikkhave satthuno anekāni[^135-4] sāvakasatāni ahesuṃ[^135-5]. Sunetto satthā sāvakānaṃ Brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ deseti. Ye kho pana[^135-6] bhikkhave Sunettassa satthuno Brahmalokasavyatāya dhammaṃ desentassa cittāni na[^135-7] pasādesuṃ[^135-7], te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjiṃsu[^135-8]. Ye kho pana bhikkhave Sunettassa satthuno Brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desentassa cittāni pasādesuṃ[^135-9], te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjiṃsu[^135-8].

2. Bhūtapubbaṃ bhikkhave Mūgapakkho nāma satthā ahosi . . .[^135-10] Aranemi nāma satthā ahosi . . .[^135-11] Kuddālo[^135-12] nāma satthā ahosi . . .[^135-11]Hatthipālo nāma satthā ahosi . . .[^135-11] Jotipālo nāma satthā ahosi . . .[^135-11] Arako nāma satthā ahosi titthakaro kāmesu vītarāgo. Arakassa kho pana bhikkhave satthuno anekāni sāvakasatāni ahesuṃ. Arako nāma[^135-13] satthā sāvakānaṃ Brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ deseti[^135-14]. Ye kho pana[^135-6] bhikkhave Arakassa satthuno Brah-


<!-- PTS Page 136 -->
<a id="page-136"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 136]*

malokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desentassa cittāni na[^136-1] pasādesuṃ[^136-1], te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjiṃsu[^136-2]. Ye kho pana bhikkhave Arakassa satthuno Brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desentassa cittāni pasādesuṃ, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjiṃsu[^136-2].

3. Taṃ kim maññatha bhikkhave, yo ime satta satthāre titthakare kāmesu vītarāge anekasataparivāre sasāvakasaṅghe[^136-3] duṭṭhacitto akkoseyya paribhāseyya, bahuṃ so apuññaṃ pasaveyyā ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante'; . Yo kho[^136-4] bhikkhave ime satta satthāre titthakare kāmesu vītarāge anekasataparivāre sasāvakasaṅghe[^136-3] duṭṭhacitto[^136-5] akkoseyya paribhāseyya, bahuṃ[^136-6] so apuññaṃ pasaveyya, yo ekaṃ diṭṭhisampannaṃ puggalaṃ duṭṭhacitto[^136-7] akkosati paribhāsati, ayaṃ tato bahutaraṃ apuññaṃ pasavati. Taṃ kissa hetu? Nāhaṃ bhikkhave ito bahiddhā evarūpiṃ khantiṃ vadāmi yathā'; maṃ sabrahmacārīsu. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:-- Na[^136-8] no[^136-8] sabrahmacārīsu cittāni paduṭṭhāni bhavissantī ti. Evaṃ hi vo[^136-9] bhikkhave sikkhitabban ti. LXX.

1. Bhūtapubbaṃ bhikkhave Arako[^136-10] nāma satthā ahosi titthakaro kāmesu vītarāgo. Arakassa kho pana bhikkhave satthuno anekāni sāvakasatāni ahesuṃ. Arako satthā sāvakānaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ desesi[^136-11]:--

2. Appakaṃ brāhmaṇa jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ[^136-12] bahudukkhaṃ bahūpāyāsaṃ. Mantāya bodhab-


<!-- PTS Page 137 -->
<a id="page-137"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 137]*

baṃ[^137-1], kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa tiṇagge ussāvabindu suriye uggacchante khippaṃ yeva paṭivigacchati na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hoti, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa ussāvabindūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahūpāyāsaṃ. Mantāya bodhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa thullaphusitake[^137-2] deve vassante udake[^137-3] udakabubbulaṃ khippaṃ yeva paṭivigacchati[^137-4] na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hoti, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa udakabubbulūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahūpāyāsaṃ. Mantāya bodhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udake daṇḍarāji khippaṃ yeva paṭivigacchati[^137-4] na ciraṭṭhitikā hoti, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa udake daṇḍarājūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ[^137-5] lahukaṃ[^137-5] . . .[^137-6] natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa nadi pabbateyyā dūraṅgamā sīghasotā[^137-7] hārahārinī, natthi so khaṇo vā layo vā muhutto vā, yaṃ[^137-8] sā[^137-8] āramati[^137-9], atha kho sā gacchat'; eva[^137-10] vattat' eva[^137-11] sandat'[^137-12] eva[^137-11], evam eva kho brāhmaṇa nadipabbateyyūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ[^137-13] lahukaṃ[^137-13] . . . pe[^137-14] . . . natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ. Seyyathā[^137-15] pi brāhmaṇa balavā puriso jivhagge kheḷapiṇḍaṃ saññūhitvā appakasiren' eva vameyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa kheḷapiṇḍūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ[^137-13] . . .[^137-16] natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa divasasantatte


<!-- PTS Page 138 -->
<a id="page-138"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 138]*

ayokaṭāhe maṃsapesī[^138-1] pakkhittā[^138-2] khippaṃ yeva paṭivigacchati[^138-3] na ciraṭṭhitikā hoti, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa maṃsapesūpamaṃ manussānaṃ jīvitaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ[^138-4] . . .[^138-5] natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa gāvī vajjhā[^138-6] āghātanaṃ niyyamānā yañ ñad eva pādaṃ[^138-7] uddharati santike 'va[^138-8] hoti[^138-8] vadhassa[^138-9] santike 'va[^138-10] maraṇassa, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa govajjhūpamaṃ[^138-11] jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahūpāyāsaṃ. Mantāya bodhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇan ti.

3. Tena kho pana bhikkhave samayena manussānaṃ saṭṭhivassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahosi. Pañcavassasatikā kumārikā alaṃpateyyā ahosi. Tena kho pana bhikkhave samayena manussānaṃ chaḷ eva ābādhā ahesum: sītaṃ uṇhaṃ jighacchā[^138-12] pipāsā uccāro passāvo. So hi nāma bhikkhave[^138-13] Arako satthā evaṃ dīghāyukesu manussesu evaṃ ciraṭṭhitikesu evaṃ appābādhesu sāvakānaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ desessati[^138-14] ‘appakaṃ brāhmaṇa jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahūpāyāsaṃ. Mantāya bodhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇan'; ti. Etarahi kho[^138-15] taṃ[^138-8] bhikkhave sammā vadamāno vadeyya ‘appakaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahūpāyāsaṃ. Mantāya bodhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇan'; ti. Etarahi kho[^138-15] bhikkhave yo ciraṃ jīvati, so vassasataṃ appaṃ vā bhiyyo[^138-16]. Vassasataṃ kho pana bhikkhave jīvanto tīṇi yeva utusatāni jīvati: utusataṃ hemantānaṃ, utusataṃ gimhānaṃ, utusataṃ vassānaṃ. Tīṇi kho pana bhikkhave utusatāni jīvanto dvādasa[^138-17] yeva māsasatāni jīvati: cattāri māsasatāni


<!-- PTS Page 139 -->
<a id="page-139"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 139]*

hemantānaṃ, cattāri māsasatāni gimhānaṃ, cattāri māsasatāni vassānaṃ. Dvādasa[^139-1] kho pana bhikkhave māsasatāni jīvanto catuvīsatiṃ[^139-2] yeva addhamāsasatāni jīvati: aṭṭhaddhamāsasatāni hemantānaṃ, aṭṭhaddhamāsasatāni gimhānaṃ, aṭṭhaddhamāsasatāni vassānaṃ. Catuvīsatiṃ[^139-3] kho pana bhikkhave addhamāsasatāni[^139-4] jīvanto chattiṃsaṃ[^139-5] yeva rattisahassāni jīvati: dvādasa rattisahassāni hemantānaṃ, dvādasa rattisahassāni gimhānaṃ, dvādasa rattisahassāni vassānaṃ. Chattiṃsaṃ kho pana bhikkhave rattisahassāni jīvanto dvesattatiñ[^139-6] ñeva[^139-6] bhattasahassāni bhuñjati: catuvīsatiṃ[^139-2] bhattasahassāni hemantānaṃ, catuvīsatiṃ[^139-2] bhattasahassāni gimhānaṃ, catuvīsatiṃ[^139-7] bhattasahassāni vassānaṃ saddhiṃ[^139-8] mātuthaññāya[^139-8] saddhiṃ bhattantarāyena[^139-9]. Tatr[^139-10]'; ime bhattantarāyā[^139-9]: kupito pi bhattaṃ na bhuñjati, dukkhito pi bhattaṃ na bhuñjati, vyādhito[^139-11] pi bhattaṃ na bhuñjati, uposathiko[^139-12] pi bhattaṃ na bhuñjati, alābhakena[^139-13] pi bhattaṃ na bhuñjati. Iti kho bhikkhave mayā vassasatāyukassa manussassa āyu pi saṃkhāto, āyuppamāṇam pi saṃkhātaṃ[^139-14],utū pi saṃkhātā, saṃvaccharā[^139-15] pi saṃkhātā, māsā pi saṃkhātā, addhamāsā[^139-16] pi saṃkhātā, rattī pi saṃkhātā, rattindivā[^139-17] pi saṃkhātā, bhattā pi saṃkhātā, bhattantarāyā pi saṃkhātā.

4. Yaṃ bhikkhave satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitesinā anukampakena anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vo taṃ mayā. Etāni bhikkhave rukkhamūlāni etāni suññāgārāni. Jhāyatha bhikkhave, mā pamādattha, mā pacchā vippaṭisārino ahuvattha Ayaṃ vo amhākaṃ anusāsanī ti.

Mahāvaggo[^140-1] sattamo[^140-2]. Tass'; uddānaṃ: Hiri suriyaṃ[^140-3] nāgaraṃ[^140-4] upamā dhammaññū[^140-5] pārichattakaṃ[^140-5] Sakkatvā[^140-6] bhāvanaṃ[^140-7] aggi Sunetta-Arakena[^140-8] cā[^140-8] ti. LXXI.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu vinayadharo hoti. Katamehi sattahi?

2. Āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ[^140-9] jānāti[^140-9], lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sīlavā hoti pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno anumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu, catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^140-10] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ[^140-9] khaya[^140-9] anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu vinayadharo hotī ti. LXXII.

1. Sattahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu vinayadharo hoti. Katamehi sattahi?

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave puggalā āhuneyyā[^145-9] pāhuneyyā dakkhiṇeyyā añjalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katame satta?

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo cakkhusmiṃ aniccānupassī viharati aniccasaññī aniccapaṭisaṃvedī satataṃ samitaṃ abbokiṇṇaṃ cetasā adhimuccamāno paññāya pariyogāhamāno. So āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchi-

##### *[PTS Page 146]*

katvā upasampajja viharati. Ayaṃ kho[^146-1] bhikkhave paṭhamo puggalo āhuneyyo[^146-2] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco puggalo cakkhusmiṃ aniccānupassī viharati aniccasaññī aniccapaṭisaṃvedī satataṃ samitaṃ abbokiṇṇaṃ cetasā adhimuccamāno paññāya pariyogāhamāno. Tassa apubbaṃ acarimaṃ āsavapariyādānañ ca hoti jīvitapariyādānañ ca[^146-3]. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo puggalo āhuneyyo . . .[^146-4] anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco puggalo cakkhusmiṃ aniccānupassī viharati aniccasaññī aniccapaṭisaṃvedī satataṃ samitaṃ abbokiṇṇaṃ cetasā adhimuccamāno paññāya pariyogāhamāno. So pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saññojanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hoti . . .[^146-4] upahaccaparinibbāyī hoti . . .[^146-4] asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hoti . . .[^146-4] sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hoti . . . uddhaṃsoto hoti akaniṭṭhagāmī. Ayaṃ bhikkhave sattamo puggalo āhuneyyo[^146-5] . . .[^146-4] anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Ime kho bhikkhave satta puggalā āhuneyyā pāhuneyyā dakkhiṇeyyā añjalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave puggalā āhuneyyā[^146-6] . . .[^146-4] anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katame satta?

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo cakkhusmiṃ dukkhānupassī viharati . . .[^146-4] cakkhusmiṃ anattānupassī viharati . . .[^146-3] cakkhusmiṃ[^146-7] khayānupassī viharati . . .[^146-4] cakkhusmiṃ vayānupassī viharati . . .[^146-4] cakkhusmiṃ virāgānupassī viharati . . .[^146-4] cakkhusmiṃ nirodhānupassī[^146-8] viharati[^146-8] . . .[^146-9] cakkhusmiṃ[^146-8] paṭinissaggānupassī viharati . . .[^146-4] sotasmiṃ . . .[^146-9] ghānasmiṃ . . . jivhāya . . . kāyasmiṃ . . . manasmiṃ . . . rūpesu . . . saddesu . . . gandhesu . . . rasesu . . . phoṭ-

ṭhabbesu . . .[^147-1]dhammesu . . . cakkhuviññāṇe . . .[^147-1] sotaviññāṇe . . . ghānaviññāṇe . . . jivhāviññāṇe . . . kāyaviññāṇe . . .[^147-1] manoviññāṇe . . .[^147-2] cakkhusamphasse . . .[^147-1] sotasamphasse . . .[^147-1] ghānasamphasse . . . jivhāsamphasse . . . kāyasamphasse . . .[^147-1] manosamphasse . . .[^147-2] cakkhusamphassajāya[^147-3] vedanāya . . . sotasamphassajāya vedanāya . . . ghānasamphassajāya vedanāya . . .jivhāsamphassajāya vedanāya . . . kāyasamphassajāya vedanāya . . . manosamphassajāya[^147-4] vedanāya[^147-4] . . . rūpasaññāya . . . saddasaññāya . . . gandhasaññāya . . . rasasaññāya phoṭṭhabbasaññāya . . . dhammasaññāya . . . rūpasañcetanāya . . . saddasañcetanāya . . . gandhasañcetanāya rasasañcetanāya . . . phoṭṭhabbasañcetanāya . . . dhammasañcetanāya . . . rūpataṇhāya . . . saddataṇhāya . . . gandhataṇhāya . . . rasataṇhāya . . . phoṭṭhabbataṇhāya . . . dhammataṇhāya . . . rūpavitakke . . . saddavitakke . . . gandhavitakke . . . rasavitakke . . . phoṭṭhabbavitakke . . . dhammavitakke . . . rūpavicāre . . . saddavicāre . . . gandhavicāre . . . rasavicāre . . . phoṭṭhabbavicāre . . . dhammavicāre . . .[^147-5] rūpakkhandhe . . . vedanākkhandhe . . . saññākkhandhe . . . saṅkhārakkhandhe . . . viññāṇakkhandhe aniccānupassī viharati . . . dukkhānupassī viharati . . . anattānupassī viharati . . . khayānupassī viharati . . . vayānupassī viharati . . . virāgānupassī viharati . . . nirodhānupassī viharati . . . paṭinissaggānupassī viharati[^147-6].

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya satta dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame satta?

2. Satisambojjhaṅgo . . . pe[^148-1] . . . upekhāsambojjhaṅgo . . .[^148-2] Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya ime satta dhammā bhāvetabbā ti[^148-3].

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya satta dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame satta?

2. Aniccasaññā, anattasaññā, asubhasaññā, ādīnavasaññā, pahānasaññā, virāgasaññā, nirodhasaññā. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya ime satta dhammā bhāvetabbā ti[^148-4].

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya satta dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame satta?

<!-- PTS Page 149 -->
<a id="page-149"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 149]*

Idam[^149-1] avoca Bhagavā. Attamanā te bhikkhū Bhagavato bhāsitaṃ abhinandun ti[^149-2]. Sattakanipāto[^149-3] samatto[^149-4].


<!-- PTS Page 150 -->
<a id="page-150"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 150]*

AṬṬHAKA-NIPĀTA. Namo[^150-1] Tassa Bhagavato Arahato Sammāsambuddhassa. I [^150-2].

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante[^150-3] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Mettāya bhikkhave cetovimuttiyā āsevitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānikatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya aṭṭhānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā. Katame aṭṭha?

3. Sukhaṃ supati, sukhaṃ paṭibujjhati, na pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, manussānaṃ piyo hoti, amanussānaṃ piyo hoti, devatā rakkhanti, nāssa[^150-4] aggi vā visaṃ vā satthaṃ vā kamati, uttariṃ[^150-5] appaṭivijjhanto brahmalokūpago hoti. Mettāya bhikkhave cetovimuttiyā āsevitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānikatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya ime aṭṭhānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā ti. Yo ca mettaṃ bhāvayati appamāṇaṃ[^150-6] paṭissato tanū[^150-7] saññojanā[^150-8] honti[^150-9] passato upadhikkhayaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 151 -->
<a id="page-151"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 151]*

Ekam pi ce pāṇam aduṭṭhacitto mettāyati, kusalī[^151-1] tena hoti, sabbe 'va[^151-2] pāṇe manasānukampi[^151-3] {pahūtam ariyo}[^151-4] pakaroti[^151-5] puññaṃ. Ye sattasaṇḍaṃ paṭhaviṃ vijetva[^151-6] rājīsayo[^151-7] yajamānā- {nupariyagā}[^151-8] assamedhaṃ purisamedhaṃ sammāpāsaṃ[^151-9] vājapeyyaṃ[^151-10] niraggaḷaṃ, mettassa cittassa subhāvitassa kalam pi te nānubhavanti soḷasiṃ candappabhā tāragaṇā va sabbe. Yo na hanti na ghāteti na jināti[^151-11] na jāpaye[^151-12] mettaṃso[^151-13] sabbabhūtānaṃ[^151-14], veraṃ tassa na kenacī ti. II.

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave hetū aṭṭha[^151-15] paccayā[^151-15] ādibrahmacariyikāya[^151-16] paññāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattanti. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthāraṃ upanissāya viharati aññataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ[^151-17] sabrahmacāriṃ[^151-18], yatth'; assa tibbaṃ hirottappaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti pemañ ca gāravo. ca. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo hetu paṭhamo paccayo ādibrahmacariyikāya paññāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati.


<!-- PTS Page 152 -->
<a id="page-152"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 152]*

3. So taṃ Satthāraṃ upanissāya viharanto[^152-1] aññataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ[^152-2] sabrahmacāriṃ, yatth'; assa tibbaṃ hirottappaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti pemañ ca gāravo ca, te[^152-3] kālena kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati: Idaṃ bhante kathaṃ?  Imassa ko[^152-4] attho[^152-4] ti? Tassa te āyasmanto avivaṭañ c'; eva[^152-5] vivaranti[^152-6], anuttānīkatañ[^152-7] ca uttānīkaronti[^152-8], anekavihitesu ca kaṅkhāṭṭhāniyesu dhammesu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinodenti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo hetu dutiyo paccayo ādibrahmacariyikāya paññāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati.

4. So taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā dvayena vūpakāsena sampādeti: kāyavūpakāsena ca cittavūpakāsena ca. Ayaṃ bhikkhave tatiyo hetu tatiyo paccayo ādibrahmacariyikāya paññāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati.

5. Sīlavā hoti pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Ayaṃ bhikkhave catuttho hetu catuttho paccayo ādibrahmacariyikāya paññāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati.

6. Bahussuto hoti sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ[^152-9] savyañjanaṃ[^152-10] kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa[^152-11] dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā[^152-12] vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Ayaṃ bhikkhave pañcamo hetu pañcamo paccayo ādibrahmacariyikāya paññāya appaṭiladdhāya patilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati.


<!-- PTS Page 153 -->
<a id="page-153"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 153]*

7. Āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Ayaṃ bhikkhave chaṭṭho hetu chaṭṭho paccayo ādibrahmacariyikāya paññāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati.

8. Saṅghagato[^153-1] kho pana {anaṇākathiko}[^153-2] hoti atiracchānakathiko, sāmaṃ vā dhammaṃ bhāsati, paraṃ vā ajjhesati, ariyaṃ vā tuṇhībhāvaṃ nātimaññati. Ayaṃ bhikkhave sattamo hetu sattamo paccayo ādibrahmacariyikāya paññāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati.

9. Pañcasu kho pana upādānakkhandhesu udayabbayānupassī viharati ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayo, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamo; iti[^153-3] vedanā[^153-3], iti[^153-4] vedanāya samudayo, iti vedanāya atthaṅgamo; iti saññā . . .[^153-5] iti saṅkhārā . . . iti viññāṇaṃ, iti viññāṇassa samudayo, iti viññāṇassa atthaṅgamo'; ti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave aṭṭhamo[^153-6] hetu aṭṭhamo[^153-6] paccayo ādibrahmacariyikāya paññāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati.

10. Tam enaṃ sabrahmacārī evaṃ sambhāventi: ayaṃ[^153-7] kho[^153-7] āyasmā Satthāraṃ upanissāya viharati aññataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ, yatth'; assa tibbaṃ hirottappaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti[^153-8] pemañ ca gāravo ca; addhā ayam āyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī ti. Ayaṃ pi dhammo piyattāya[^153-9] garuttāya[^153-10] bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

11. Taṃ kho panāyam āyasmā Satthāraṃ upanissāya viharanto[^153-11] aññataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ, yatth'; assa tibbaṃ hirottappaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti[^153-8]


<!-- PTS Page 154 -->
<a id="page-154"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 154]*

pemañ ca gāravo ca, te[^154-1] kālena kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati: Idaṃ bhante kathaṃ? Imassa ko[^154-2] attho[^154-2] ti? Tassa te āyasmanto avivaṭañ[^154-3] c'; eva[^154-4] vivaranti[^154-5], anuttānīkatañ ca uttānīkaronti, anekavihitesu ca kaṅkhāṭṭhāniyesu dhammesu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinodenti; addhā ayam āyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī[^154-6] ti. Ayaṃ pi dhammo piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

12. Taṃ[^154-7] kho panāyam āyasmā dhammaṃ sutvā dvayena[^154-8] vūpakāsena sampādeti: kāyavūpakāsena ca cittavūpakāsena ca; addhā ayam[^154-7] āyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī ti. Ayaṃ pi dhammo piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

13. Sīlavā kho panāyam āyasmā pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu; addhā ayam āyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī ti. Ayaṃ pi dhammo piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

14. Bahussuto kho panāyam āyasmā sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ[^154-9] savyañjanam[^154-10] kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa[^154-11] dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; addhā ayam āyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī ti. Ayaṃ pi dhammo piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

15. Āraddhaviriyo kho panāyam āyasmā viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu; addhā ayam āyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī ti. Ayaṃ pi dhammo piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.


<!-- PTS Page 155 -->
<a id="page-155"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 155]*

16. Saṅghagato kho panāyam āyasmā anānākathiko[^155-1] atiracchānakathiko, sāmaṃ[^155-2] vā dhammaṃ bhāsati, paraṃ vā ajjhesati[^155-3], ariyaṃ vā tuṇhībhāvaṃ nātimaññati; addhā ayam āyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī ti. Ayaṃ pi dhammo piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

17. Pañcasu[^155-4] kho panāyam āyasmā upādānakkhandhesu udayabbayānupassī viharati ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayo, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamo; iti vedanā . . .[^155-5] iti saññā . . . iti saṅkhārā . . . iti viññāṇaṃ, iti viññāṇassa samudayo, iti viññāṇassa atthaṅgamo'; ti; addhā ayam āyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī ti. Ayaṃ pi dhammo piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha hetū aṭṭha paccayā ādibrahmacariyikāya paññāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattantī ti. III.

1. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyo ca hoti amanāpo ca[^155-6] agaru ca abhāvanīyo ca. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu appiyapasaṃsī ca hoti piyagarahī ca[^155-6] lābhakāmo ca[^155-6] sakkārakāmo ca ahiriko[^155-7] ca anottappī ca pāpiccho ca micchādiṭṭhi ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyo ca hoti amanāpo ca agaru[^155-6] ca[^155-6] abhāvanīyo ca.

3. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca hoti manāpo ca garu ca bhāvanīyo ca. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

M8 tev'; assa vaso [M8 vasso] ca vaso [M8 vasso] bhavitta[ṃ] taṃ) dve mittā|| (M8 inserts dve patisambhīdā) dve samādhi (Ph. M8 samānā) ca niddiṭṭhā (Ph. M8 nidditā) niddasavatthu (M8 niddesavatthuṃ) apare dve ti; T. M6 have: appamāda (M6. M7 -do) hiri ca (or hirima) sedhena (M6 so ca dhena; M7 sevadena) vittha vittha (M6. M7 vitthā) ce pi || vittā (M6 citta; M7 ce paṭicca for ce pi vi-) paṭisambhidā ca niddasavatthu apare dve ti.

[^147-6]: M. adds the following uddāna: Cha dvārārammaṇesv'; ettha viññāṇesu ca phassesu vedanāsu ca dvārassa suttā honti visuṃ aṭṭha saññā sañcetanā taṇhā vitakkesu vicāre ca gocarassa visuṃ aṭṭha pañcakkhande ca pacceke soḷasesv'; attamūlesu aniccadukkha-anattā khayā vayā virāgā ca nirodhā paṭinissaggā kamaṃ aṭṭhānupassī ti sambhinditesu sabbesu honti pañcasatāni ca aṭṭhavīsati suttāni āhuneyye ca vaggite āhuneyyavaggo dasamo.

Satisaṃbojjhaṅgo dhammavicayasaṃbojjhaṅgo viriyasaṃbojjhaṅgo pithi- (sic) passaddhi- samādhi- upekkhā-. Rāgassa bh- abhiññāya ime satta dh- bh- ti. Rāgassa bh- abhi- satta dh- bh-. Katame satta? Aniccasaññā, anatta- and so on, as above, till the end.
